# Frontend change documentation (for the AI agent that ports the local frontend to the live site)

## Mouse and "Right click" view in the shortcuts pop-up — 7 November 2026

The keyboard shortcuts pop-up (the ? button, all modes) now has a mouse next to the keyboard, as its own element and not part of the keyboard: Left / Right buttons and a wheel, with the right button in orange and pulsing gently. A click on any part of the mouse (left, right, wheel) swaps the keyboard for the "Right click" view and back (also the X, Esc, or the pop-up closing). The view is a small copy of the current page; every spot where a right click does something has an epicentre of soft orange ripples, and a click on one explains it in a frosted tip:

* the timeline: "Jump to beginning of that round" (only with the timeline switched on in the settings, in replay and sandbox);
* the zoo map of the point of view: "Select enclosure to preview its map placement";
* a sponsor building card in the hand: "Select on sponsor building to preview its map placement" (the last two only with "Building preview" on in the settings, and only when such a card is in the hand).

The page behind the pop-up is dimmed like the card spotlight (same teal dim, light blur) instead of the flat brown dim.

## Jumping by rounds — 7 November 2026

Replay and sandbox only (`roundJumps()` in `playback.js`; fork and live play leave the keys and the context menu alone). A right click on the timeline goes to the start of the round under the pointer (`tl.oncontextmenu`); Ctrl + Left goes to the start of the current round (the one before when already there), Ctrl + Right to the start of the next round (the end of the game from the last round); Ctrl + 1-9 goes to the start of that round, Alt + 1-9 does the same (browsers keep Ctrl + 1-9 for their tabs, so the page may never see it). Round starts: replay = the step after "End of the break" (as the timeline marks), sandbox = the first step whose state has a higher `round`. A jump stops autoplay. The shortcuts pop-up (`shortcuts.js`) has a Ctrl layer: the Ctrl keys are clickable, a click latches them (orange, pressed, padlock) until the next click, and the real Ctrl key works the same way (one press locks, the next press unlocks, nothing has to be held; while locked the bare keys act as their Ctrl versions); while it is on only the keys with a Ctrl function are lit (1-9, Left, Right) and the plain keys are quiet; the prompt line says "Jump to start of round 3" / "... of this round" / "... of next round". The layer exists only where the jumps do (`CTRL_LAYER`).

## Lighter lightbox dim, gold ends on the draft ribbon — 6 November 2026

`.draftbox::before` (action card draft and every reveal lightbox) is lighter: flat dim .72 -> .45, edge of the spotlight .78 -> .55, warm centre .30 -> .28, blur 3 px -> 2 px (about 75 % dark at the edges instead of 94 %). The "Action card draft" headline (`action-bar.js` `draftBar`) now sits in a `.headrow.noicon` like the reveals' headlines, so the gold line follows the swallowtail on both ends (`.headrow::after` is the right end, `.headrow.noicon::before` the left one).

## Base projects and track bonuses are shown from the start — 6 November 2026

The three base conservation projects, the bonuses of the 5 / 8 conservation spaces and (Marine Worlds) the bonus of 16 reputation were hidden until the action card draft was over (`draftOn` in `shared.js`). They are known from the start of the game by the rules, so `draftOn` is gone and they are drawn at once, in every mode (an empty base project list, as the Ark360 live projection sends it before the draft is over, still shows three empty slots).

## Hand fan: lifts only over its cards — 6 November 2026

The fan (Hand display = Fan) used to rise when the pointer was anywhere over its fixed 560 px wide panel / `::after` strip, even far to the right of a 2-card hand. `dock.js::fitDockSpace` now measures where the cards lie at rest and sets `--fl` / `--fw` on the dock (the `::after` strip uses them); in fan mode `.dockpanel` has `pointer-events: none`, only the cards take the pointer. Tray mode is unchanged.

## Category university: hover shows its card — 6 November 2026

Hovering a player's category university (reptile, marine, bird, predator, herbivore, primate) on the association strip shows the card it gave: it slides out from behind the tile, tilted, with a notched ribbon "<CATEGORY> UNIVERSITY" at the bottom in a colour per category (reptile #4B3F9E, marine #2F7FC4, bird #6EC1E4, predator #C23B2E, herbivore #3E9B52, primate #EE8B1E). New `web/js/unicard.js` (overlay on `<html>`, so zoom-safe; placed inside the viewport, hidden at once on scroll, redraw or when the pointer leaves the tile), hook in `association.js`, styles `.unipeek` at the end of `parkposter.css`. Data: new `PlayerState.uni_cards` (category -> card key), set by `effects.search_for_category` (engine, sandbox, fork, live) and, in log replays, by `replay/builder.py::h_draw_cards` from the "for gaining a new university" draw. Old saved states default to `{}` (no hover). Python changes: restart the server.

## "gets N x <bonus>" lines in the replay (general rule) — 6 November 2026

`_drop_announced_gains` in `replay/view.py`: "A gets N x <bonus> (<source>)" is not a step of the replay when the next step is that bonus' own effect (table `_EFFECT_AFTER_GETS`): `gains N money|xtoken|appeal|reputation|conservation` (any source), `snaps ... from the display` (Snapping), `adds a size-2 / size-3 enclosure for free`, `adds a pavilion|kiosk`, `pouches ... for N appeal` (Pouch), `plays <card>` (sponsor-person-card), `adds a multiplier token`, `discards ... for the Shark Attack effect`, `gains a new Association worker`. Checked against 10 tables (log_examples 770429314, 800966139, 800999064, 853899676, 920295323, 927683584, 927726814, 930781276, 931947993, 941429583): 187 lines dropped, nothing else changed in any of them (before / after step lists compared), every dropped line is followed by the effect of the same player. Lines that stay on purpose: a bonus whose effect line does not follow (X tokens at their limit: BGA logs no gain; an optional size-2 that was not built), and the bonuses that are not in the table because the next line is a different effect or none: Extra Shift, "ignore conditions", increased hand, Clever, Fac, Partner Zoo, the grey sponsor bonus, `bonus-icon` (meaning unclear). Replay only (raw log, statistics, engine untouched); the "MAP_13_INCOME" lines are handled by `_ANNOUNCES_TAKE`. Test: `tests/test_drop_announced_gains.py`. Python: restart the server.

## Reputation track bonus lines in the replay — 6 November 2026

The reputation track's conservation and X token bonuses (11 / 14, 12 / 15) were worded twice ("A gets 1 x xtoken (reputation track bonus)", then "A gains 1 xtoken (reputation track bonus)"). The first line is no longer a step of the replay (`_drop_announced_gains` in `replay/view.py`); it stays when the gain line does not follow (a bonus that cannot be taken). The other bonuses of the track (upgrade card, worker, card) had no second line already (`_ANNOUNCES_TAKE`, the worker has one line). Replay only; the raw log, the statistics and the engine are untouched. Game 941429583: 627 -> 623 steps. Only this one log was available to check. Python: restart the server.

## Map 13 income lines hidden in the replay — 6 November 2026

The log line "X gets N x MAP_13_INCOME (map bonus space)" (N = the quadrant: 0 money, 1 appeal, 2 reputation, 3 hunters) only announces the "Map 13 quarter bonus" lines that follow, so the replay no longer has a step for it (`_ANNOUNCES_TAKE` in `replay/view.py`, the same mechanism as "gets 1 x take-in-range-or-deck"). Only the replay's step list changes: the raw log, the statistics (`mapstats.py` reads the raw events) and the engine are untouched. Game 941429583: 638 -> 627 steps (the 11 income lines). Python: restart the server.

## Map 13 quadrant statistics — 6 November 2026

"Reputation / Appeal from quadrant effect" counted most triggers twice (`replay/mapstats.py`): the income of a break was counted from its `takeBonus` and again from its "Map 13 quarter bonus" event, and an instant trigger from the filled quadrant (`mapStatus`, sometimes one move earlier) and again from its quarter bonus event. Money / appeal / reputation are now counted from the quarter bonus event only (one per trigger); the hunters' quadrant, which pays no bonus event, from its fill and its income. Game 941429583 now gives appeal 4 / 4 triggers (8 / 8 points) and reputation 2 / 5 (it was 7 / 8 and 4 / 9), as the log lines say. Python: restart the server. Test: `tests/test_mapstats.py`.

## Association animations — 6 November 2026

Reputation / conservation cylinders, partner zoos and universities (taken, and refilled in the break), workers (spend, gain, Extra Shift, break) and the cubes of supported projects and donations now glide instead of blinking. Details: docs/animations_v01.md, "Association side". New: `web/js/assoc-motion-plan.js`, `web/js/assoc-motion.js`, `web/js/break-stages.js` (+ `tests/break-stages.test.mjs`; call in `main.js`), `tests/assoc-motion-plan.test.mjs`; small data attributes in `shared.js`, `association.js`, `assocbar.js`, `side-panel.js`, `projects.js`; hooks in `card-motion.js`; `.cm-piece` in `card-motion.css`. Web only (F5).

## Small changes — 6 November 2026

- **Tab switches the cards.** `cycleDockTab(dir)` in `dock.js` (called by the keydown handler of `main.js`; `kindsOf(p)` is the one list of the tabs: hand, endgame cards, the storage of map 11, the played cards when the setting is the tray). Tab goes to the next tab of the shown seat, Shift + Tab to the previous one, round and round; a folded hand stays folded, so the user can tab through the tabs of a collapsed hand. Not in mini games, not while a dialog or the settings are open, not while typing. Listed on the Tab key of the keyboard pop-up (`shortcuts.js`).
- **Enter confirms the player choice** in the start dialog once a player is selected (`pov.js`, already there; checked). It is not in the keyboard pop-up on purpose.
- **Wave animation off.** `WAVE_ANIMATION = false` in `card-motion.js`: a wave is animated like a normal discard from the display for now (docs/animations_v01.md, Wave section). Code kept.
- **Public site: the logo is not a link.** `scripts/deploy_pages.py` (production branch only) turns `<a class="brand" href="/">` of every page into `<span class="brand">`; `parkposter.css` `span.brand` has no pointer events (no hover lift). The develop copy and the repository files keep the link to the park.

## Action-card glides, edge-led upgrades and token flights — 6 November 2026

Implemented the approved action-card animations in the sidebar and the optional full action-card strip below each player board. No replay state, backend rules or action resolution was changed.

### Behaviour

- **Order changes:** match each player's cards by action type across the old/new states. Every card that changes slot glides from its old rectangle to its actual new slot, 480 ms at 1×, cubic ease-in/out. This covers returns to 1, Clever/boost-down, boost-up to 5 and the other cards displaced by those moves. Chained actions animate the successive orders supplied by the replay; no invented intermediate action steps. The full strip's numbered binder clips remain fixed.
- **Upgrades:** the approved flip replaces an instant blue-to-red change; the card now turns **in place** around its vertical centre line (`rotateY` 0 to -180°, 650 ms at 1×) with no sliding, lifting or edge pivot (the earlier left-edge pivot with sideways recovery and viewport clamping is gone). This works for both the small sidebar icon and full artwork; the sidebar border and Marine Worlds variant badge use their old/new sides. Upgrade and reorder may run together.
- **Sidebar token add/remove:** Venom, Constriction and Multiplier badges glide between the move bar's centre (the invisible deck) and their exact icon attachment positions. Timing follows the existing 650 ms / 850 design-pixel glide, clamped to 650–1500 ms, scaled by the animation-speed setting. Tokens fade at the arrival/departure ends only. Duplicate tokens retain identity by multiset matching; a surviving badge closes a gap after the spent badge leaves, over 220 ms at 1×.
- **Hidden player info boxes:** token flights still run while Log or Chat is selected. The old and final icon/badge positions are measured with Control temporarily measurable but invisible, and all pane flags/styles are restored in the same synchronous call. The user's tab is not changed and Control does not flash. Specific badge targets use this measurement rather than a zero-sized hidden rectangle or the info box's generic right-edge point.
- **Tokens below the board:** existing full-card badges already render all three token types. Addition/removal stays immediate there; moving/flipping full-card copies also reflect the new token state immediately. There is no duplicate flight to the board strip.

### Integration and cleanup

`web/js/action-motion-plan.js` is a pure per-player classifier for action identity, reorder, side-I-to-II upgrade and token multiset changes. It excludes concealed orders and newly assigned identities. `web/js/action-motion.js` captures old/final geometry and rendered styles, and creates copies in the existing viewport overlay outside the zoomed body. `web/js/side-panel.js` and `web/js/zoo.js` add action type/seat/view and token type/index metadata. `web/css/card-motion.css` adds the action overlay's perspective and two faces.

`web/js/card-motion.js` captures before redraw and runs after final layout. All new effects use its existing animation registry, two-frame deferred start, speed scaling, hidden-target restoration and cancellation; autoplay therefore waits for reorders, flips, token flights and badge-gap closure. Changing speed also retimes effects already running. Backward seeks, initial loads, reduced motion and minigames show the final state immediately. Navigation/redraw and the existing scroll/resize interruption paths remove copies and restore real nodes. Existing card, display, building, temporary-tab and tray-fold behaviour is retained.

### Validation

- `node --test tests/action-motion-plan.test.mjs tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`: **32 passed** (six new action classifier cases; 26 existing card-motion cases). Includes all displaced identities, boost/return sequences, variants, duplicate tokens and simultaneous reorder/upgrade/token changes across both players. Syntax checks passed for the touched JavaScript modules.
- Isolated headless Chromium loaded the actual local frontend against a saved table 937181556 response. The fixture adds synthetic token states because that saved replay does not demonstrate every attack token. **48 transitions passed:** real replay reorders/upgrades and synthetic add/remove of all three token types, for both players on Control, Log and Chat pane states. Verified exact hidden badge endpoints equal the Control coordinates, both action-card sizes, instant board-strip tokens, busy registration and final cleanup. Additional checks passed for backward interruption, disabled full-card strip, reduced motion and all six animation speeds. No page JavaScript errors.
- Chat was represented by a synthetic pane in the replay page to exercise the hidden-Control path, not by a live multiplayer session. Browser tests used a cleared Chrome cache, a fresh profile and local `Cache-Control: no-store` responses; external network requests were blocked.
- A further real-time browser pass verified natural completion and changing speed while reorder, upgrade and token effects run. Hidden token endpoints were checked at 1366- and 1920-pixel window widths for both players. A combined reorder/token transition with duplicate multipliers also completed and restored the real cards/badges, including the delayed badge-gap closure; the shared busy state returned to idle.

The approved behaviour and implementation contract are recorded in `docs/animations_v01.md`. This is a local implementation; no deployment or BGA requests were made.

## Folder chips: size and cards leaving — 6 November 2026

- **Chips grew during the opening replenishment (root cause).** The chip copies of `tabCovers()` took their font size, border and radius from the page zoom (`scale()`), but the display row has a scale of its own on top of the page zoom (the same reason `shotOnScreen` measures with `getBoundingClientRect`). The copy's number was therefore too large (about 30 % at a 1366 px window, `Range` rect 6.84 x 13 against 5.25 x 10). The copy now uses the real zoom of each tab, `k = rect.width / offsetWidth`, and sets the font family, weight and size separately (the `font` shorthand of a computed style is not reliable). Text rects of the real and the copied chip are equal at 1366 and 1920 px.
- **A card leaving the display crossed above the chip.** The covers were only made for the display's own flights (`runDisplay`), not for cards taken to a player (`displayTake`, from `run`/`runOther`). `tabCovers()` now runs once at the end of `afterRender` whenever the display's cards changed, for the length of every flight of that render (`flightEnd`, the latest end of any `animate()` call, reset in `beforeRender`). The leaving ghost starts on the card's resting place in the first frame (no empty plate frame) and passes behind the chip.

## Display cards are dealt behind the folder numbers — 6 November 2026

- The number tabs (`.folnum`) lie on top of the display cards, but flights run in the overlay `#card-motion`, above the whole page: a card crossed the tab and slipped behind it when the real card appeared (a blink). `tabCovers()` in `card-motion.js` draws every tab again in the overlay (`.cm-tab`, `z-index: 5`, above `.cm-card` at 3; colours, border, radius and font read from the real tab, sizes times the page scale) for exactly the length of the display's flights (`spanEnd - shift`, an `animate()` so it follows the animation speed and counts as busy); called after `runDisplay` in `afterRender`. Covers sit exactly on the real tabs, so nothing changes visually except the layering. Checked in headless Chromium (first replenish of table 800966139): a card in flight passes behind tab 4, landed cards sit behind tabs 1 and 2.

## Display folders are always drawn — 6 November 2026

- `parkposter.css`: the tan plate behind every display slot (`.folder::before`, with the number tab `.folnum` on top) used to exist only while the folder held no card (`.folder:not(:has(.card))::before`). A card that is still hidden for its flight (first replenish, every later replenish) counts as a card, so its slot showed neither plate nor card until the flight landed. The plate is now always there and the card lies on top of it; the numbers are unchanged. Exception: `.folder:has(> .card.dim)::before { display: none }`, because a greyed-out card is see-through and the tan would shine through it.
- Checked in headless Chromium (table 800966139): empty display after the draft (six plates and numbers 1-6), first replenish mid-flight (plates under the slots that wait), later replenish (step 69 -> 70).

## Autoplay plays the same animations as a manual step — 5 November 2026

Autoplay (`playback.js` timer) and a click on Next take the same path (`stepBy(1)` -> `go` -> `render`); the differences came from what is still unfinished when the timer fires. Found by recording every flight (`Element.animate` in `#card-motion`) of the same steps in autoplay and by hand, and by tracing the display cards frame by frame.

- **Tray fold not awaited (cards flew differently).** (Removed later together with the unfold-and-play workaround of the folded tray, see "Folded tray: card motions play in place": there is no automatic fold any more.)
- **Other things autoplay now waits for (`cardAnimationsBusy`):** a running CSS transition inside `#dock` (`dockSettling`: a hand that is still sliding into place would be measured half way) and the opening effect of the reveal lightbox (`#draftbox.lbopen`, 1.7 s).
- **Display blinked and rippled at the start of an action.** Steps such as "X chooses action card" show a bar above the display, so the whole display moves by ~32 px between two steps. `runDisplay` took every card whose screen position changed for a card that slid left (the "domino" slide), hid all six real cards and ran six ghosts 70 ms apart: a blink followed by a ripple, on every action. A card that keeps its slot (`old.state.display.indexOf(key) === i`) is no longer slid; only cards that really change slot are (replenish, cards behind a gap).
- **Greyed-out cards arrived in a ripple.** Display cards that cannot be taken get `.dim` (opacity .35, grey). The ghosts of cards that slide or fly were bright and the real card (dim) appeared when each ghost landed, so the greying came card by card. `shotOnScreen` records `dim`, and `ghost()` draws the picture grey and see-through the same way (no shadow), so a dimmed display looks the same while it moves.
- Files: `web/js/card-motion.js`. Checked in headless Chromium on tables 800966139, 902906029, 714192218 (autoplay vs manual steps at several speeds, steps 10-60): flights identical except for the pixel rounding of some positions; no display animation on the steps that only move the board; the real replenish (step 69 -> 70 of 800966139) still plays. `node --test tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`: 26 passing.
- Not changed: the delay between two autoplay steps is still `1000 / speed` ms and the timer only fires when nothing is busy, so a step never starts while a flight, the tray fold or the lightbox effect is still running.

## Played animals and sponsors — 5 November 2026

Implemented the approved card-play motions across Top/bottom, Tabbed and all three Side-by-side drawer states. This adds animal/sponsor flights; existing conservation-project flips and draw/discard designs remain as documented below.

- `web/js/card-motion-plan.js`: new `playedCards(before, after)` classifies public additions to each player's `animals`/`sponsors`, with display origin only when that actual card left the display. Hidden-hand plays use the newly public played identity. The other-player discard calculation now subtracts played hand cards, preventing a simultaneous unrelated discard from causing a false discard flight. Reordering and expedition removals produce no play. Added classifier regression tests.
- `web/js/zoo.js`: map containers expose `data-zoo-seat`, played blocks expose `data-played-seat`, and beside-map cards boxes carry their owner. `fullCardWidth` is shared by actual full-drawer fitting and `fullDrawerCardWidth(row, player)` for closed-drawer flight targets. Corrected the stale two-thirds overlap comment to the CSS's actual 80%; stack CSS/layout is unchanged.
- `web/js/card-motion.js`: capture source and existing pile rectangles before renderer reconciliation, then animate against the final fitted DOM. Public played cards fly face-up; POV origins use the hand/fan or display, non-POV origins use the right edge/vertical centre of their own info box with a 16% fade-in. Fallback when no hand card is displayed also uses that player's box and public artwork. Artwork respects snake-safe settings. Continuous translation, upright rotation and resizing use the same cubic glide clock: 650 ms per 850 page/design pixels, 650–1500 ms, 140 ms stagger, all scaled by the existing animation-speed setting. No destination scrolling.
- Top/bottom: final animal/sponsor pile slot beside the correct board, whether visible or offscreen. Tabbed: immediately select the owner when hidden, complete every played-card flight, hold for exactly 1,000 wall-clock milliseconds, then restore the prior tab without a transition or saved-setting change. The animation busy check covers the tab visit and tray-unfold wait, so autoplay does not advance during either. A canceled visit clears its return timer and avoids overwriting a manual tab change.
- Side-by-side: closed drawers stay closed and receive flights at the right map area (82% across/52% down), ending at the **expanded drawer's fitted card size** and vanishing in the final 18%. Small drawers receive the card in their actual single column per kind; full drawers receive it in their actual alternating two-column piles.
- Twitch correction: keep initial pile geometry, let the renderer fit the final layout once, and animate affected existing cards' positions and sizes over the full incoming flight window. The mock's early 260 ms reflow and explicit intermediate pile reconstruction are not used. Existing live card nodes are animated, retaining sponsor cubes and other overlays. Visible hand gaps close smoothly; folded POV trays use the existing temporary-unfold lifecycle before playing.
- Cleanup uses the existing overlay/hidden-target/animation registry: navigation, reduced-motion changes, resize, scrolling, hand interaction and view changes cancel and restore visibility. Initial loads, backward seeks and reduced motion do not create flights. No backend files, storage schema, dependencies or build step changed. Detailed behavior is also recorded in `docs/animations_v01.md`.
- Validation: `node --test tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs` passes all 19 tests; syntax checks pass for the three changed JS modules. Isolated Chromium loaded the real frontend with saved replay 937181556 and verified 24 card-play cases across both players, both card types, all requested layouts/drawer states, fan, actual display-origin plays and a folded tray. Checked final positions/sizes, preserved viewport, completed tab holds/returns and overlay cleanup, with zero page errors. An additional real six-to-seven animal transition verified every existing pile card retained its initial rectangle before the synchronized reflow; separate checks passed for interrupted navigation, manual tab cancellation, active speed changes and reduced motion. Browser cache was cleared before runs and the local test server used `Cache-Control: no-store`. This validates replay behavior with a saved backend response; live-game/fork/sandbox action submission and CDN deployment were not exercised.


## Card movement animations — 5 November 2026

Implemented the approved `animations_v01.md` designs for both hand layouts. This section supersedes earlier descriptions of starting-hand drops and `handfly.js` reveal arrivals; ordinary page-load fan rise/spread remains in `dock.js`.

- New `web/js/card-motion-plan.js` classifies visible hand differences: deck/display/targeted draws, ordinary discards, Sunbathing, Glide and Pouch. Display departures match card identity; Pouch source comes from the changed `player.under` entry. Map pouch uses an unbranded pouch. Played, stored and transferred departures are excluded from discard effects. Targeted draws use the `<SEARCH-species>` marker. BGA's `sells` wording identifies Sunbathing.
- New `web/js/card-motion.js` captures visible card geometry before redraw and runs Web Animations after the final layout. Its pointer-transparent viewport overlay sits outside the zoomed body; measurements use viewport coordinates and card dimensions adjusted for the page scale. Only the displayed hand is animated; unknown cards are excluded. Artwork comes from the rendered card, respecting snake-safe images. Pouch portraits use the source animal's current large artwork and name.
- Floating hand: face-up display glide (650 ms); Soft dissolve (380 ms, 80 ms stagger), with gap closing after departures. Fan deck arrivals preserve the existing 520 ms / 110 ms rise-and-deal keyframes and easing, generalized to any draw count. Fan display and targeted arrivals use one continuous 650 ms upright flight with late rotation into the final fan angle, without raising the fan or pausing above it.
- Both layouts: Sunbeam pickup (800 ms), Bubble lift (900 ms), Portrait canvas pouch (900 ms per card, 160 ms stagger, cloth reaction and closing). Targeted draw: originally a Species spotlight, replaced by the magnifying glass (see the last section). No cream label/pill or separate pouch source box.
- `main.js` calls the before/after-render lifecycle. `handfly.js` preserves the `setPending` entry point used by `reveal.js`; selected reveal cards are dealt after the lightbox closes. `dock.js` no longer starts a competing setup deal/drop. `playback.js` marks forward stepping and waits for active card effects during autoplay; direct seeks/backward navigation remain immediate. Fork commits mark their resulting transition as forward.
- **Deck draws use the original deal in both layouts, 5 November 2026 (user decision, replaces the Direct glide for deck draws).** `arrive()` in `card-motion.js` animates the real card node (no overlay ghost) for every `deck` arrival, one card or the whole starting hand. Fan: the original rise-and-deal, 520 ms, 110 ms stagger, `cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.08)`, from `translateY(240 * --hk * 1.2)`. Floating tray: the original drop, tilt 9 degrees to alternating sides (`index % 2`), fade in, a 6 px dip at 78 %, then rest; 100 ms stagger; plus a 350 ms delay when the tray has just been drawn (`.handtray.enter`, the slide-up of `parkposter.css`). What changed against the old `dock.js` drop: the cards no longer start 260 px above their slot but at the **top edge of the viewport** (centre 40 px from the top, the height of the move bar, as BGA's cards do): `fall = (card centre - 40) / page scale`, at least 260 px, and the flight time grows with it, `clamp(320 + 0.45 * fall, 560, 800)` ms (about 730 ms for a hand at the bottom of the screen), easing `ease-in-out`, fade-in finished at 8 % so the card seems to come out from behind the bar. `dock.js` still returns early for the setup deal (`introState = null`): card-motion owns every deal. The tray's constants are my own: `bga_animations.md` holds no card-flight timing (its 300-800 ms values belong to markers, action cards and the hand panel); take BGA's real numbers from `Cards.js` / `arknova.css` when they are saved. Checked in headless Chromium with replay 937181556 (single draw at step 32, starting hand at step 9, tray and fan): keyframes, durations and stagger as listed, no page errors; the fall was seen in frames. Not checked: the 350 ms slide-up delay after a draft in the browser, autoplay.
- **Floating tray: the discard is the deal played backwards (5 November 2026, user decision, replaces the soft dissolve in the tray; the fan keeps the dissolve).** `depart()` in `card-motion.js`, `kind === 'discard'` and `S.handMode !== 'fan'`: the ghost of each card dips 6 px, then rises, tilted 9 degrees to alternating sides (`index % 2`), to the top edge of the viewport (centre 40 px from the top, the move bar) and fades out in the last 8 %; 100 ms stagger, `ease-in-out`, flight time `clamp(320 + 0.45 * rise, 560, 800)` ms with `rise` the card's distance to the top in page pixels (the same rule as the deal). Checked in headless Chromium (replay 937181556, step 11, four cards): the ghosts rise one after another and are gone; no page errors.
- **Settings pop-up changes (5 November 2026, `settings.js`, `parkposter.css`).** Control order: Animation speed, Endgame live count, Timeline in the control panel, Autoplay speed. The animation speed bubble reads `1x`, `1.5x`, `2x` (the plain number plus x, like the autoplay slider; it was `1.0x`). Board order: Player boards, Cards in hand (was "Hand display"), Played cards, then Action cards under the board, Map placement preview, Show enclosure borders as before. Player boards list: Side by side, Top / bottom, Tabbed ("Tabbed (one board, arrows)" is now "Tabbed"). The greyed "Tray in the hand dock" entry (fan active) is darker: `rgba(23,38,43,.6)` in `settings.js` and `.setopt:disabled` (was .22 / .3). "Apply discounts to animals" has a ? with a sticky note like the Map placement preview ("Automatically apply discounts from partner zoos, Expert on Small Animals and Expert on Large Animals to animal cards"); the note code is now `showNote(help, note)` + `helpMark(label, note)`, both notes are children of the modal. Icons: Animation speed has a new stopwatch (`stopwatch`, gold), Autoplay speed keeps the double arrow (now coral `#e58b6b`), Endgame live count has a shield (`shield`, lavender, was the flag), Action cards under the board has a new row-of-cards icon (`actions`, purple, was a copy of the Played cards icon). Every tab has one colour per icon: Control gold / lavender / blue / coral; Board green / blue / yellow / purple / pink / teal (the border icon was `#d8b45a`, too close to the yellow, now `#6fd0c3`); Cards yellow / salmon / lavender / grey. The unused `flag` icon was removed.
- **One invisible deck for the tray (5 November 2026, `card-motion.js` `deckPoint()`).** Every deck draw and every discard in the floating tray now starts / ends at one fixed point, the centre of the header row (`.movebar`, in viewport pixels), instead of straight above / below each card, so the cards of a draw fan out from one spot and a discard converges on it. Draw: `translate(deck - card centre) rotate(+-9deg)` (page-scale corrected), distance `max(260, hypot)`, flight `clamp(320 + 0.45 * distance, 560, 800)` ms; discard: the same point and rule backwards. This replaces the "top edge of the viewport, 40 px from the top" start described in the two entries above (the vertical rule, the tilt, the bounce, the stagger and the timings are unchanged). Settings: the Action cards under the board icon is a ring binder (`actions`); the discounts note names "Expert in Small Animals" / "Expert in Large Animals".
- **Speed setting (the slower baseline was removed, 5 November 2026):** `S.animationSpeed` defaults to 1 and runs every card effect at its documented duration, hold and delay (`original / speed`; a 650 ms flight takes 650 ms at 1x, 325 ms at 2x). Animation speed is the first row in Settings → Control, with discrete options **0.25x, 0.5x, 1.0x, 1.5x, 2.0x, 3.0x**. It is saved as `localStorage.settings.animationSpeed`, validates loaded values and falls back to 1. Changing the setting adjusts active animations' playback rates too. Autoplay speed remains independent. Reduced-motion preferences bypass the effects. Redraw/interruption, viewport changes, scrolling, POV/layout changes and hand interaction restore hidden targets and remove overlays.
- Speed-setting validation: all seven classifier tests pass; syntax checks and an isolated runtime check verified the six values (this was done while the baseline was still doubled; with the doubling removed, 1x is the original timing, 0.25x is four times it). Browser control timed out, so refreshed UI inspection and a cache-bypassing browser reload could not be completed. Existing `/js/*` and `/css/*` production headers require cache revalidation, and the development server uses `no-store`; no CDN purge or deployment was performed.
- Validation: `node --test tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs` covers identity, species, special losses, hidden cards, reordering and non-discard departures (7 passing tests). Syntax checks passed. Replay 937181556 loaded and advanced through the university targeted draw without recorded JavaScript errors. Full midpoint visual checks and the required desktop/mobile replay/fork/sandbox matrix remain unverified because browser control stalled; do not treat this as full visual QA. No backend files changed and no deployment performed.


Audience: an AI coding agent, not a human. This document describes **the final state** of every frontend change made locally on top of the baseline, as instructions you can apply to a codebase whose backend has moved on. Changes that were later overwritten or abandoned are deliberately not described. The folder this file lives in is a **reference implementation**: when this text and the reference files disagree, the reference files win, and you report the difference.

## 0. How to use this document

Status: this change set has been applied to the repo that also has the live games (section 13 lists what the merge added: live play, render reconciliation, loading progress). The reference files in `web/` are the merged result; a later port to another codebase follows the same rules.

Scope
- Only `web/` changed (plus `docs/frontend_architecture.md` and the local-only helpers of section 10). The backend (`src/`, `data/`, `data_manual/`) was not edited and **no change here REQUIRES a backend change** (sections 9.6 / 9.7 list optional improvements marked BACKEND NEEDED). The frontend only reads fields the replay API already returned when these changes were made. Fields read by the new code (check each still exists with the same meaning on the live API; if one was renamed, adapt the read in the frontend only): per step `fork` (truthy = the Fork button is enabled), `index`, `actor`, `irreversible` / `irreversible_reason`, `actions` (fork: legal moves), `sandbox`, `label`, `label_pov[]`, `move_id`, `options` (`prompt`, `seat`, `kinds`, `pieces`, `effects`, `take`, `sponsors`, `discard`, `skip`, `association`, `value`, `only`, `cards`), `engine` (`source`, `status`), `engine_state` (fork / sandbox), `state` (`turn`, `phase` setup / turn / final_turns / over, `draft` {`stage` pick1 / pick2 / keep / done, `offers`, `picked`, `kept`}, `current_action` {`seat`, `slot`, `strength`}, `active_player`, `display`, `conservation_options`, `projects_in_play`, `base_projects`, `players[]` {`hand`, `endgame_hand`, `action_cards[]` {`type`, `level`, `variant`}, `hand_limit`, `initial_offer`, `icons`, `tokens`, `x_tokens`, `score`, ...}); per replay `table_id`, `players[]` {`seat`, `id`, `name`, `color`}, `marine_worlds`, `cards`, `base_projects`, `base_pool`, `maps`, `shapes`, `setup`, `sandbox`; the fork response also carries `fork` {`step`, `seed`}. The exact set is whatever the modules read: grep `S.replay.` and `st.` in `web/js` if in doubt. The BGA profile link of a player name is built from `players[].id`. If the live API renamed or dropped such a field, adapt the read in the frontend; do not touch the backend.
- The live site serves `web/` with FastAPI `StaticFiles`. There is no build step and none may be introduced. JS is native ES modules.
- Applies identically to `replay.html`, `fork.html`, `sandbox.html` (one viewer, three modes: `replay.html` sets no flag; `fork.html` sets `window.FORK_MODE = true`; `sandbox.html` loads the viewer only with `?play=1` and a stored game and then sets `window.FORK_MODE` and `window.SANDBOX_MODE`; `state.js` exports `FORK`, `SANDBOX`). The sandbox counts as `FORK` everywhere below (so it reads the eyes from the `pov` URL parameter only and never uses localStorage for them).

Strategy (the live backend and possibly parts of the live frontend changed since the baseline, so do not overwrite the live `web/` with this folder)
1. Check the live `web/js/replay.js`: if it still is the baseline file (3021 lines, sha256 starts `8322cad87526e14e`), section 1 can be applied by copying the modules. If it differs, diff it against the baseline and port the differences into the modules by hand (a former top-level `let foo` is `S.foo`; function names are unchanged; see the module table in `docs/frontend_architecture.md`).
2. Sections 1-9 are ONE atomic port, not independent steps: the reference modules and HTML files are the FINAL state, and `main.js` imports every module (`sidebar`, `settings`, `frames`, `notips`, ...) and wires elements (`#tabControl`, `#settings`, `#timeline`, ...) that only the final HTML has. So take the final `replay.html`, `fork.html`, `sandbox.html`, the complete `table.css` (3.3, which already contains the CSS of 8.7, 8.9, 8.10 and 9.5: do NOT add those rules a second time when a later section says "append to table.css") and all modules together, then work through sections 2-9 to check each behaviour and to port what the live code does differently. Zero console errors can only be expected once everything is in place.
3. Dependencies between sections: 8.10 (`settings.js`) imports `sidebar.js` (4) and `setSpeed` from `playback.js`, and `alignControl` in `sidebar.js` reads the `#timeline` state of 8.10; 8.9 and 8.10 describe the `<header class="bar">` that 3.1 shows in its final form; 5.2 needs the `.placepick` / `.placemenu` CSS that is in 3.3; section 9 builds on 3.2 and 5.1. Sections 2 and 3 change the layout of the whole page: after them the pages look different on purpose.
4. Do not "improve" the old CSS while porting. `web/css/replay.css` is intentionally one non-consolidated file in which later rules override earlier ones; reordering or merging rules changed computed layout by 1-4 px in an experiment.
5. Local-only helpers are listed in section 10: do not port them, but read the caching advice there.
6. Interfaces the frontend relies on (API calls, URL parameters, browser storage) are listed in section 0.1.

Vocabulary: "design viewport" = 1920x1080 CSS px. "zoomed px" = the unit `getBoundingClientRect` and mouse coordinates return inside the zoomed body. "layout px" = the unit of `offsetWidth`, `clientWidth`, `scrollHeight` and of CSS lengths.


### 0.1 Interfaces the frontend relies on (check each against the live site)

API calls (all JSON): `GET /api/tables/{id}/replay` (the replay); `POST /api/tables/{id}/replay` (body: the raw text of an uploaded log, when the URL has `source=upload`; the log itself is read from IndexedDB, see below); `POST /api/tables/{id}/fork` (body `{step, seed}`; the fork page's start state); `POST /api/fork/apply` (body `{state, action, names, ...}`; plays one legal action in a fork); `POST /api/sandbox/new`, `POST /api/sandbox/apply`, `POST /api/sandbox/edit`, `GET /api/sandbox/map/{id}`, `GET /api/sandbox/maps?marine_worlds=true|false` (sandbox). Static JSON: `/enclosures/sprites.json`, `/icons/icons.json`, `/icons/names.json` (a failed load falls back to an empty object). Live play (`play.html`, `play.js`, `end.html`): `GET /api/live/options`, `GET /api/live/config` (`{ws_base}`; empty = poll every 2 s), `POST /api/games` (create; returns the two seat tokens), `GET /api/games/E<n>`, `POST /api/games/E<n>/join`, `GET .../setup`, `GET .../state`, `POST .../preview`, `POST .../actions` (`{version, action, request_id}`), `POST .../concede`, `POST .../timeout`, `GET|POST .../abandon`, `POST .../abandon/withdraw`, `POST .../abandon/answer`, `GET .../result`, and the WebSocket `<ws_base>/ws/E<n>?s=<token>` (messages `state`, `lobby`, `status`, `abandon`, `waiting`; the client sends `ping`, the server answers `pong`). The seat token goes in the header `X-Seat-Token` (or `?s=`). Other: `GET /api/lookup?q=`, `GET /api/tables/{id}/log`, `POST /api/tables/{id}/verify` (used by `index.html` / `submit.html`). The exact request bodies are in `load.js`, `fork.js`, `sandbox.js`, `play.js` (reference files win). Check the HTTP status before reading the body: error bodies have a `status` field too.

URL parameters (`state.js` exports `params`, `table`): `table` (game id; the replay page redirects to `/` when it is missing or not numeric), `step` and `seed` (fork page), `pov` (fork page: `all` | `0` | `1`, see 8.8), `source=upload` (replay of a manually submitted log), `play=1` (sandbox page: start the stored game), `game=E<n>` and `s=<seat token>` (play page; `end.html` takes `game`), and the hash `#<step>` or `#<step>.<frame>` (replay; written by `go()`, read once at start).

Browser storage: localStorage `settings` (8.10), `pov:<table id>` (8.8), `sidebarTab` (`control` | `log`, 4), `dockSel` and `dockHidden` (the hand dock: selected cards and folded state, `dock.js`); sessionStorage `sandboxGame`; localStorage `playToken.<game id>`, `playerName`, `playAlerts` (turn alert on/off), `liveUnlocked` (set by the start page; without it `play.html` shows "coming soon"), `progress.<key>` (`progress.js`: past loading times) (the sandbox game handed from the lobby to `?play=1`); IndexedDB database `ark-nova-replay`, object store `logs` (`logstore.js`, a classic script: uploaded logs are too big for sessionStorage; written by `submit.html` and by `import.html`, read by `load.js` for `source=upload`). `import.html` is opened by the browser extension (`extension/button.js`) with `?table=<id>` and receives the BGA log with `window.postMessage` (`ark-nova-import-ready` from the page, `ark-nova-log` from the extension).

## 1. Module split of `web/js/replay.js` (no visual or behavioural change)

Before: `web/js/replay.js`, one IIFE (3021 lines, ~60 top-level `let`, ~200 functions) loaded with `<script src="/js/replay.js">`.
After: ES modules in `web/js/`, entry point `web/js/main.js`. The module list and roles are in `docs/frontend_architecture.md` (section "Modules"); the modules are: `action-bar, association, board, cards, dock, fork, frames, icons, layout, load, log-labels, main, notips, playback, pov, projects, sandbox, shared, side-panel, sidebar, state, util, zoo`; plus `settings` (8.10); `logstore.js` is unchanged and **stays a classic script** (it defines the global `LogStore`; it is loaded by `replay.html` and `submit.html`).

Steps
1. Add the module files. Delete `web/js/replay.js`.
2. `replay.html`, `fork.html`: replace `<script src="/js/replay.js"></script>` by `<script type="module" src="/js/main.js"></script>`. Keep `<script src="/js/logstore.js"></script>` before it (replay.html; `submit.html` loads it too) and the inline `window.FORK_MODE = true;` script before it (fork.html).
3. `sandbox.html`: the inline script that creates the script element must create a module: `const s = document.createElement('script'); s.type = 'module'; s.src = '/js/main.js'; document.body.append(s);`.
4. Do not change the inline mode flags; `state.js` reads them once and exports `FORK`, `SANDBOX`.

Semantics to preserve when porting by hand
- Former top-level `let` variables live on the exported object `S` (`state.js`). `params`, `table`, `FORK`, `SANDBOX` are exported consts of `state.js`.
- The tail of the old IIFE (init code) is wrapped in `boot()` in `main.js` (a module cannot `return` at top level).
- The import graph is cyclic on purpose; it is safe because modules only use imported functions at call time. Never read an imported binding in a top-level statement (except `state.js` exports).
- New mutable state: add a field to `S` in `state.js` with a comment.

Dead code that was removed (delete it if the live code still has it; nothing referenced it): `iconImg`, `SUMMARY`, `associationSummary`, `projectRow`, `SB_TYPES`, `renderInitialDiscard`, the unreachable tail of `renderForkMoves` (it only clears `#forkpanel` and keeps the last error now; legal moves are played from the action bar, see `forkBar`), and `piecesRow` and the `PIECE_SCALE` constant (section 5), `naturalTop` in `state.js`. The CSS rules `.forkhead .forkgroup .forkfilter .forkdiscard .forknone` are unused (verified: no class name appears in `web/js`) and were left in `replay.css`; delete them if you like.

Verification: load `/replay.html?table=<id>#<step>`, `/fork.html?table=<id>&step=<n>&seed=1`, `/sandbox.html`; zero console errors; step through a replay with the arrow keys; click through a fork (action card, build, association task) and a sandbox setup. In the reference run 126 scripted scenarios gave identical normalised DOM before/after the split (except sandbox games, whose card deal is random).

---

## 2. Uniform scaling: the desktop layout is always the 1920 px layout, zoomed to the window

Problem solved: width / height breakpoints and fixed-size boards gave small laptop windows (e.g. 1280x550) stacked zoos, a drawer and ~7 screens of scrolling. Now every desktop window shows the same layout, only smaller or bigger (like BGA).

Behaviour
- Window width >= 900 px ("desktop mode", `S.scaled = true`): the page is laid out as a 1920 px wide page and `document.body.style.zoom = clientWidth / 1920`. Height does not change the layout; the page scrolls vertically.
- Window width < 900 px: nothing is scaled and the old narrow-window rules apply (phones are out of scope for now; there is only a simple fallback, section 3).

Files and exact changes
1. `web/js/layout.js`: exports `fitScale()` (below). `main.js` `boot()` calls `fitScale()` as its first statement; the `resize` listener in `layout.js` calls `fitScale(); fitSidebar(); fitDisplay();` (`fitSidebar`, `fitDisplay`: section 4).
```js
const DESIGN_W = 1920, DESKTOP_MIN = 900;
export function fitScale() {
  const root = document.documentElement, w = root.clientWidth;
  S.scaled = w >= DESKTOP_MIN;
  S.scale = S.scaled ? w / DESIGN_W : 1;
  document.body.style.zoom = S.scaled ? String(S.scale) : '';
  if (S.scaled) {
    root.style.setProperty('--vw', DESIGN_W / 100 + 'px');
    root.style.setProperty('--vh', window.innerHeight / S.scale / 100 + 'px');
  } else {
    root.style.removeProperty('--vw'); root.style.removeProperty('--vh');
  }
}
```
2. `web/js/state.js`: new fields on `S`: `scale: 1`, `scaled: false`, `sbTab: 'control'`.
3. `web/css/replay.css` (mechanical transformation of the baseline file; if the live `replay.css` changed, redo it by these rules):
   - Every `@media` block that tests only width / height is split in two: (a) the original condition AND `(max-width: 899px)`, which keeps the old behaviour for narrow windows; (b) if the original condition is true at 1920x1080, a copy of the same rules under `@media (min-width: 900px)` **at the same position in the file** (cascade order unchanged). Blocks that are false at 1920x1080 get no desktop copy. Blocks testing `hover` or `prefers-reduced-motion` are untouched.
   - Every `Nvh` / `Nvw` becomes `calc(var(--vh, 1vh) * N)` / `calc(var(--vw, 1vw) * N)`. (Inside a zoomed body the real `vh` / `vw` would be multiplied by the zoom a second time; `fitScale` sets `--vh` = `innerHeight / scale / 100` px and `--vw` = 19.2 px on `<html>`.)
   - `html { overflow-y: scroll; }` must be present (the reference has it in `table.css` at the top and also in `replay.css`; one of them is enough) so a scrollbar appearing never changes the width the scale is computed from.
   - Remove the drawer rules (`.asidetoggle`, the `aside` drawer block and its reduced-motion line); the drawer no longer exists (section 3).
4. Rules for all future frontend code
   - Never add a width / height `@media` query or a raw `vh` / `vw` unit for the desktop layout; use `--vh` / `--vw`, and keep `(max-width: 899px)` in conditions of narrow-window rules.
   - Chrome reports `getBoundingClientRect` and mouse coordinates in zoomed px but `offsetWidth`, `clientWidth`, `scrollHeight` in layout px. When mixing them divide the zoomed ones by `S.scale` (see `fitDisplay`).
   - Elements with `position: fixed` (card preview, pile popup, sidebar) are inside the zoomed body and scale with it; they need no extra handling.

Known limits: below 1920 px all text is proportionally smaller (intended). Phones are not designed yet.

Verification: zoo width divided by window width is the same (0.404) at 1280x551, 1536x730, 1920x1080, 2560x1300; live resizing 1920 -> 1280 -> 1000 -> 2560 -> 1920 keeps the layout; no horizontal overflow; at exactly 1920x1080 the DOM is identical to the pre-scaling code.

---

## 3. Page structure: move bar on top, fixed sidebar (Control | Log), shared area, maps below

Target layout (the display element and the association element are equally high, see 3.4; design viewport; identical for replay, fork and sandbox):
```
+--------------------------------------------+------------------+
| MOVE BAR (step text | pending decision)    | [Control] [Log]  |   <- sidebar, position: fixed, always in view
+---------------------+----------------------+------------------+
| Display             | association board    | control panel    |
| (conservation track | (project spaces above| (2 button rows;  |
|  + folders + rep.   |  and below)          |  optional third  |
|  track)             |                      |  row: timeline)  |
+---------------------+----------------------+                  |
| zoo / map of seat 0 | zoo / map of seat 1  | info (trackers)  |
+---------------------+----------------------+------------------+
```
In the Log tab the sidebar shows the move list instead of control panel + info.

3.1 HTML (`replay.html`; `fork.html` and `sandbox.html` get the same structure plus their extras)
- Removed: the full-width top `<header class="bar">`, the second move bar `#current` below it, the old `<aside>` with the move list and its `<h2>Moves</h2>`.
- New body of `#app`: `.layout` containing `<main>` and `<aside class="sidebar">`. All ids used by the JS are unchanged. Link `/css/table.css` after `/css/replay.css` in the three pages.
```html
<div id="app" hidden>
  <div class="layout">

        <button id="tabLog" class="sbtab" type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false">Log</button>
      </div>
      <div id="paneControl" class="sbpane">
      <header class="bar">
        <div class="controls" role="group" aria-label="Replay controls">
          <button id="first" title="First step (Home)" aria-label="First step">&#9198;</button>
          <button id="prev" title="Back one step (Left arrow)" aria-label="Back one step">&#9194;&#xFE0E;</button>

          <button id="settings" class="settingsbtn" title="Settings (S)" aria-label="Settings"><svg class="gearicon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10.29 3.57 L10.57 1.09 L13.43 1.09 L13.71 3.57 L16.75 4.83 L18.70 3.28 L20.72 5.30 L19.17 7.25 L20.43 10.29 L22.91 10.57 L22.91 13.43 L20.43 13.71 L19.17 16.75 L20.72 18.70 L18.70 20.72 L16.75 19.17 L13.71 20.43 L13.43 22.91 L10.57 22.91 L10.29 20.43 L7.25 19.17 L5.30 20.72 L3.28 18.70 L4.83 16.75 L3.57 13.71 L1.09 13.43 L1.09 10.57 L3.57 10.29 L4.83 7.25 L3.28 5.30 L5.30 3.28 L7.25 4.83Z M12 8.4a3.6 3.6 0 1 0 0 7.2a3.6 3.6 0 1 0 0-7.2Z"/></svg></button>
          <label class="jump">Move <input id="jump" type="number" min="0" aria-label="Jump to move"></label>
```
