With the map as wide as the display element (about 875 px at the 1920 px layout) the zoo box is about 520 px high and the page scrolls a little in a window as high as 1080 px; nothing is fitted to the height of the window (an earlier version fitted the map to the end of the sidebar and made it tiny in browser windows). There is no drawer chip, size button or association bar in this mode (they belong to the drawer).

- **Chip and bar.** `renderZoo` calls `playedDrawer(box, p, seat, narrow)` right after the name tab. It adds `.drbar` (absolute, `right: 22px; top: -38px`, flex) holding the size button `.drexp` (only while the drawer is open) and the chip `.drtab`: 38 px high like the name tab, charcoal `#30373C`, Bowlby One 17 px, rounded top, reading "Cards", the number of ALL played cards (animals + sponsors) in a pill that carries the player colour (`--pc` set on the button, `color-mix(in srgb, var(--pc) 72%, #30373C)`) and an arrow that is always â–¼ (turned 180 degrees while open: `.drtab.open i`), in both sizes. The hand floats over the lower edge of the page, so there is no bar under the map. Open / close: the chip, and Esc (not while a card pile or a `.modalback` dialog is open). `S.drawerSeats[seat]` says which boards are open; `.zoo.drawer-open` raises the board above its neighbour.
- **The sheet.** `.sheetclip` is a child of the map row (`renderZoo` appends `box._sheetclip` to the `.zooRow`; `.zoo.drawer-open .zooRow { position: relative }`), absolute with `inset: 0`, `overflow: hidden`, radius 16 px: exactly the map's place and height whatever the map or the window, nothing is measured. `.sheet` fills it: flat butter `#F7EFD3`, no frame, no lip, no title row; the played cards are the same `playedBlock` as under the map. `.zooRow > .sheetclip { width: auto; height: auto }` is needed because the rule for the parts of the row (`.zooRow > *`: width 100%, no radius) would win otherwise.
- **Expanded** (`S.drawerFull[seat]` true): the sheet covers the whole map, two stacks per kind, nothing shifts. `fitPlayed()` sets `--cw` of `.played` so that the tallest stack (half the cards of a kind per column, 0.2794 x width visible per covered card) and the four columns fit: 60-170 px wide. The free width is spread evenly (`.sheet .played { justify-content: space-evenly }`).
- **Narrow** (`.zoo.nrw`, the default; the class is `nrw`, not `side`, because `.side { margin-bottom: .8rem }` in replay.css gave the sheet a gap): the map (`.zooRow > :nth-child(2)`) slides left over the project bonus panel and the association strip (`:nth-child(3)`) tucks under the map; the sheet (`left: var(--sd-left)`, `right: 0`, radius only on the right) takes both freed areas (about 238 px at the 1920 px layout, with a shadow on its left edge where the map lies over it). `fitPlayed()` sets `--sd-b` (map shift), `--sd-s` (strip shift) and `--sd-left` on every `.zoo.drawer-open` box from the widths of the three parts of the row (`getBoundingClientRect` divided by the zoom; the parts can be SVG, which has no `offsetWidth`), and `--cw` of `.sheet.nrw .played` from the longest pile (48-120 px, one column per kind: `playedDrawer` joins the two stacks of each `.pkind`; the heading is 13 px without the action icon).
- **Size button.** `.drexp` (four corner arrows, "Expand over the map" / "Shrink beside the map") switches the size of THIS board only: `S.drawerFull` is an object seat -> boolean (missing = narrow), so each board's drawer is expanded or narrow on its own (one can be expanded while the other stays narrow, and a closed drawer keeps its size); it fires `drawerfull` (`settings.js` saves it as `drawerFull: { 0: bool, 1: bool }` in the `settings` entry and still reads the old single boolean as the size of both boards), so the next opening has the size of the last one. The switch wipes: `S.drawerAnim = { seat, t, kind: 'resize' }` puts `.rz` on the box of that seat only, narrow -> expanded: the sheet (already drawn at full width) is revealed by `clip-path` (`sd-wipe-out`, from the narrow width to the whole map) while the map and strip slide back (`sd-map-in`, 360 ms). Expanded -> narrow is the reverse and also a slide: the redraw has already replaced the expanded sheet by the narrow one, so the click handler clones the old `.sheetclip` first and `shrinkGhost(seat, ghost)` (zoo.js) appends the copy (`.sheetclip.ghost`, `aria-hidden`, its `.enter` removed) to the new `.zooRow`; `.zoo.nrw.drawer-open .sheetclip.ghost` (parkposter.css) overrides the narrow sheet's placement (`left: 0; right: 0; z-index: 20; pointer-events: none`) and runs `sd-wipe-in` (360 ms, the same curve): its `clip-path` goes from the whole map to the narrow width (`inset(0 0 0 var(--sd-left))`) while the map and strip slide aside (`sd-map-out`), and it fades out over the last third so that the real narrow sheet below takes over without a jump; it is removed on `animationend` (fallback timer 600 ms). Reduced motion: no ghost, no animation.
- **Animation.** `openDrawer(seat)` sets `S.drawerAnim = { seat, t }`; the box gets `.fresh` (narrow) or the sheet `.enter` (expanded) only when that is under 700 ms old, so a redraw of an open drawer (stepping through the game) does not replay it. `closeDrawer(seat, box)` puts `.closing` on the board first and draws the board without the sheet after `TRAY_IN_MS + 20` ms (300 ms + 20; the chip and Esc go through it: `box._closeDrawer`). `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`: no animation, the drawer opens and closes at once (`reducedMotion()` and a media query).
  - **Narrow: a door, the sheet never moves.** The sheet lies at its final place all the time, behind the map and the strip (`.zoo.nrw.drawer-open .sheetclip { z-index: 0 }` against the map's 3 and the strip's 1). Opening: the map and the strip slide left (`sd-map-out`, 400 ms, `cubic-bezier(.3, .7, .2, 1)`; the strip travels farther than the map, so it tucks under it) and uncover the sheet, right edge first; the chip arrow turns in 400 ms (`.zoo.nrw .drtab.open i.enter`). Closing: the map and strip slide back over the sheet (`sd-map-in`, 300 ms, `ease-in`), the arrow turns back in 300 ms. There is no sheet animation in this size (`zoo.js` gives the sheet `.enter` only when it is not narrow; `.zoo:not(.nrw).closing .sheet`). Chosen from five variants (today's simultaneous drop, a drop behind the map, slide then drop, a cascade, the door) because it is one motion with nothing to time.
  - **Expanded: like a wooden tray.** The sheet comes down from above (`sheet-out`, 460 ms: `translateY(-100%)` fast and decelerating, an overshoot of 1.6 %, back past the end by -0.5 %, settle) and runs back up with a pull-back of 1.2 % (`sheet-in`, 300 ms); the arrow turns with it (`sheet-arrow-out` 460 ms / `sheet-arrow-in` 300 ms). The keyframes were called `tray-out` / `tray-in` before; `tray-in` is also the keyframe name of the hand tray's entrance (`.handtray.enter`, further up in `parkposter.css`), and the later definition overrode it, so the hand tray entrance was broken: the drawer's keyframes are named `sheet-*` now. Keep the two names apart.
- **The bar under the zoo.** While a drawer is open, `playedDrawer` also appends `assocBar(map, p, seat)` (`assocbar.js`) to the box: a `.assocbar` that hangs from the box like the stub of a ticket. The box and the bar are one shape: while a drawer is open `.zoo.drawer-open` has square bottom corners, no shadow of its own and square bottom corners of the map row and of the sheet (`.zooRow`, `.zooRow > .sheetclip`, `!important`); the bar is absolute (`left: -4px; right: -4px; top: calc(100% - 4px)`, so nothing in the layout moves; it lies over the free strip under the boards, 58 px high with its edge, plus the 6 px hard shadow) and exactly as wide as the box, so its two outer edges continue the box's edges: the box's side borders run on into the bar's. The seam is a 3 px dashed ink line (the bar's top border), the bar has the ink edge (4 px), a radius of 20 px at the bottom, the box's hard shadow and the fill `--stone` (`#D9D6CE`, a light warm grey, a token of `body.viewer`: a step darker than the paper, without being another cream). A semicircle is punched out of each side at the seam (a ticket stub), a real hole: the zoo box carries a `mask-image` of two circles (radius `--hole: 11px`, centred on the side edges, `--seam: 7px` above the bottom edge of the box) that cuts the box and map, so the poster behind shows through. The absolutely positioned bar repeats those two circles in its own coordinate system, centred `8px - var(--seam)` below its top; this explicitly cuts its grey fill out of the lower half of each hole instead of relying on an ancestor mask to affect overflowing content. Both mask tiles are 8000 px larger than their boxes (`mask-size: calc(100% + 8000px) ...; mask-position: -4000px -4000px; mask-clip: no-clip`) so that shadows and other overflow remain visible, and `mask-composite: intersect` joins the two circles; the old prefixed properties are set as well. The ink rim is drawn by `.zoo.drawer-open::before` (a transparent layer over the whole box, `z-index: 6`, `pointer-events: none`, above the map, which runs right up to the side edges; 20 px longer than the box at the bottom so that the lower half of the rims fits; two ring gradients from `--bite` = 11 px to 4 px more): in the box it is a half ring. (The gold ring that used to mark the active zoo, round box and bar, is gone: the mask, the rim and the bar do not depend on whose turn it is.) The bar keeps its state in view because both drawer sizes cover the bonus panel and the association strip. One row with two groups and a dashed divider (`.abperf`): on the left the map's bonus spaces (the 3 purple income squares, 40 px, a small gap, then the 4 yellow pentagons, 44 px; sorted by `index`; a map without `bonus_slots` has no left group), on the right the 4 partner zoo slots (32 px) and the 3 university slots (31 px). A bonus space is taken when its bit of `p.flags.bonus_used` is set (they can be uncovered in any order, so they light up one by one): full colour; a space that is still covered is `.off` (opacity .34, saturation .35). The picture of a bonus space is `slotGlyph(root, sl, y)` of `association.js`, the function the bonus panel draws each of its spaces with (extracted from `bonusPanel`, so a bonus looks the same in both), here in a small svg with `viewBox="69 60 80 80"`. The partner zoo and university slots come from `p.tokens` (`partner_1..4`, `university_1..3`, filled from the first): the token picture cropped to its drawn part, or for an empty slot the silhouette of the strip (`HANDSHAKE` / `UNIVERSITY` of `handshake.js`, black at 20 %). Every item has a tooltip ("Bonus space 4 (taken): 3 reputation", "Partner zoo 2: empty", "University 1: science and reputation"). The number styles `.slot-number`, `.slot-label` and `.money-border` of replay.css also apply inside `.assocbar`. Animation: a freshly opened drawer puts `foot-in` on the box (`fresh`, the same 700 ms rule as the sheet) and the bar (the shape of the ticket, the box's mask and its rim are there at once; the bar's own mask belongs to the bar, so its two cut-outs travel with it and sit up to 14 px off the box's bites while it is still faded out, and are exactly on them at rest) slides 14 px down from the box while it fades in (`assocbar-in`, 320 ms after a 120 ms delay, so it follows the sheet); `.closing` makes it go up and fade out (`assocbar-out`, 200 ms); a size switch or a redraw does not replay it; reduced motion has none. The bar exists only in the drawer mode (not under the map, not in the tray) and, like the drawer, per board. The hand can cover the left end of the left board's bar, so while a drawer is open the page gets more room to scroll (`parkposter.css`, end of the file, `body.viewer:has(.zoo.drawer-open)`): the bottom padding becomes `169px * --hk + 56px` with an unfolded tray and `124px * --hk + 56px` with the fan (the part that shows at rest), and the page can be scrolled until the bar's lower edge lies above the top of the hand; a folded tray is low enough for the normal room, and with no open drawer nothing changes.
- **Keeping it fitted.** `fitPlayed()` runs after every render (`main.js`); a `ResizeObserver` on the map rows runs it again when a row changes (a map picture that loads late, a window change). `fitPlayed()` observes each row once (`watched`: a new observation fires at once and would call `fitPlayed` again, so there is no `disconnect()` and no repeated `observe()`) and lets go of rows that have left the page.

- **Tabbed layout: board switch.** The two arrows (`.zootab-arrow`, `zooStep` in `main.js`) are no longer in the name tab, where the right one moved with the length of the name. `renderZoo` (zoo.js) builds `.zootab-switch`, a small ink tab of its own, 38 px high like the name tab, `position: absolute; right: 22px; top: -38px` in the zoo box, so it sits at the right edge of the box whatever the name; it holds the two buttons (40 x 26 px pills, 14 px arrow icon). The buttons are outline buttons: transparent, a cream rim (`rgba(248,237,203,.65)`, 2 px) and a cream arrow; on hover the fill, rim and gold are the fully saturated `--gold` and the arrow is ink; `:active` moves them 1 px, `:focus-visible` has the 3 px gold outline. The old rules of the arrows (cream round buttons with a shadow, the smaller ones in `.zoo h2`) are gone from `parkposter.css`. Checked at 1920 x 1080: the switch is at x 756-862, y 514-552 for both players.

- **Top / bottom layout = the tabbed layout, both players.** `S.zooLayout === 'stack'` (the replay only) now draws every player like the tabbed layout does: the zoo box in the display's column and the played cards in their own plate-free box (`renderCards`) in the association element's column, one row per player in the order of the tracker, and no board switch (the switch and the arrows only exist when `tabbedLayout()`). `zooBoards` (main.js) returns `[renderZoo, renderCards]` for each seat and `#zoos` has `zoos-stack zoos-beside`; the grid rule is now `.zoos.zoos-beside` (two columns, `minmax(0, 1fr) var(--assocw)`), and `.zoos.zoos-stack.zoos-beside` has `row-gap: 66px` so the second player's name tab (38 px above its box) has room and there is some air between the rows. `besideLayout()` (zoo.js, tabs or stack) replaces `tabbedLayout()` in `playedMode()` and in `syncPlayedOptions` (settings.js): both layouts offer only "Cards next to the map". The page is taller than the window (about 1830 px at 1920 x 1080 with this game) and scrolls. `fitPlayed()` sizes the cards of each box to the zoo box before it, as in the tabbed layout.

- **Settings: only the options of the layout are listed.** `syncPlayedOptions` (settings.js) now rebuilds the "Played cards" select (`psel.replaceChildren()`) whenever the layout or the hand display changes: the tabbed and the top / bottom layouts list only "Cards next to the map", the side-by-side layout lists "Below the map", "Slide-out drawer" and "Tray in the hand dock" (no "Cards next to the map"). Nothing is greyed out in the first case; in the second only the tray is greyed (pale, `disabled`) while the hand display is not the floating tray. Checked by switching the layout in the settings: side [below, drawer, tray], tabs [beside], stack [beside].

- **Settings panel: wider, aligned, speed slider.** `#settingsModal .modalbox` is 620 px wide (was 440 px), and the controls form one column of equal width at the right (`--setcol: 300px` on `#settingsModal`): the selects are all 300 px wide with their text centred (`text-align-last: center`), the two sliders take the same column (`.setslide` grid `minmax(0, 1fr) var(--setcol)`) and the timeline switch sits flush right; the labels stay left. The autoplay speed is a slider now, like the size of the cards in the hand: `knobSlider(values, current, label, onPick)` (settings.js) builds the track with the knob that carries the value, and both rows use it. `SPEEDS = [0.5, 1, 2, 3, 4]` (1x default; a stored 1 / 2 / 4 still loads); the knob reads `0.5x`, `1x` ... and calls `setSpeed(v)` + `save()`. The three speed buttons (`.setspeed`, parkposter.css) and the toggling of `.speed button` in `setSpeed` (playback.js) are gone. Autoplay interval is still `1000 / S.speed` ms.

- **Settings panel: three tabs.** Under the title a gold pill with three tabs (`.settabs` / `.settab`, like the Control | Log tabs of the sidebar; `role=tablist`, arrow keys move between them), one `.setpanel` per tab: **Control** (Autoplay speed, Timeline in the control panel), **Board** (Player boards, Played cards, Hand display), **Cards** (Hand card size, Statue card, No-snake mode). Renamed: "Players' boards" -> "Player boards", "Cards in hand" -> "Hand card size". `setupSettings` (settings.js) still builds the rows as before and `showTab(i)` shows one panel; the tab last used (module variable `settingsTab`, not stored) stays open while the page is open, Control at the start. The panels have `min-height: 232px`, so the box keeps its height (384 px) on every tab; the first row of a panel has no dashed line above it.

## Endgame live count

- **What it is.** A violet "+X" pill under the Appeal tile and under the Conservation points tile of the player info box (`.rt.ov small.eg`, parkposter.css: pill like the income pill of the money tile, but violet `#7B4FB5` and centred under the tile, so it is not taken for income). It shows what the endgame effects would give *right now*: sponsors' appeal under the appeal tile, sponsors' conservation points (and, where the cards are shown, the endgame cards) under the conservation tile. Zero values show no pill. Works in the replay, the fork, the sandbox and live play (all of them draw `state_view`).
- **Setting.** Settings > Control > "Endgame live count" (switch, default on; `S.endgameLive` in state.js, saved as `egLive` in the `settings` localStorage JSON; the `eglive` event makes main.js draw the boards again).
- **Data.** `replay/view.py` `state_view` adds per player `endgame_live` = `{appeal, conservation}` (sponsors; `null` when the rules cannot read the position) and `endgame_cards` = conservation points of the endgame cards that would be kept. Both come from `engine/endgame.py` `live_scoring(state)`, which scores like `final_scoring` (Arcade first, Mascot Statue / Victory Column last) on a copy, changes nothing and returns zeros once the final scoring has begun (phase SCORING / OVER: the points are in the score). Endgame cards: all of them once the discard is done, before it all but the lowest one (`kept_endgame_cards`: every player with more than one card discards exactly one).
- **Who sees the card points.** `side-panel.js` adds `endgame_cards` only for a seat whose hand is shown (`!hides(seat)`: the open eye in the replay / fork, the own seat in live play). `live/projection.py` sets `endgame_cards = None` for every other seat, so a live viewer never receives the opponent's card points.
- **Rule fixes found on the way (engine 0.2.5-dev, not released).** Cable Car (S246) scores at 6 rock icons, not 7; Polar Bear Exhibit (S251) gives 1 conservation point at 3 bears and 2 at 6 (`HIDDEN_ICON_TIERS` now holds tiers); Expert on Europe (S211) counts 5 occupied 1-size enclosures with `effective_size` (next to the gate on maps 2 / 2a an enclosure counts as 3). Run `scripts/check_endgame_cards.py` and `scripts/problem_list.py` on the full log set before releasing.
- Tests: `tests/test_live_scoring.py` (live count equals `final_scoring`, state unchanged, zero at the end, best-all-but-one cards), `tests/test_live.py` (the opponent's `endgame_cards` is masked).

- **Ticket bar under the zoo (drawer open): evenly spread.** `assocbar.js` groups the icons into clusters (`.abcl`: the purple income bonus spaces, the yellow ones, the partner zoos, the universities; no more `.abgap` spacers). The two sides of the perforation (`.abgroup`) each take half of the bar (`flex: 1 1 auto`) and spread their two clusters with `justify-content: space-evenly`, so the icons are centred in their half with equal air between the clusters; bar padding 0 10 px (was 0 16 px). Partner zoo icons 36 px high (was 32), university icons 34 px (was 31); the bonus spaces are 38 / 42 px (were 40 / 44) so that the larger icons still leave room to breathe at the narrowest board width (766 px, side by side).

## Placement preview (right click on a sponsor building card, or on a map)

- **What it does.** With the setting on (Settings > Board > "Map placement preview", default on, `S.previewBuilding`, saved as `bpreview`), a right click on a sponsor building card in a hand (the 27 of `PREVIEW_CARDS` in cards.js: Mascot Statue / Victory Column, Arcade, Franchise Business, Landscape Gardener, the three Experts, Adventure / Water Playground, Underwater Tunnel, Side Entrance, Expansion Area, Zoo School, the animal houses, Amazon House, Aquarium, Cable Car, Excavation Site, Waza Large Animal Program) puts the building on that player's zoo, half see-through (`.pghost`, opacity .5), without placing anything. A sponsor building on the shared display (right click on its folder in `shared.js`) is previewed on both zoos: the building follows the cursor on whichever zoo it is over, each zoo with its own legal anchors (its buildings, Build action level, Diversity Researcher). The hand card gets a dashed orange outline (`.card.previewing`).
- **Enclosures.** A right click on a zoo map (either player's, whichever the cursor is on) opens a small picker (`.bpmenu`, `openMenu` in bpreview.js): 1-size ... 5-size, Aviary, Reptile house, Petting zoo and, in Marine Worlds only, Small / Large aquarium, each with its sprite. The special enclosures exist once per zoo: when the previewed zoo already has an Aviary, Reptile house, Petting zoo, Small or Large aquarium, its picker button is greyed out and disabled (`.bpmitem.taken`, title "... already built"; read from the shown state's `buildings`). A choice starts the same preview. Only the geometry is shown: every enclosure can be previewed, whatever the Build action's level (the upgraded level is assumed: flagged spaces count), the money or the enclosures already built; Diversity Researcher's overbuild is honoured.
- **Using it.** The building follows the cursor (the hex under it is the anchor); it keeps following the mouse also while it is turned (there is no pinning, no anchor mark and no turn buttons: a click does nothing). R / Shift + R and the mouse wheel over the map turn it around the hex under the cursor. The outline is green where it fits and red where it does not, and a large pill at the top of the map says how to turn and stop it ("Wheel / R: turn     Right click: stop"; no name and no fits / doesn't fit text, the outline says it). Diversity Researcher: when the previewed zoo has played S219 (`overbuild` of the request) water and rock spaces may be built on and the rock / water requirements are ignored, for the buildings and the enclosures alike. Terrain Build (Build action card side 4, `terrain_build` of the request, from `action_cards` variant 4): the enclosure previews may also cover exactly one rock / water space (the card's once-per-action rule; no cost shown); sponsor buildings never get it, they come from the Sponsors action. Nothing is placed, there is no check mark.
- **Ending it.** A right click on the same card, a right click on the previewed map, Esc, a step change (replay / fork / live: `S.preview.step`, checked at the start of `render()`) or switching the setting off. A right click on another building card shows that one instead; a right click on a map without a preview opens the picker (so switching the enclosure takes two right clicks). The picker closes with Esc, a click outside, the wheel or a choice. Only a hand whose cards are shown (`!hides(seat)`) can start a card preview; in the replay, the fork, the sandbox and live play alike. With a real building placement running in a fork, neither the preview nor the picker is used.
- **Code.** `js/bpreview.js` (state `S.preview` = {seat (hand seat / 'display' / null), seats, per: {seat: {type, shape, options}}, card | enclosure, name, step, hx, hy, hs, rot, error}; one request per zoo;  `begin`, `togglePreview`, `openMenu`, `turnPreview`, keys; started by the `bpreview-toggle` event that `cardRow` in cards.js sends from the card's `contextmenu`, and by the `bpreview-menu` event of the map's `contextmenu`), `previewLayer` block at the end of `zooBoard` (board.js: pick hexes, ghost, border, pill, buttons). Legal anchors and turns come from `POST /api/preview/placements` (`replay/preview.py`): {card | enclosure, map_id, marine_worlds, buildings, build_level, overbuild} in, {type, shape, options: [[x, y, rotation]]} out. It works from what the viewer shows (no engine state, so every mode can use it) and applies the rules of the card when it is played (`effects.build_rules` + `build_action.valid_placements`); the Experts (kiosk / 1-space enclosure / pavilion, placed later by their trigger) use the plain rules. When the request fails the pill says why (`S.preview.error`: "the server has no preview route yet (HTTP 404)" for a backend that predates the route, or the HTTP status) instead of the preview ending silently; a right click clears it. Tests: `tests/test_building_preview.py`. The R key is listed in the keyboard pop-up (shortcuts.js).

## Endgame scoring card count (replaces the "+n" tile of the second row)

- **What it is.** For the player whose cards are hidden (`hides(seat)`: the non-POV player), a small card (terracotta back, light inner border: the look of an endgame card) with the number of endgame cards that are scored printed on it, at the upper right of the Conservation points tile (`egCardIcon` in side-panel.js, `.egcard`, parkposter.css; variant D of the mocks, the hourglass would not stay readable at small window sizes). 1 normally, more with Resistance (the elephants). The count is known while the cards are not. The player whose cards are shown gets no icon: the cards are in the hand and their points in the violet "+X" pill. The card hangs 15 px outside the right edge of the tile (`.egcard { right: -15px }`), so it no longer covers the second digit of a two-digit conservation value; the live endgame chip (`small.eg`, lower right) stays clear of it. The row of the five tiles (`.pp .ppres`, `justify-content: space-evenly`) has `padding-right: 8px` so the tiles move left together and the card keeps a few px from the box border.
- **The count.** `p.endgame_hand.length`, minus 1 while the player has 2 or more cards and fewer than 10 conservation points (the discard at 10 points is still to come; same rule as `engine/endgame.py` `kept_endgame_cards`). No cards = no icon (the draft / setup).
- **Removed.** The `.ppres2` tile `.egx` ("+n" next to the hand) and its CSS. Hover title: "Endgame scoring cards: n are scored".

## Typography (final state; supersedes every earlier mention of Bowlby One, Barlow Condensed and Myriad Pro)

Where an earlier section of this file says "Bowlby One" or "Barlow Condensed" for a text, read the role below. Nothing is loaded from a font CDN; all faces are in `web/fonts/` and declared at the top of `web/css/parkposter.css`.

- **Families.** `Bowlby One` (400, `bowlby-one-latin-400-normal.woff2`): ONLY the round and turn numbers (`.pill.rnd .rn`, `.pill.rnd .tn`, 28 px) and the break counter (`.pill.brk .bn`, 34 px), exactly as before; later exception: the category names in the spine of the game stats (`.gssh span`). `Park Head` = Bricolage Grotesque 800 (`bricolage-grotesque-800.woff2`, a static instance of the variable font, width 100, optical size 24): player names, zoo tabs and their map tooltip title, Control | Log tabs, Fork and the "?" button, dialog titles, the Control | Board | Cards tabs of the settings, the POV chooser, the placement picker title, the initials of the preview roll. `Park Num` = Archivo 800 at 90 % width (`archivo-800-w90.woff2`): every counter and number (player box resources and icon counters, score flip digits, hand / deck counts, tray counts, X tokens, conservation / reputation / association numbers, the numbers in svg, the dock number, the rating in the account bar, the preview stats, the folder number, the draft tab). `Park Label` = Archivo at 78 % width, weights 500 / 600 / 700 (`archivo-w78-500|600|700.woff2`): everything that was `'Barlow Condensed'` (buttons, chips, the Move field, map labels, Elo pills, settings rows, the placement preview text, ...). `Barlow` 500 / 600 / 700 is unchanged (running text, the move log, the step text).
- **Metrics.** The three `Park` faces carry `size-adjust` (their capitals are as tall as Bowlby One's; Park Label: as Barlow Condensed's), `ascent-override`, `descent-override` and `line-gap-override: 0%` (the baseline sits where Bowlby's / Barlow Condensed's sat in a line box). So every font size, line height and vertical centring tuned for the old fonts still holds; do not retune them. The numbers in the `@font-face` rules are computed from the fonts' capital heights and ascent / descent; keep them when replacing a file.
- **How it is wired.** Each rule that used `'Bowlby One'` now names `'Park Head'` or `'Park Num'` by role; `'Barlow Condensed'` became `'Park Label'` everywhere (`parkposter.css`, `reptrack.js` where the unused `numFont: 'barlow'` option lives). JS with its own font strings uses `'Park Num'`: `association.js` (the II on the project card), `ctrack.js` (`numText`), `reptrack.js`, `icons.js` (`inkDxEm`, the canvas that centres HTML numbers by their ink). `side-panel.js` `centreInk` still measures Bowlby One (it only centres the round and turn numbers).
- **Removed (they existed only to make Bowlby One look lighter).** The 0.45 px / 0.4 px text strokes in the ground colour on names and on the first row of the player box (`.ppname .nm`, `.zootab-name`, `.ppres > :nth-child(1|3|4|5) b`, `.rt.xo b` keeps its black text-shadow outline but no stroke); the thin strokes of the svg numbers (`.num-top`, `.dk`, `.dk.appeal`, `.dk.conservation`, `.cons-number`, `.cons-number.rep-number` have `stroke: none`; the dark outline copy `.num-under` of `thin-numbers.js` stays, it is the outline); the 86 % squeeze of the player name (`.ppname .nm` has no `transform` and no `width: calc(100% / .86)`, `NAME_SQUEEZE` and the inline width are gone; `squeezeName` in `side-panel.js` puts the name in the `.nm` span and shrinks the font to fit (see the next point)).
- **No letter-spacing on numbers.** Park Num is narrow already, so several-digit numbers keep its own spacing: the appeal / conservation / reputation tiles of the player box (`tile()` in `side-panel.js`) and the two-digit money numbers of the bonus tiles and the association strip (`moneyDx` in `icons.js`) have no negative letter-spacing (the `.two` rule is gone, the old per-number `MONEY_INK` table is replaced by `inkDxEm`, which measures the real font). They are only shifted so the ink is centred. `inkDxEm(text, ls = 0)`.
- **Two number positions tuned for Park Num.** The 1 on the appeal ticket at the end of the reputation track is Park Num 30 px, baseline `ty + 36.8` (`reptrack.js`); the conservation number on a bonus pentagon (`bonusTile` in `shared.js`, the shield under the reputation / conservation tracks) has its baseline at y 55.5 instead of 59 (the other icons keep 59): the shield narrows towards its tip, so with 59 the foot of a 1 hung over the narrow part and the number looked low.
- **Conservation track cubes.** The player cube (`.trackcube`, `conservationTrack` in `shared.js`) sits 18 px higher than the centre of the shield (`top: calc(57 / 188 * 100% - 18px)`), so it no longer covers the numbers 0, 2, 5 and 10 (it overhangs the top of the track, which is not clipped). Chosen from 6 / 10 / 14 / 18 px.
- **Take-a-card bonus tile.** `bonusTile('take-in-range-or-deck', n)` in `shared.js` (n > 1): the card is 38 px high (was 48) at x 52 / y 42 and the number is 32 px (was 38) at x 24 / baseline 53, so both lie inside the pentagon (before, the number hung over its left edge and the card reached the bottom corner). A fan of n cards was tried and rejected as too small on the board.
- **Player names in the info box shrink to fit.** `squeezeName` in `side-panel.js`: the name (20 px) is measured with a canvas against the room left in the `.pphead` (header width minus its padding, the score pill, the other children of `.who` and the gaps; a `ResizeObserver` on the header re-runs it). A name that does not fit is set smaller, down to 16 px (`NAME_MIN`, inline `font-size` on `.ppname`, 2 px spare); at 16 px it is still cut with an ellipsis (`.nm` has `text-overflow`). The name tab above the zoo is not shrunk: it grows with the name.
- **Control buttons.** The gear and the "?" (`.settingsbtn`) are sand (`--sand: #E7D39A`, new token in `parkposter.css`) instead of the panel cream, so they stand out from the panel; hover is still gold. The Fork label is Park Head 19 px (was 15) in its 92 x 40 px button.
- **Removed files.** `web/fonts/barlow-condensed-latin-500|600|700-normal.woff2` and (earlier) `web/fonts/MyriadPro-Bold.woff` with its `@font-face` in `table.css`; the three rules of `table.css` / `replay.css` that named Myriad now name `system-ui` (Park Poster overrides them anyway). `web/fonts/` now holds: Bowlby One, Bricolage Grotesque 800, Archivo 800 w90, Archivo w78 500 / 600 / 700, Barlow 500 / 600 / 700.
- **Verification.** Screenshots of the replay (steps 108 and 420, the settings dialog, the Log tab) at 1920 px are identical to the approved mock that swapped the fonts in on top of the old CSS (the mock: "Bricolage names, Archivo counters"); no request for a removed file. Names sit centred in the coloured part of the player header (ink centre 24.25 px of 24.5 px).

## Results of a finished replay (BGA's "Game results" tab)

Replays only (`replay.html`; not forks, the sandbox, live games or the mini games). New module `web/js/results.js`; styles at the end of `parkposter.css` (section "results of a finished replay").

- **When.** The results exist for a replay whose last state has `phase === 'over'` (its `result` gives scores and winner). A conceded game has no final state result: once the log has been read (see Statistics) `S.postStats.conceded` names the seat that conceded, the winner is the other seat, the scores are those of the last position, and the hero says "X conceded Â· the scores are those of the position when the game was given up". Not tested on a real conceded log yet (the sample logs all ended normally).
- **The switch.** At the last frame of the last step (`atLast()`), when results exist, the move pill gets the class `rsgate` (padding 0, no wrap, overflow hidden): its normal children are hidden and `.rsswitchrow` fills it: a flush flap at each end and the text "Game over Â· <winner> wins <a> : <b>" (Barlow 600 19 px) centred between them. The flaps (`.rsflap`, 180 px each, the same width, Park Head 18 px, a 18 px chevron): left `.fb` "â€¹ Final board", right `.fs` "Game stats â€º". One colour: the view that is shown is forest green `#2F7D50` with cream text (default cursor), the other one pale mint `#CFEEDB` with ink text (`#A9E0BE` on hover); a 3 px ink line separates each flap from the text. Tooltips carry the arrow keys. The final step's text stays in the log. (Earlier a segmented chip inside the pill, then a mint / gold pair; replaced. Gold is not used: it is the Control / Log tab colour.)
- **Navigation.** `S.results` (state.js) is true while the results are on show. `stepBy(1)` on the last frame with results available opens them (`openResults`), `stepBy(-1)` while they are open closes them (`closeResults`) without changing the step; every `go()` (first, last, the jump box, the move list, Home / End) sets `S.results = false`, so â­ / End always shows the final board. Buttons: â€º is enabled on the last step while results exist and the results are closed; â­ is enabled while the results are open; â€¹ / â® stay enabled while they are open; Fork is disabled on the results. The keyboard help (`shortcuts.js`) says so for â†, â†’ and End. Autoplay stops at the last step as before (it never opens the results).
- **The view (Game stats).** `body.showresults` hides `#shared`, `#zoos` and `#dock`; `section#results` (after `#zoos`) holds `.gs`, a grid `300px | 1fr` x `auto | 1fr` on the dark page (no cream, no cards). The seat colours come in as `--c0` / `--c1` (`seatColor`), light tints `--gl` / `--gr` by `color-mix`; the spine is `--sp` = 260 px. `section#results` has `padding: 5px 37px 0 0` (a margin would collapse through the section; the frame reaches 1 px outside the table) so the frame's top edge lines up with the top of the Control / Log tabs (y 90) and its right edge with the right edge of the Round / Turn chip (x 1526). The page does not scroll while the table is shown: `syncResults` also toggles `html.showresults`, which sets `overflow-y: scroll` on `html` (the window's scrollbar stays, with nothing to scroll, so the width does not jump) and `overflow: hidden` on `body`, and drops the body's bottom padding (the room kept for the dock, which is hidden); `.gs` is `calc(var(--vh, 1vh) * 100 - 104px)` high (`--vh` = a hundredth of the window height in layout px, set by `fitScale` on every resize, so the table grows in full screen; the frame ends 12 px above the window bottom like the sidebar), and only `.gsbody` scrolls. The Final board keeps its normal page scroll. The table has 8 px corners (header, stats and frame 9 px). The stats fill (`.gsbody`) is near opaque: each seat colour at 34% on the outer edge fading to 14% towards the spine, over `#0a2630`. The frame is `.gs::before` in grid column 2 (header + stats only, not the rail), 1 px outside the table: a 1 px gradient from the left seat colour through cream (35%) to the right seat colour. Map and Marine Worlds are not shown.
- **Top left: the view icons** `.gstg`: two flush square buttons (66 x 52, ink border 3 px, radius 14 px, 4 px ink shadow), Comparison (three lines) and Graph (a line chart); the active one teal2 with cream icon, the other `#0a3540`. `S.gsGraph` keeps the choice while the page is open.
- **Top right: the names and the score** `.gshd`, three columns `1fr | spine | 1fr`, 8 px radius at the top, min 136 px high: seat 0's half (gradient from its colour darkened to its colour; `.gswho` = the name `.gsnm` in Park Head 34 px (40 px cut long names; 34 px fits e.g. "Propaganda Panda" in the half), right-aligned towards the spine, mirrored for seat 1; no Winner / Loser caption) with the Elo chip `.gselo` hanging below the name (absolute, `top: calc(100% + 16px)`, on the same side as the name's alignment, so it never takes width from the name and the name is centred in the header like the score: `.gswho` is nudged `top: -5px` and `.gssc` `top: -6px`: both sit a little above the middle for room above the chip, and the name's cap height and the score's digits share a centre). The inner edge of the name and of the chip lines up with the inner edge of the stat values: `.gshh.l` has `padding-right: 54px`, `.gshh.r` `padding-left: 54px` (= the 30 px of `.gsv` + its invisible pointer slot of 10 px + 14 px gap). The chip is a see-through dark pill (`rgba(0,0,0,.28)`, 1 px cream hairline at .18); on a (nearly) black seat (`results.js` adds `.dk` when the brightest channel of `seatColor` is < 80) it is the solid grey `#24343a` with the hairline at .22 instead, because the see-through layer vanishes on black; Park Num 20 px: the new rating and its change `.gsd`, green `#7EE0A0` with an up triangle / coral `#FF8C7A` with a down triangle). The chip values are a placeholder (`eloAfter` in `panel`: the winner 130 +7, the other 116 -7) until the real new ratings are supplied. The ink spine holds only the score, `105 - 138` (`.gssc`, Park Num 46 px, gap 12 px, the dash 34 px at .5 opacity; two 3-digit scores leave 28 px on each side in the 260 px spine; the lower score at .55 opacity, none dimmed in a tie).
- **Left: the category rail** `.gsrail`: the category names in Barlow 700 19 px spaced capitals (`#9db8bd`, 46 px rows, 6 px gap, `ul` padding 0 20 0 46) on a faint vertical line (x 25) that glows orange in the middle; a glowing orange dot (`.gsdot`, 14 px, x 19, two shadows) rides to the active category (transition .4 s); the active name is white 22 px with an orange glow (no fill). The view switch `.gstg` above (grid column 1, row 1, centred on the header row, 24 px side margins) is two stacked 48 px buttons (icon, `Table` / `Graph`, a radio mark `.chk` at the right): the active one is filled teal (`#1a8aa5` to `#137088`, white text, ink edge below, filled orange radio centre), the other dark with an ink inset edge and grey text. The active category is the last section whose top is above scroll + 120 px. A click scrolls the stats to that category (smooth); in the graph view it switches back to the comparison. Under the last row the stats keep 20 px (`padding-bottom` of `.gsbody`, the same distance as between a category title and its first row), no more. There is no spacer under the last category (the stats end with the last row): at the very bottom the category last clicked in the rail counts as active (`pick`, cleared by the reader's own wheel / touch / key / pointer input), else the last one.
- **Right: the stats** `.gsbody` (scrolls, thin scrollbar; the scroll position is kept in `S.gsScroll` across renders and reset when the stats close): the background is the duel itself, seat 0's colour fading towards the ink spine, seat 1's colour mirrored. Categories, in order: Overview (First player, Triggered end, Breaks triggered, Break advancements, Thinking time), Actions (Animals, Association, Build, Cards, Sponsors, X-back = an X token taken instead of an action, Clever), Association (Projects, Releases = animals released, Donations, Universities, Partner zoos, Reputation, Workers), Cards (Drawn from deck, Drawn from range, Snapped, Manual snaps, Projects snaps, Discarded, Animals played, Sponsors played), Money (Gained overall, Gained from income, Spent on animals / buildings / donations / display cost), X-tokens (Gained, Used), Zoo (Enclosures, Free 2-sizes, Pavilions, Kiosks, Unique buildings, Covered hexes, Empty hexes). Each starts with `.gssh`: the name in the spine (Bowlby One 22 px, capitals, spacing .06em, orange `--sun`; an exception to the Typography rule that keeps Bowlby One for the round / turn / break numbers) between two glowing orange lines that fade at their ends. Each stat is `.gsrow` (min 56 px, a 1 px hairline `rgba(248,237,203,.06)` on top, a faint highlight on hover): seat 0's value right-aligned, the label in the spine (Barlow 600 18 px), seat 1's value; values Park Num 30 px white (rows stay min 56 px, labels 18 px), the smaller one at opacity .38, the larger one with an orange pointer (`.gsch`) towards the spine; `gsRow` takes an optional 4th element (0 / 1 / -1) naming the seat that gets the pointer instead (the Yes of a Yes / No row, the lower thinking time) while the other value is dimmed. Text values (thinking time, First player / Triggered end as Yes / No: `.gsrow.tx`) use the same 30 px and reserve the same invisible pointer slot as the numbers, so every value sits on one vertical line.
- **Graph view** `.gsgraph`: the score race (`raceSvg`, 1180 x 760, the big variant) on a dark field, grid and axis text in faint cream; the real graph view is still to be designed. The old enlarged race overlay is gone.
- **Statistics.** Counted in the browser from the raw log, once, when the last step is first shown (`loadStats`: `GET /api/tables/{id}/log`, or the LogStore text for `source=upload`), only the `/table/...` channel: turns = `actionCardCleanup` "(finishing action)", a turn without `chooseActionCard` = X token instead of an action, actions = `chooseActionCard` type, first player = the first `chooseActionCard`, X tokens and money = the `xtoken` / `money` amounts of `getBonuses` (income = money gained between `startBreak` and `finishBreak`), breaks triggered = `getBonuses` source "triggering break", break advancements = the sum of `n` of the player's `advanceBreak` events (every step of the break track the player caused: a Sponsors card for 5 = 5, Cards = 2, a jumping animal = its jump, which is an `advanceBreak` followed by a `getBonuses` with source "Jumping action"; steps beyond the last space still count in full), money on animals / buildings / donations = `buyAnimal.amount` / `buyBuilding.amount_money` / `donation` money, cards from the range = the folder number of a card played with `fromDisplay` (its index + 1 in the display of the last step before that move id), drawn = `drawCards` "from the deck", manual snaps / projects snaps = the `snapCard` "snaps ... from the display" events told apart by their source (animals, Waza, bonus tokens and Sponsors4 snaps are in neither): a project snap = the seat's snap selector (`gameStateChange` id 59, `active_player`) has `source` "map bonus space" (the conservation bonus slot of the map, instant or at break income), or else a break-income `takeBonus` with `bonus_type` "Snapping" / source "map bonus space" granted one that is not used yet; a manual snap = the snap state of the Cards action (`gameStateChange` id 34, `descSuffix` "snap", `nSnap` > 0) is open (cleared when the action ends, `actionCardCleanup` "finishing action"). A selector is forgotten at the next `chooseActionCard` / end of that action, so an unused offer does not leak into a later snap. Checked on the nine sample logs: 49 project and 27 manual of 80 snaps, the rest 3 Veiled Chameleon and 1 source-less, taken from the range / snapped = `snapCard` "in reputation range" / "snaps", discarded = `discardCards` "card(s)" and "during break", association tasks and workers = the `slideMeeples` texts (workers start at 1) + `donation`, buildings = the `buyBuilding` types (kiosk, pavilion, the sponsors' unique buildings of `data/unique_shapes.json`, everything else an enclosure), covered / empty hexes = the cells of the final buildings and the plain hexes of the map that are not covered (as on BGA: covered + empty = the plain hexes). Ended the game = `endOfGame`. Counting rules still to be checked against BGA's own stats table (whether "money gained" includes the 25 at the start, which draws and discards BGA counts, what BGA calls a unique building).
- **Free 2-sizes** (Zoo, below Enclosures): the free 2-hex enclosures from the conservation bonus (map bonus space). `countStats` keeps the `source` of each seat's build selector (`gameStateChange` id 30, `args.source`); a `buyBuilding` of type `size-2` whose log says "for free" counts when that source is 'map bonus space' (then the selector is cleared). Such a building also counts in Enclosures. Checked on the 9 sample logs: 44, equal to all free size-2 builds there.
- **Seat order = zoo order.** The left column of the table (header, score, values, colours `--c0` / `--c1`) is the seat whose map and info box are on the left, `seatOrder()[0]` (`pov.js`, `S.orient`): `panel` takes `[L, R]` from it, `gsRow` swaps its two values (and the `pick` seat) when the seat order is [1, 0]. Scores, winner highlighting, Elo chips and the thin / dark seat colours follow the seat, not the position. `syncResults` runs on every render, so choosing the other player's eye turns the table too.
- **Rows fade out under the header.** `.gsbody` and `.gsgraph` have `mask-image: linear-gradient(180deg, transparent, #000 var(--gsfade))` (`--gsfade: 14px` on `.gs`, a thin fade): rows and the body's own background fade together, so the page behind (teal) shows through the faded top. The score area (`.gsspine`) keeps the colour of the centre column (`var(--ink)`). The first category title has `padding-top: 24px` (was 16) so it sits below the fade at scroll 0. The frame `.gs::before` is a 1 px ring (mask with `exclude`), not a filled gradient, so nothing but the page shows through the fade.
- **Table bottom = last info box bottom.** `fitResults()` (`results.js`, after every `syncResults` render, again after a frame and 400 ms, and on window resize / full screen) measures the bottom of the last player info box (`aside.sidebar .pp`) and sets `--gsh` on `.gs` (`height: min(100 vh - 104 px, var(--gsh))`, minus 1 px for the frame): where the boxes are shorter than the window (a tall window or full screen) the table ends with the last box, where they are cut off by the window it ends with the sidebar as before.
- **Graph fits the height.** `fitResults()` also redraws the race graph at the pixel size of the graph area (`.gsgraph` content box, layout px): `raceSvg(race(), w, h, true)` gets the real width and height as its viewBox, `.gsgraph .rsrace-svg.big` is `100% x 100%`, so the plot stretches or compresses with the window and the text, lines and the break numbers keep their size (no scaling of the whole picture). It runs after the table height is set and on every resize / full screen; below 300 x 240 px it keeps the last drawing.
- **Clever** (Actions, after X-back): the number of resolved Clevers per player = `actionCardCleanup` events whose log contains "(Clever effect)" (the action card the Clever moved). Whatever the source (sponsor, placement / map bonus, animal, project), only an actual cleanup counts; an offered or passed Clever creates none. These cleanups are not turns (the turn counter only uses "finishing action"). Checked on the 9 sample logs against the raw cleanup events.
- **Thinking time** (`thinkingTime(sec)` in results.js): the seconds come from BigQuery `all_games_stat.thinking_time` per player (`TableRecord.thinking_times`, `view.players[i].thinking_time`). Under an hour `12:34` (mm:ss, no unit), under a day `4h 15 min`, from a day `5d 12h`. A local copy has no value and shows the placeholder `12:34` for both players. The lower time gets the orange pointer.
- **BACKEND NICE-TO-HAVE.** The raw log is about 12 MB; a server endpoint that returns the counted statistics (or BGA's own table stats) would spare the download. The frontend only needs `S.postStats` in the shape `{players: [...], first, ender, conceded}` of `countStats`.

## Bonus pentagons in HD (bonusart.js, web/icons_hd)

The bonus pictures of the pentagons that came from BGA's raster icon sheet (soft at every size) are BGA's own pictures upscaled 4x, not redrawn: `scripts/build_bonus_hd.py` runs Real-ESRGAN (model realesrgan-x4plus-anime, on the CPU with the `ncnn` Python package; the script header says where the model comes from) over the icons of `web/icons`, writes `web/icons_hd/<name>.webp` and `web/js/bonus_hd_data.js` (`BONUS_HD`: where each picture sits, x / y / width / height in the units of the composed pentagon of `bonusTile`: 80 high centred on (40, 40), viewBox -4 -4 88 94). The outputs are committed; the raw upscales are cached in `build/bonus_hd_raw`.

- **Which.** The random bonuses of 5 / 8 conservation and 16 reputation: Partner-Zoo, Fac (university), size-3, and BGA's ready-made tiles bonus-ignore-conditions, -increased-hand, -icon, -scoring-cards, -sponsor-gray, -extra-shift, -kiosk-pavilion; the pentagons of the reputation track and of 2 conservation: upgrade-card, Worker, conservation (the shield; the number is still drawn by `bonusTile` at y 55.5). Marketing (`bonus-sponsor`) has no pentagon on BGA and stays a plain `img.ctbonus`, now with the HD picture (`marketingTile`).
- **Unchanged:** 10 money, 5 money, 2 reputation, 3 cards, 3 X tokens, the 1-card and 1-X-token pentagons. **Multiplier** is a vector drawing (blue disc with a radial gradient, ring #141D5C, white "x2" in Park Num 29 px).
- **BGA's ready-made tiles** lose their own ground and grey ring: the build keys them out (the ring = the pixels near the edge whose colour is the ring's grey, a mix of it and a ground, or the darker rim the upscaler draws inside it, connected to the outside; the ground = a flood fill from just inside the ring for each ground colour: yellow, beige, the light blue lower half of the hand tile; edge pixels un-blended from the ground; small leftovers along the ring and specks under 350 px are dropped; the few transparent pixels inside two of BGA's tiles are filled before upscaling), so the picture lies on the same ink-edged vector pentagon as every other bonus. Yellow tiles use the yellow of the bonus pentagons; the Marine Worlds tiles (ignore conditions, wildcard icon, sponsor gray, extra shift) a beige ground (`BONUS_HD.beige`, measured from BGA's tiles). BGA's green Marine Worlds token on those tiles is left out (it says nothing in online play). Parts of a picture that ran into BGA's ring (the red -1 column, the bottom of the wheel, the arrow of the extra shift) are kept whole and may leave the fitting pentagon; the tile's picture is clipped to the inner edge of the ink line (`clip` in the data). The hand tile's lower part is a light blue rect (`split`, `lower`) clipped to the pentagon, with the ink line drawn again on top.
- **Centring and size.** Every picture's box is centred on (40, 43) of the pentagon (where the money / X token / reputation pictures sit). It keeps BGA's size (a composed picture is 48 units high, a ready-made tile's picture keeps its size relative to the tile) unless it would leave the pentagon (the inner edge of the ink line less a margin: a pentagon 71 high); then it is made smaller until it fits (the university and the handshake are).
- **How `bonusTile` picks:** `bonus-sponsor` -> `marketingTile()`; `hdBonus(name)` not null -> the pentagon (fill from `hdBonus`, beige for the Marine Worlds tiles) + `drawHdBonus(t, name, edge)`; everything else as before. The two fixed tiles of 2 conservation in `ctrack.js` call `drawHdBonus` into a group scaled by HEX / 80.
- **Not changed:** the bonus pictures of the association strip / map bonus slots (`association.js`, `assocbar.js`) still use the icon sheet.

## Build II hexes on the zoo maps (as on BGA)

The hexes that need the upgraded Build action (`special_hexes` of kind `upgrade_flag`) are drawn like BGA draws them (`zooBoard` in `web/js/board.js`; the map editor's `drawFlag` in `web/js/map_editor.js` draws the same): a red parallelogram (`.flag-wedge`, #dd5749, opaque) over the upper left of the hex, from the left corner to the top edge, down to the centre line (corners at -0.95 R / 0, -0.475 R / -0.95 h, 0.36 R / -0.95 h, -0.115 R / 0, R = MAP.dx / 1.5, h = 0.866 R; measured from a BGA screenshot), and over its right end BGA's own Build II badge `web/badges/BuildII.webp` (from BGA's badge sheet, 296 px, so no upscaling is needed) as an SVG `image` clipped to a circle (`.flag-badge-img`, `clip-path: circle(48.5%)`) with a thin white ring (`.flag-ring`, stroke 2.5), radius 0.42 R, centred 0.02 R right of and 0.42 R above the hex centre (its bottom touches the centre line). The old purple circle with the shovel icon r3c9 and the "II" text (`.flag-badge`, `.flag-label`) is gone, with its CSS (`web/css/replay.css`, `web/css/map_editor.css`).

**Hex with a placement bonus too** (map 3 / 3a: three hexes with a 2 money under the Build II zone; BGA lays the badge over the number). `zooBoard` draws every wedge FIRST (before the bonus loop), so the bonus pentagon lies on top of it, and the badges AFTER the bonuses: on a hex without a bonus the big badge as above, on a hex with one (`hasBonus`) a small badge (radius 17, centred 27 left of and 29 above the hex centre, so it overlaps the pentagon's upper-left shoulder like a notification dot). The pentagon keeps its full size and position. The map editor does the same: `drawFlag(g, wedgeOnly)` draws the wedge into the flag layer, `flagBadge(g, bx, by, br)` the small badge after the bonus in the bonus layer (before, the editor shrank the bonus to 62% towards the lower right corner). Chosen from five options (badge at the wedge's tip, red rim, shrunk pentagon, corner badge).

## Marketing picture: HD and optically centred (bonusart.js `marketingAttrs`)

The Marketing bonus (an X token over a sponsor card: purple square 4 of the worker panel on maps 1 / 1a, the yellow placement bonus on 2, 3, 4a, 5, 7, 9, T1) was drawn from the 79 x 116 icon-sheet sprite r7c6: soft, and its small X token upper left against the big card lower right made the bbox-centred picture look off-centre. Both places now draw `web/icons_hd/bonus-sponsor.webp` (428 x 463, the HD picture of the tracks) through `marketingAttrs(cx, cy, h)` in `bonusart.js`: width = h x 428 / 463, centred on the optical centre of its opaque pixels (0.068 w right of and 0.018 h above the middle of the box, so it is moved that much left / down), not the box. Heights: 46 in the purple square (`slotGlyph`, association.js; 40 in a pentagon space), 42 in the placement pentagon (board.js, drawn at cy + 3 like the other icons).

## Upgrade and Extra Shift = the bonus of the track (ctrack.js `hdPentagonSvg`)

`hdPentagonSvg(name, size, cx, cy)` (`ctrack.js`) returns a nested `<svg>` (viewBox -4 -4 88 94, the 80 high pentagon of the 2 conservation track: `pentagonShape` with edge 2.2 / shadow 2, beige ground for `bonus-extra-shift`, plus `drawHdBonus`) and, when cx / cy are given, puts it on that centre. It replaces three older drawings: (1) the action card upgrade of the association strip (`putUpgrade` -> `hdHex` in association.js, name `upgrade-card`; the sprite on `bonusHex` was a little off-centre), (2) the Extra Shift of map 11's second set in the strip (`hdHex`, `bonus-extra-shift`, 92 high), (3) the Extra Shift hex of map 11's board (board.js, was the icon-sheet tile r5c15, 80 high). The sprite tile had a small shield with a flip arrow at its upper left (redundant; the HD picture has none), so it is gone everywhere. (4) the four bonus tokens of the info box (`bonusToken` in side-panel.js: bonus-icon, bonus-sponsor-gray, bonus-ignore-conditions, bonus-extra-shift; 36 high) all use the same svg (class `btoken-hd`), no sprites any more. `ICON_IDS['bonus:upgrade-card']` is no longer read by association.js.

**Upgrade picture nudge** (`nudged` in `bonusart.js`, applied by `drawHdBonus` to `upgrade-card` only): the card (heavy on the left; the arrow is a thin line on its right) sat a little high and left so its upper-left corner grazed the pentagon's edge. The picture is 6% smaller and moved 1.8 right / 1 down (units of the 80 high pentagon). It applies wherever the upgrade pentagon is drawn: the 2 conservation track, the association strip and the sandbox bonus pickers.

## Full screen switch (settings) and F11 in the shortcuts (settings.js, shortcuts.js)
- Settings, tab Control: a first row "Full screen" (`.setrow` + `.setswitch`, above "Autoplay speed"). It calls `documentElement.requestFullscreen()` / `document.exitFullscreen()` (rejections caught) and follows the real state: `inFull()` = `document.fullscreenElement` or a window as big as the screen (F11 browser full screen sets no `fullscreenElement`); it is re-read on `fullscreenchange`, `resize` and whenever the pop-up opens. Not stored. There is no button in the control panel or the top bar (tried, rejected).
- Keyboard shortcuts pop-up: the F11 key is coloured (`D.F11`, the F row passes the id `'F11'`); the browser itself does the switching, the key only explains itself.

## Start page (park map) (web/index.html, js/landing.js, css/landing.css)

The start page is an illustrated park map instead of the old grid of mode boxes. Everything is drawn by `js/landing.js` as one SVG (no images apart from `/brand/logo.svg`); `css/landing.css` has the styles and three own font faces, `Land Head` (`/fonts/bricolage-grotesque-800.woff2`), `Land Text` (the `archivo-w78-500/600/700.woff2` files) and `Land Sign` (`/fonts/fredoka-latin-600-normal.woff2`, the option signs), with no size-adjust. The page is laid out at 1920 x 1080 inside `#stage` and zoomed to the window (`fit()`: scale = min(width / 1920, height / 1080), centred). The SVG draws its ground far beyond the stage (overflow visible), so other window shapes show more land instead of bars. `body.parkmap` overrides the narrow body of `style.css`; `style.css` is still loaded for the account dialog.

- **The five options** (`OPTS` in landing.js: colour, icon, pin / label / ticket positions in stage pixels, link). The aviary's three round mesh domes are joined by mesh tunnels: **Replay** is the left dome (teal #1D8AA1, icon: a circular arrow around a play triangle), **Custom game** the big top dome (orange #E9662A, icon: two meeples), **Sandbox** the right dome (gold #E0A02A, icon: bucket and spade). **Mini games** is the kiosk at the lower left (purple #8058C4, icon: a puzzle piece with a star), **Map editor** the construction site at the lower right (green #3E9A55, icon: three map hexes and a pencil; a blueprint lies on the grass to the right of the site, clear of the stakes). Each option has a map pin with its icon (`pin()`, size `s`: 1.4 for Custom game, 1.28 for the other four) and a wooden name sign (`label()`): a national park sign standing on two posts, dark brown board #5E3D26 with faint grain, a golden line inside the edge #E9C46A and golden letters #F4D27A in `Land Sign` (Fredoka SemiBold 600, 25 px, `/fonts/fredoka-latin-600-normal.woff2`); `signShape(w)` draws board, grain, line and posts to the text's width + 66 px, called by `fitLabels()` after the fonts load; the icons are `ICON` in landing.js, drawn in a -22..22 box and centred in it (the map editor icon by its outline box; the puzzle piece halfway between its outline box and its square, so its knobs do not pull it off centre), and `badge()` draws them in a round disc for the tickets and the portrait list.
- **Tickets.** Hovering a dome, pin or label (anything with `data-opt`) marks the option (`.on`: the pin grows, a ring in its colour (`.halo`) around the dome / kiosk / site, always following the ground's outline with a small gap: a circle round each dome, an ellipse round the kiosk's paved plaza (the kiosk stands on it, drawn over the ring), the construction site's own irregular blob scaled up about its centre (1.08 x 1.127, non-scaling stroke), the sign's board takes its colour, its line and letters turn cream, the letters with an ink outline so they stay legible on the light colours) and opens its ticket (`#ticket`, positioned at the option's `card`): badge, name, one-line description, a dashed tear line and the action. A click or keyboard focus keeps the ticket open (`pinned`); a click elsewhere or Escape closes it. Actions: Replay has the table id field and Open, which does what the old form did (GET `/api/lookup?q=`, then `next`; "needs_log" waits 1.2 s and goes to the extension guide; errors are shown under the field). Typing the live-game word there unlocks the live game for this browser exactly as before (localStorage `liveUnlocked`, word unchanged). Custom game links to `/play.html` once unlocked, otherwise its button is disabled ("Coming soon"). Sandbox links to `/sandbox.html`, Mini games to `/minigames/`, Map editor to `/map_editor.html`.
- **Other pieces:** the name sign at the top left (`.brandchip`): logo, "Ark Nova Park" and under it "Maintained by the United Emu Friends Association" (17 px, pale green #D6EADD), both centred on each other, on a hanging bottle-green enamel sign (green gradient, 14 px corners, ink outline, a cream double rule inside the edge, ink drop shadow) hung from two ropes (`::before` / `::after`, striped rope colours, running up past the window top) with a rivet where each rope is fixed; the portrait list uses the same text; the account widget of `account.js` is moved into `.account` at the top right (a MutationObserver picks up `#acctbar` when account.js appends it). There, logged out, it is a single admission ticket in Uluru red #D2553A without text: 108 x 58, ink outline, notches top and bottom at a dashed tear line near the right end, a cream person sign in the main part and a small cream star on the stub, drawn as an SVG background of the button (the button's text is hidden with font-size 0 and copied into its `title` and `aria-label` by landing.js); ink drop shadow, lifts on hover, presses down on click. Logged in, the name is a red ticket tag (cream letters, dashed inner line), the rating is hidden and Log out is an underlined ink link beside it; the Terms and privacy link at the bottom right (no Ratings link), not a chip but "mowed into the lawn": Land Text 700 17 px, the letters filled with fine horizontal mower stripes of two greens darker than the lawn (#5A8A37 / #4A782C, 2 px each, background-clip text) with a light cut edge under them (drop-shadow 0 1px rgba(205,232,160,.55)), darker on hover; the compass rose (N, E, S, W round it, letters with a cream outline) with the scale bar under it (four segments of 29 px, labels 0 m at its left end, 50 m centred, 100 m at its right end; a click on the rose spins it two to four turns either way, overshoots, wobbles back and settles on north in 2.2 s (Web Animations on `.rose`; nothing else happens, an easter egg; off with reduced motion), drawn by `compass()` into `#compass` at the bottom left (28 px x k from the left, 14 px x k from the bottom). These four are not in `#stage` but in `#wrap`, so they keep to the window's corners whatever its shape: `fit()` sets `--k` (the zoom of the stage) on `#wrap`; each is scaled by k from its corner and its distance to the window edges is multiplied by k (name sign 34 px from the side and 50 px from the top, leaving room for the ropes; account widget 34 px from the side and 30 px from the top, Terms link 34 px from the side and 16 px from the bottom, compass as above). The page title stays "Ark Nova Replay".
- **Scenery and life** (all from scratch): outback with dunes and spinifex, Uluru (with a lookout) and Kata Tjuta at the top, a hedge around the park lawn, a billabong with a creek, pier, pelican and ducks, gum trees (one with a koala), wattle bushes, flower beds, termite mounds (four shapes: spire, twin peak, lumpy cathedral, low dome; `termites(x, y, s, kind)`), rocks in the outback of four kinds (angular with facets, flat slab, a cluster of three pebbles, round boulder; `rock(x, y, s, seed, colour, kind)`, round by default as in the domes and the construction site), a picnic blanket, the entrance gate (an Australian flag, simplified to 28 x 14 px, on each post; "UEFA" carved into its wooden board: `carved()` draws the four letters as chisel grooves, light lower lip + dark groove + darker core, no font, tilted along the board's arch; a booth by the gate with a window and no sign, since nothing can be bought). There are no place names on the map. Animated: an emu with the logo's gold crown and two striped chicks walk the lower path to and fro (SMIL `animateMotion`), two kangaroos (a light one and a dark umber one, `rooSVG(dark)`) hop continuously (`roos()`, no rests, 1100 ms hop cycle) along routes on a visibility graph: obstacles (domes, tunnels, pins, signs, buildings, beds, benches, bushes, the pond via `isPointInFill`, the hedge) are painted once into 8 px raster tables (`blocked`, `creek`; `CELL`, `PAD`, `paintDisc`), nodes sit on an integer 16 px grid, edges are tested on the raster, the creek may be jumped by long edges (`nearCreek`) but not landed in; the graph builds in ~100 ms. The emus are not dodged (that never worked reliably); the kangaroos simply never enter their lane. The lane is a static obstacle painted into `blocked` (feet cells only; `LANE` = the `low` path as a polyline, `laneY(x)`): from `LANE_GAP` (140 = the adult's head ~126 + a margin) above the path downwards, from the path's first to its last x plus `LANE_END` (90) with the ends rounded as a quarter ellipse, so the kangaroos can walk around its ends but never through or between the emus (no y cutoff, no x band; `inside()` only limits y < 1000). Summary of the kangaroo controller of this version, relative to the remodel in `kangaroo_change.md`: (1) performance: obstacles and the pond rasterised once into 8 px tables (`CELL`, `PAD`, `paintDisc`, `inCell`), integer 16 px node grid (`GRID`), long edges tested near the creek only (`nearCreek`), Dijkstra on typed arrays; the graph builds in ~100 ms (was 430-730 ms); (2) roaming: nodes cover the whole lawn, east region x 1120-1824 / y 328-984 and west region x 112-592 / y 344-792 (separate by scenery on purpose, one kangaroo each), the south-west pocket stays separate; (3) no emu avoidance (the lane above); (4) hopping is continuous (1100 ms cycle, no rests), kept as in the remodel;  with reduced motion they stand still), birds fly circles inside every dome (budgies under Replay, galahs and cockatoos under Custom game, lorikeets under Sandbox), a flock of galahs crosses the park now and then, cloud shadows drift, the construction site builds its map over and over (`siteBuild()`, Web Animations on `.sitetile`: all twelve spaces start as dashed outlines, the tiles (sand, water, rock, one green) drop in one by one 1.5 s apart, the finished map stays 5 s, then all tiles fade and it starts again; with reduced motion the finished map is shown), the pins drop in once at load and then bob. A paper grain (canvas noise, multiply) and a soft vignette lie over everything. With reduced motion the CSS animations are off and the SVG animations paused.
- **Kangaroos switched off (for now)**: `KANGAROOS_ON` in `web/js/landing.js` is `false`, so the start page neither draws the two `.roo` elements nor calls `roos()`. All kangaroo code (model, poses, `rooSVG`, `roos()`, `kangaroo_change.md`) stays untouched; set the constant to `true` to bring them back.
- **Animals in the bushes** (`PEEKERS`, `bushLife()`): every gum tree (`tree()`, class `bush`, `data-bush="x y s"`) has a `.behind` slot between its shadow and its canopy and a `.front` slot on top. Now and then (every 1.8-5 s, at most three at once, not while the tab is hidden, never on the koala's tree, only trees whose top edge is inside the stage) an animal peeks over the top edge of a random bush: wombat, cassowary, Tasmanian devil, dingo, platypus, quokka or kookaburra (no koala, there is the permanent one; never the same kind twice in a row), facing left or right at random (mirrored). Each is drawn at 1.35 x the tree's size factor as a head with a body below it in `.behind`, so the canopy hides everything below its edge; a clip path (per animal, on an untransformed wrapper) cuts off whatever reaches below 0.6 x the canopy radius, so a hidden body never shows under the bush. Two paws in `.front` grip the edge while the head is up (`paw` colour; birds have none; `feet: 'web'` = `webFeet()` for the platypus, `feet: 'claws'` = `clawPaws()` for the wombat and the devil: broad rounded paws with faint toe lines and four short, thick, curved dark claws hooked over the edge); `lift` raises the platypus and the cassowary higher so the bill and neck show. Animals never peek out of the sides of a bush. The drawings: the platypus from the side (round brown head, pale patch round the small eye, a slender flat slate bill widening a little to a rounded, slightly drooping tip, a crease where it meets the head); the quokka with upright oval ears, a round face a little wider at the cheeks, eyes close to a big nose and an open smile; the kookaburra in three-quarter view facing right (scruffy streaked crown with a ragged crest, rust eye stripe, long bill dark on top and pale below, a brown shoulder over a blue wing patch on dark blue feathers); the wombat with a broad domed head, chubby cheeks, small rounded ears, small eyes set wide, a pale muzzle round a big rounded nose, fine whiskers and a pale chest V; the cassowary in profile facing right (tall brown casque, blue head paler round the face, orange eye, pale pinkish bill, blue neck with a red collar and red wattles, shaggy black body); the Tasmanian devil with a round black head, big upright pink-lined ears, a grey-brown snout, brown eyes, an open mouth with two fangs and a pink tongue and the white chest blaze; the dingo with an orange head broad at the brow that flares to pointed cheek fur and narrows to the chin, a cream lower face wrapping the cheeks, cream brow spots, big ears with cream inside, round dark eyes, a small nose, a smile and a cream chest. The motion (Web Animations on the inner `.pk`, 4.2-6.8 s): it rises until only the eyes are over the edge, looks left and right, comes up fully with a small overshoot, looks around, rises a little and ducks back down. A click on a bush always brings one out at once (a shorter version without the careful first look; an animal already there is replaced); a click on the koala's tree makes the koala bob. This is an easter egg: the cursor does not change over a bush. The walking animals, the cloud shadows and the galah flock are drawn in `<g class="deco">` with `pointer-events: none`, so they never block a click on a bush (a drifting cloud shadow used to cover some trees). With reduced motion nothing peeks by itself and a click shows an animal without movement for 3.2 s.

- **Uluru lookout = full screen** (`lookout()`, `lookoutToggle()`): the lookout is a wooden hex platform with a coin viewer (binoculars on a post with a base plate), horizontally centred and at the far edge of the platform, facing Uluru: the base plate stands near the hex's upper edge and the binoculars reach out over it. A click on the hex switches the page to full screen (`requestFullscreen` on the document) and back (`exitFullscreen`); an easter egg, the cursor does not change. `fullscreenchange` (also when Esc leaves full screen) and the `(display-mode: fullscreen)` media query (the browser's own full screen, F11, where `document.fullscreenElement` stays empty) set `body.fullview`, which is the indicator: in a window the lens caps are on (dark lenses #3E4C52), in full screen the lenses are light blue with a white glint and the dashed ring of the platform turns gold #E0A02A (0.3 s transitions). In the browser's own full screen (F11) the page cannot leave it, so a click shows the pill `.fullhint` "Press F11 to leave full screen" for 2.6 s instead. Nothing happens in browsers without the Fullscreen API.

- **You are here** (`hereDot()`, `followHere()`): a red dot (#E2453B, white ring, ink outline, pulsing ring) with "You are here" as plain red text beside it (Land Text 700 16 px, #C2311F, a thin cream halo `paint-order: stroke` for legibility, no chip; the text moves to the left of the dot when the dot is right of x 1740). It starts at the gate (1106, 978) and follows the mouse, but only over places a visitor can be: `herePoints()` samples every path of `PATHS`, the creek and its outflow (`CREEK`, `OUT`, every 6 units, only inside the stage) and points inside the billabong, the gate plaza and the lookout platform; on each `pointermove` the dot heads for the sample point nearest the pointer (eased per frame, 22 % of the remaining distance; jumps with reduced motion). `#here` has no pointer events. It is drawn after the wildlife, so it stays above trees and domes.

- **Portrait windows** (width < 0.9 x height): the map is shown at the top at full width and a plain list follows (name, table id field + Open replay, the four other options as links, Terms and privacy). Mobile is not designed yet.

## Game stats graph: hover (results.js `raceSvg`, parkposter.css `.rshover`)
Moving the mouse over the score graph snaps to the nearest turn: a dotted guide line, a dot on each player's line and a small tip (turn, then both names with their colour dot and the score). The tip flips to the left near the right edge. Pointer events on a transparent `.rshit` rect; works in the big graph and the small one. The tip names the turn as round.seat (`1.1, 1.2, 2.1 ...`: the state's turn counts both players, the game's turn counts a round). The graph has real axes (`.rsaxis`: y at the left, x at the bottom, tick marks per round, no numbers on x) and the x label is just "Turns".

## Game stats graph: axes polish, final-score colour, â†‘/â†“ shortcut (results.js, main.js, shortcuts.js)
The x-axis is a plain line (no per-turn ticks). Axis labels are bigger (20 px, y labels 18 px away from the axis) and the graph area has less padding (8 / 10 px) so the plot does not shrink. The end scores at the right of the lines use the exact line colour (`seatColor`, not the darkened text colour). On the game stats, â†‘ shows the Table and â†“ the Graph (main.js key handler, only while `S.results`); both keys are listed in the shortcuts pop-up.

## Game stats graph: one point per round, "End" (results.js `race`)
A point is the score after both players have played the round (the state's turn counts both players, round = ceil(turn / 2); the tooltip says "Turn 20"). What the end of the game adds (the endgame cards, phase `over`) is one more point at the right, named "End" in the tooltip. Break lines moved to the round scale.

## Game stats graph: hover as bare numbers + round chip (results.js `raceSvg`, parkposter.css `.rsnum`, `.rschip`)
The hover tip box is gone. Each score is a bare number in the line colour (with a halo in the plot colour) next to its dot on the right of the guide line (left near the right edge): the leader above, the trailer below, so close scores never touch; a tie keeps the order of the last untied round. The round is a small chip on the x-axis ("Start" for the first point, "Turn n", "End"), the "Turns" title is hidden while hovering. At "End" only the dots and the chip show (the final scores already stand at the line ends).

## Names: "Bonus snaps", site name "Ark Nova Park"
The stats row "Projects snaps" is "Bonus snaps". Every page title, the logo's link/aria text and the back links say "Ark Nova Park" instead of "Ark Nova Replay" (titles end with "- Ark Nova Park"); `tests/test_api.py` follows.

## Game stats graph view: own categories (results.js `GRAPH_CATS`, `race(key)`)
In the graph view the left rail lists the graph categories: Points, Key events, Conservation, Appeal, Reputation, Money, X-tokens (no Cards). They are clicked, nothing scrolls (the glowing dot sits on the open one, `S.gsCat`). Each category is one graph with the same hover and break lines: Points = the score race (goal line at 100), Conservation / Appeal / Reputation / Money / X-tokens = that value of each player after every round. The graphs have no x-axis title. The y-axis of those graphs starts at 0 with round ticks (`niceTicks`). Key events is empty ("nothing to show here yet") until it is defined.

## Game stats graph: break lines, mug, 100 line, hover tick (results.js `raceSvg`, `mugIcon`)
Break lines are one solid cream line (2.5 px, fading from 60 % at the top to 18 % at the x-axis, a gradient in user space so it works on a vertical line) with the coffee mug on top, drawn as a vector in the colour of the player who triggered the break (cream when unknown); no numbers. The 100-point line (Points only) is an ordinary grid line with a faint red tint, its label "100" is tinted, and a barely visible red wash lies above it (the game-end zone). The hover has no vertical line any more: only a short tick on the x-axis next to the round chip. Padding above the plot grew (50 / 36 px) for the mugs. A break is drawn exactly midway between the two round points around it (`race`: `floor((turn - 0.5) / 2) + 0.5`), whenever in the round it started.

## Game stats graph: pills, discs, black seat (results.js `graphStyle`, `pillEl`, parkposter.css `.rsblk`, `.rscore`, `.rspillt`)
Every seat is drawn with the same parts: the line, a pill behind each number (end scores and hover numbers) and a disc behind the break mug. A seat colour fills pill and disc with dark (or white, `inkOn`) content; a white seat is ice white (#cfe8f2) with a dark casing under the line; a black seat is a black line (5.6 / big 8 px) with a thin grey core (#86949b), a black pill with a grey edge and grey text, and a black mug on a slate disc (#4e5d64).
