The hover dots are the same size for every seat (the black one gets a grey ring instead of the white one). The plot's right padding grew (big 78, small 52) for the end pills; the hover number pills sit above (leader) / below (trailer) the dot as before. Near the x-axis (low scores) the trailer's pill is held above the axis (`low` in `show`) and the leader's pill is pushed up to stay clear of it; the round chip sits 0.8 chip-heights below the axis (big graph) so the two never touch.

Break lines are one solid cream line (2.5 px, fading from 60 % at the top to 18 % at the x-axis, a gradient in user space so it works on a vertical line) with the coffee mug on top, drawn as a vector in the colour of the player who triggered the break (cream when unknown); no numbers. The 100-point line (Points only) is an ordinary grid line with a faint red tint, its label "100" is tinted, and a barely visible red wash lies above it (the game-end zone). The hover has no vertical line any more: only a short tick on the x-axis next to the round chip. Padding above the plot grew (50 / 36 px) for the mugs.

## Game stats graph: pills, discs, black seat (results.js `graphStyle`, `pillEl`, parkposter.css `.rsblk`, `.rscore`, `.rspillt`)
Every seat is drawn with the same parts: the line, a pill behind each number (end scores and hover numbers) and a disc behind the break mug. A seat colour fills pill and disc with dark (or white, `inkOn`) content; a white seat is ice white (#cfe8f2) with a dark casing under the line; a black seat is a black line (5.6 / big 8 px) with a thin grey core (#86949b), a black pill with a grey edge and grey text, and a black mug on a slate disc (#4e5d64). The hover dots are the same size for every seat (the black one gets a grey ring instead of the white one). The plot's right padding grew (big 78, small 52) for the end pills; the hover number pills sit above (leader) / below (trailer) the dot as before.

## 100. Starting hand with multi-word names and the discard decision
Scope: `web/js/frames.js`. (1) The lines of a merged setup step are grouped by their verb (after the player name, which can have several words: "Papa Emeritus V draw ..."; grouping by the second word put every line of such a player in one group, so the 8 starting cards had no frame). (2) The decision frame is dropped after a choice only for the real action card choice ("chooses action card X with strength N"), not for the draft line "chooses action cards (action card draft)", which dropped the initial discard frame. Found on table 930752686 as the second player.

## 101. Reveals hidden from the other player; Expedition and Scuba Dive 3 buttons
Scope: `src/ark_nova/replay/view.py` (`_reveal`, `_reveal_hidden`: the Hunter / Scuba Dive / Scavenging / Perception draws and keeps of the other player read "X draws N cards for the Y effect", "X keeps Z for the hunter / scuba dive effect", "X keeps N card(s) for the scavenging / perception effect"), `src/ark_nova/replay/options.py` (`_expedition_buttons`: Marine Research Expedition is two buttons, Expedition (greyed out without a person sponsor) and Scuba Dive 3), `web/js/action-bar.js` (`effectOption`, a disabled button, the fork sends the chosen option). The expedition buttons are unit-tested only; no sample game reaches that prompt in the replay.

## 102. Production start page: coming-soon options
Scope: `web/js/landing.js` (`off`: the options listed in `window.ARK_COMING_SOON`, and the live game until its word), `scripts/deploy_pages.py` (the production copy sets the list for the start page; the start page is a drawn map now, so the HTML is no longer rewritten).

## 103. Extension 1.3.0: base projects read off BGA's replay
Scope: `extension/button.js`, `extension/manifest.json`, `web/import.html`, `web/js/load.js`, server `base_projects` (`api/main.py`, `replay/config.py`, `replay/view.py`, `storage/index.py`).
- The extension parses the captured log and checks that `base_0..2` all appear in `slideMeeples` events (the same test as `infer_base_projects`). If yes, the "Open in Ark Nova Replay" button shows as before. If not, the review page shows a text prompt to pick a player's point of view with BGA's own replay buttons (the extension adds none). On that page the extension reads `#base-projects-holder #base-project-0..2 .project-card[data-id]` (the keys `P103`, ..., in board order), stores them as `ark:bp:<table>` and shows the button there too; the review tab gets it through `storage.onChanged`.
- The projects travel with the log: the import page keeps them as `baseprojects:<table>` in `LogStore`, `load.js` sends `base_projects` to `/api/tables/{id}/upload` and `/fork`; the server uses them (3 different base projects of the game, else they are ignored) in place of the inference. A log that shows all three never gets them.
- Everything the extension adds to a page has an x; a closed panel stays closed until the page changes.
- BGA's replay page holds the game in an iframe (`/archive/archive/frame.html?table=..`): `button.js` runs in all frames (`all_frames`), and the frame instance only reads the board and stores the projects (no panel); the top page shows the button through `storage.onChanged`. The prompt texts are one short line each.
- The top page of a replay also looks into same-origin iframes for the board (the script need not be injected into the frame), any `/archive/...` path counts as BGA's replay page, and the console (filter "ark-nova") says what is found.
- The replay page shows the Open button at once (no "Reading..." state); a click reads the board then and sends the projects it finds, else the replay tool infers them.
- The panel of the replay page is drawn by the script inside the game iframe (the frame covers the page and its address is known); it marks the iframe element `data-ark-nova`, and the top page draws the panel only when no such frame is marked.

## Game stats header: the real Elo after the game (results.js `eloAfter`; backend `storage/index.py`, `storage/tableinfo.py`, `replay/view.py`)
The Elo chip under each name shows the player's new Elo and its change. Source: BigQuery `all_games_stat.post_match_elo` (Elo after) and `post_match_elo - pre_match_elo` (change), read by `BigQueryIndex._elo_after` in a query of its own (a missing column or an error only drops the chip, never the replay) into `TableRecord.elos_after` / `elo_changes` (BGA player id -> int, same scale as `elos`); for an uploaded log without an index row `tableinfo.elos_after_from_tableinfo` (`rank_after_game` - 1300) and `elo_changes_from_tableinfo` (`point_win`). `replay/view.py` puts `elo_after` and `elo_change` on every entry of `players`. The panel reads them (`S.replay.players[i]`); without them the chip is left out, except on `localhost` / `127.0.0.1` where the old placeholder (winner 130 +7, other 116 -7) still shows, since a local copy has no BigQuery. Tests: `tests/test_api.py` (index), `tests/test_tableinfo.py`.

## Game stats header: the Elo chip never wraps (parkposter.css `.gselo`)
The chip is absolutely positioned under the name, so its width was limited to the name's width: with a short name ("goore") the number and the change broke into two lines. `.gselo` has `width: max-content; white-space: nowrap`, so it keeps one line (it may be wider than a short name; the right side is aligned to the name's edge on the left seat, the left side on the right seat).

## Game stats comparison: X-back and Clever swapped (results.js `groups`)
In the Actions category X-back now comes before Clever (order: Animals, Association, Build, Cards, Sponsors, X-back, Clever).

## Game stats: â†‘ on the table and â†“ on the graph do nothing (main.js keydown)
The arrow key of the view that is already open returns before `syncResults()`, so nothing is redrawn: the category no longer blinks (â†‘ on the table) and the orange category dot no longer jumps (â†“ on the graph).

## Game stats graph: Key events (results.js `keyEvents` / `keSvg` / `graphFor`, parkposter.css `.ke*`)
The rail entry "Key events" (`GRAPH_CATS` key `events`) draws `keSvg` instead of a race (`graphFor` picks it, also on resize). Same turn axis, break lines and mugs as the score race (`race('score')`); the left margin is 60 px like the other graphs. Three lanes (top to bottom: upgrades, workers, the Association actions; no lane text, an Ark Nova player knows what the icons mean), each a framed band (`.kelane`) with a row per player tinted in the seat colour (`.kerow`) ; the Association lane is 1.9x as tall as the other two, 22 px between the lanes. Events, from `keyEvents()` (one per thing, with its round = ceil(state.turn / 2)):
- Upgrades: "X upgrades <Type>II" in a label -> the action card icon (`r3c7/8/9/10/12`) with a frame of the owner's colour inside a casing (2 px; the outline colour below) and a cream II chip (`#f6edd3`, dark text `#10222a`, dark edge): readable on every seat colour, incl. the ice white and the slate of the black seat (graphStyle `df`).
- Workers: "X gains a new Association worker" -> the bare worker meeple of the seat colour (`/workers/<hex>.webp`), no number.
- Association actions: "X supports a conservation project on the ... slot : Name" -> the conservation project icon `r11c6`; "X increases reputation" -> the reputation icon; a partner zoo / university token that arrived on the player's board (counted per token type between consecutive states) -> its exact icon (`ICON_IDS[continent]`, `ICON_IDS[fac-...]`). Owner mark: a corner pip (a circle of the seat colour, r = 0.17 icon, on the icon's top-left corner, 1.6 px outline). Outline colour of pips and upgrade casings: cream `#f6edd3`, dark `#06222b` when the seat colour is bright (`inkOn(df)` is dark: white, yellow).
Several events of one row in the same round stack below each other, spread over the row's height (icon size 52 px at most, half the row height; the spacing is 1.15 icons, less when there are many so that the stack fits in the row; a little overlap is possible); upgrades and workers are at most 44 px. Hover: a faint band on the round, its icons grow (`.keev.now`), the round in the axis chip; hovering an icon shows an SVG tooltip with what happened and "Turn n Â· name".

(Key events: the seat tag bars at the left of the rows were removed; the reputation icon, which is dark, has a subtle cream glow `.kerep` in parkposter.css.)
(Key events: the player rows start 30 px left of the plot, flush with the lane frame's inner edge, so there is no empty strip; no extra axis line under the last lane.)
(Key events: the worker / project / reputation labels are matched with names that contain spaces, `([^Â·\n]+?)` instead of `(\S+)`: before, players like "Propaganda Panda" got no worker, project or reputation events.)
(Key events: the black seat's worker meeple gets the same subtle cream glow as the reputation icon, `.kerep`.)
(Key events: the glow `.kerep` is thinner (0.6 px edge, 2.5 px halo). The unupgraded Association2 card can hire a worker instead of supporting a project: log line "X hires a new worker (Association2 effect)" (a `slideMeeples` event, the same one `replay/actions.py` reads as `association_task` / `hire`): one event in the worker lane (the meeple) and one in the Association lane, `kind: 'hire'`, drawn as the silver worker+ badge of the card (`/action_icons/association_2_1.webp`) with the pip and the glow; tooltip "Hired a worker instead of a project".)
(Key events: the hire badge is drawn at 0.8 of the icon size: it fills its whole image, the other icons have a margin.)
(Key events: a worker taken as the 2 conservation points bonus (the choice worker / upgrade) has a small conservation shield with a 2 at its bottom right (`e.cp`, `.kecp`). Identifier in the log: a `takeBonus` event whose `bonus_desc.args` has `bonus_type` add-worker and `bonus_source_type` "bonus" (the same with upgrade-card is the upgrade choice); the viewer label is "X gets 1 x add-worker Â· X gains a new Association worker", the reputation track's worker has "(reputation track bonus)" after add-worker and is not marked. In the 114 logs: 5 workers and 25 upgrades taken that way.)
(Key events: the 2 on the worker's shield uses the number font 'Park Num' in black, like the conservation numbers on the boards (`.kecp`).)
(Key events: the row order follows `seatOrder()` like the maps and info boxes: the seat on the left / top is the top row of every lane.)
(Key events: partner zoos, universities and reputation taken as the free bonus of 5 or 8 conservation points have the conservation shield with 5 or 8 at the bottom right (`shield()`, `e.cp`). Identifier: a `takeBonus` event without `source` whose `remove` is "5-n" / "8-n" (`bonus_desc.args.bonus_source_type` "bonus"; the 2 point choice has an empty `remove`, 10 is the discard of scoring cards). The view carries them as `cp_bonuses` ([{move_id, seat, threshold, type}], `replay/view._cp_bonuses`); the label "X gets 1 x Fac" / "gets 1 x Partner-Zoo" at that move makes the next partner zoo / university arrival of the seat a gift (`pend` in `keyEvents`), "gets n x reputation" a reputation event. Older replays without `cp_bonuses` show no shield. Tooltip adds "(5 conservation bonus)".)
(Key events: the 5 / 8 conservation shield has the size of the worker's 2 shield (0.27 of a 44 px icon), not 0.27 of the bigger Association icon.)
(Key events: the university hover texts are "University: Hand size / 2 reputation / 2 science / Herbivores / Predators / Birds / Reptiles / Primates / Sea Animals" (`KE_UNI`); an unknown type shows just "University".)
(Key events: the tooltip suffix reads "(2 / 5 / 8 CP bonus)" instead of "conservation bonus".)
(Key events: all conservation shields (2 / 5 / 8) have one size, taken from the worker lane's icon size.)
(Controls: the first / previous / next / last buttons are never paled out (main.js no longer sets `disabled` on them); at an end a click does nothing. Fork keeps its own disabled state.)
(Game over bar: the move bar of a finished replay has ONE button at its left, only the view that can be switched to ("Game stats â€º" on the final board, "â€¹ Final board" on the results; `resultsSwitch`, forest green, `.rsgo`), and a post-game look: warm paper `#F3DFA6` with a wash of the winner's colour (`--win` on `.movepill`, the graph colour of the winner, 24 % at the left fading to 10 % at 38 %, plain at 62 %; no tint for a tie). The old two-flap switch (`.rsflap.fb/.fs`) is not drawn any more.)

## Normal turns, Break markers, sponsor and action card statistics (backend + viewer, 2026-10-25/27)
Backend work (parser, replay builder, replay view, one engine rule) plus the viewer code that reads it. This section is the porting document; it replaces the former `changelog_oct25.md`. All files touched here use LF line endings.

**Normal-turn timeline (new `src/ark_nova/parser/turns.py`, `parser/model.py`, `parser/log.py`).** `extract_turns(packets, break_orders)` reads the raw packets before `parse_log` drops state notifications and returns a `TurnTimeline` (`ParsedLog.normal_turns`): a normal turn starts at public `gameStateChange` id 7 (owner = `active_player`, per-player ordinal counted at once); id 10 / `startBreak` opens a neutral `break` phase, `finishBreak` leads to `between_turns`; id 98 = `scoring`, id 99 or `playerConcedeGame` = `terminal` (later events have no turn owner). State 20, action choice, cleanup, extra actions (Determination, Action: X), opponent decisions and borrowed (Hypnosis) action cards never start a turn. A turn runs from its start event to the next lifecycle boundary; a turn the log never closes stays (with a diagnostic). Duplicate / private copies of state 7 for the same move + owner are absorbed. `timeline.context(order)` gives `{phase, index, player, number, display, counts}` for any event order; `display` keeps the last ordinal during neutral phases. The state-20 `turn_markers` and the engine's action segmentation are unchanged.

**Break notifications counted once (new `parser/breaks.py`).** `accepted_break_orders(packets)`: one `startBreak` / `finishBreak` per game + move + kind (public copy preferred; start and finish on the same move stay distinct; without a move, packet/position identity). `parse_log` skips the other copies, so a resent `finishBreak` can no longer advance the round twice. `turns.py` uses the same set.

**Builder (`replay/builder.py`).** `Replay.history_frames`: (lifecycle transition, deep copy of the state just before it), used for graph endpoints. `h_action_card_cleanup` takes the actor from the normal-turn owner when known (fixes Hypnosis: game 930258897 had one completed turn too few from move 115). `midmessage` is in `IGNORED`.

**Replay view (`replay/view.py`).**
- Every step's state has `turn_context` = `{phase: setup|turn|break|between_turns|scoring|terminal, index, seat, number, display, counts[seat]}` (from the step's first owned event; `gameStateChangePrivateArg` does not own the following public "Starting a new break" line).
- A new informational step "<name> starts turn <n>" is inserted at every normal start (state = the board just before it, `current_action` None, no fork object). Insertion keeps the existing steps in their order (merge by `_history_order`, never a global sort: a sort swapped simultaneous Break discards and bonus card choices). These steps make every replay 50-70 steps longer, so the Move counter at the end is higher than before (e.g. 478 -> 545) and old numeric step links point elsewhere.
- `_combine_action` / `_combine_runs` never merge steps of different `turn_context` (so "Starting a new break" is its own step now, followed by the discards).
- New top-level fields: `normal_turns` = rows `{index, seat, number, start_move, end_move, players: [{score, appeal, conservation, reputation, money, x_tokens} x2]}` (metrics at the end of that turn), `turn_start` = the same metrics before the first turn, `break_markers` = `[{start_move, end_move|null, turn: display + .5, seat}]` (seat = the player of the structured `getBonuses` with `source` "triggering break"; null = unknown). With `first_moves` (mini games) the timeline is cut to the selected events, and the mini games' own payloads do not publish these fields.
- Sponsor gains: the old `_sponsor_gains` / `OWN_STATS` / `NO_STATS` / `SAVES` code moved to the new `replay/sponsor_stats.py` (see below); `tracked_sponsors` comes from there.

**Viewer.**
- `side-panel.js` `turnNumber(st)`: returns `st.turn_context.display` when present (BGA replays: the turn number goes up when the turn starts, not when the action card is chosen; setup = 0; breaks / scoring keep the last number); live games, forks, sandbox and old payloads keep the old calculation. New tooltip: "Turn: the acting player's normal turn number, counted when the turn starts. During a break or after play ends, the last turn number stays visible. Setup does not count".
- `results.js` `race(key)`: with `normal_turns`, point 0 = `turn_start`, Turn n = the last row with number n (after both players' n-th turns); the per-step `ceil(turn / 2)` sampling is only the fallback for old payloads. Break lines come from `break_markers` (an empty array = no breaks); the label parsing ("triggering break" / "Starting a new break") is only the fallback. `keyEvents`: an event's column is `turn_context.number` in a turn, `display + .5` in a break, and setup / scoring / terminal events are left out; `keSvg` keeps events between the first and last column (no longer only exact columns). `countStats`: turn counts and the first player come from `normal_turns` when present; `advanceBreak` and `getBonuses` "triggering break" are counted once per identity (channel + move + type + uid, else packet id + offset); repeated `startBreak` / `finishBreak` copies do not toggle `inBreak`.

**Sponsor hover statistics (new `replay/sponsor_stats.py`, `replay/sponsor_sources.py`, `parser/sponsor_messages.py`; viewer `web/js/sponsor-stats.js`, `cards.js` `showStats`).** Totals are per replay position (`state.sponsor_stats`), replay only. `SponsorLogStats` keeps card ownership (`playSponsor` / `discardCardsOnDisplay`) and the current action; a resource only counts for the sponsor's owner (an opponent's payment from Basic Research / Release of Patents is not counted); `RESOURCE_POLICY` limits the resources per card, `FINAL_ONLY` counts them only in the `scoring` phase. Split appeal keys: `appeal_initial` / `appeal_final` (S214, S279), `appeal_enlargement` / `appeal_final` (S269), `appeal_income` / `appeal_final` (S274, the initial 1 appeal excluded, both BGA ids incl. `S274_VictoryColumn`), `appeal_final` (S257, S280, S281). Counters: `cards_gained` (S201, from public state 27 with sourceId S201), `effect_used` (S203, a project supported in an Association action of strength exactly 4, BGA's outer strength), `tokens_spent` (S215, S218, S253, each token meeple once), `attacks_prevented` (S225, from the kept `midmessage` "is not affected by Venom|Constriction|Hypnosis|Pilfering thanks to their Quarantine Lab sponsor card", only when the attacker is the opponent: BGA also sends it for the owner's own animal), `water_covered` / `rock_covered` / `water_ignored` / `rock_ignored` (S219, from the log-built board before the effect), `placement:<type>[:<strength>]` (S221, state 91 with sourceId S221), `doubled:<type>` (S271, footprint on a known map, else explicit outcomes), `animals_marked` (S268, state 103), `scuba3` (S270 "Scuba Dives"), `kiosk` / `pavilion` (S280, state 30, new buildings only), `saved` + `donations` (S273, every donation, capped by the cost, without X Association's rebate), Franchise Business S265 shows its money even at 0. Label changes: S249 "Perceptions" (was "Bird icons played"), S270 "Scuba Dives" (the old Expeditions row and the engine observers for S270 / S273 are removed from `differential.py`). Quarantine Lab messages are now kept by the parser (passive for the engine, ignored by the builder), so they appear as log lines. `sponsorStatRows(got, key)` builds the rows: resources with icons, the split appeal rows, counters as "nÃ—", placement / doubled bonuses with readable names; zero rows hidden, "nothing yet" when empty.

**Action card variant statistics (`replay/variant_stats.py`, `cards.js` `VARIANT_NAME` / `ACTION_STATS`).** +1 Build (side II): `identical` "Additional identical standard enclosures (side II)" (each further standard enclosure of a size already placed in the same action); +1 Build counters ignore Engineer and free buildings. Keep Cards: new panel, `extra_kept` "Additional cards kept" (the discard Keep Cards avoids, `cards_action.discard_count`, once per draw action). Marketing (Sponsors variant 4, side II) counts only when a sponsor is really played. Ignore Animals (`ignored`) is now read from the log (new `parser/animal_ignores.py`, `ParsedLog.animal_ignores`, `logged_variant_gains`): the player's private `conditions.ignored` > 0 before the play, the public flow `ignore = 1, max = 1`, action Animals1, and no `bonus-ignore-conditions` token spent for that play; it no longer depends on the engine. Names: Sponsors variant 1 "Trade Sponsors", variant 4 "Marketing Sponsor" (`data/action_cards.json`, `scripts/cardgen/site/locales/en/common.json`; `scripts/import_data.py` keeps them on re-import).

**Engine and differential harness.** `engine/animals_action.py` `play`: a pending Camouflage is used up by the next animal played, even when that animal needs no bypass or another credit covers it (cleared before the new animal's own abilities, so a Camouflage animal sets a fresh one). `replay/differential.py`: `_use_ignore_branch` follows the log evidence (explicit Ignore branch -> `animals_single`, a token or an explicit normal branch -> no), only guesses when unknown; `_needs_a_token` adds up all non-Ignore credits (Research Institute + WAZA can cover two conditions). Engine stays `0.2.5-dev`.

**Tests.** New: `tests/test_turn_history.py`, `test_break_markers.py` / `.cjs`, `test_sponsor_stats.py` / `.cjs`, `test_sponsor_stats_rest.py`, `test_ignore_evidence.py`, `test_variant_stats_observers.py`, `test_variant_stats.cjs` (the `.cjs` files run the real viewer functions with node; the pytest files call them when node is on PATH). Most real-log cases need the logs in `log_examples/` (902906029, 927683584, 930258897, 937181556, 760993552, 795215166, 800966139 and the older ones) and skip without them.

### Move numbers are BGA's move numbers (replay, fork)

The number in the move list and in the "Move" box of the control panel is BGA's own move number (`move_id` of the log), so a position can be found on BGA by the same number. `view.py` `_join_labels` gives every step that joins several BGA moves `move_from` (its first move; `move_id` stays its last): the steps of a replay are no longer numbered 0, 1, 2 ... . `playback.js` `moveNumbers()` returns, per step, `first` / `last` (the BGA moves it holds), `show` (the step starts a move that no earlier step has shown) and `cur` (the number for the box); a step without a `move_id` (steps made in a fork, the sandbox, a live game) goes on counting from the last number (+1 per step), so a fork continues after the move it was made at. The move list writes the number (`show` and `first`) only on the first step of a move and leaves the others blank (the steps of one move, and the steps "X starts turn N", which BGA records in the move of the turn before, have no number of their own). The "Move" box shows the number of the step on show (`cur`); typing a number goes to the first step that holds it, or to the next step when BGA has no such move (BGA skips moves that show nothing; the first is move 2), past the end to the last step (`stepOfMove`). The `#13` in the page address and the `step=` of a fork link stay step indexes of the list (not move numbers). Tests: `tests/test_move_numbers.py` (every BGA move with a log line is held by a step, the numbers never go back).

### The move box and the full screen button of the control panel (replay, fork, sandbox, play)

Second row of the control panel (`.controls2`): settings wheel | `?` | full screen | Move | Fork (`order` puts Move first on screen). The Move label is `<label class="jump"><span class="jl">Move</span><input id="jump">`: "Move" is only a visually hidden label (`.jl`). The pill shows the pale "#" (`.jump::before`, 15px, 40% opacity) right beside the number (23px Park Label, centred text). The pair is nudged left so the NUMBER looks centred although it is not exactly (the "#" counts only a little): `fitJump()` in `main.js` sets the input's width to its digit count in `ch` and `data-len` (1 / 2 / 3+) on the label, and the CSS `padding-right` of `.jump[data-len]` (8px / 6px / 5px, twice the nudge) does the shifting. Typing a number jumps via `stepOfMove` (previous section). Widths on the replay page (`.controls:has(.forkbtn)`): Move 70px, Fork 66px, round buttons 40px, 8px gaps: the row fills its 288px exactly.

Full screen button (`#fullscreen`, class `settingsbtn extrabtn fsbtn`, in the four pages; `web/js/fullscreen.js`, started by `main.js` `init`): the same as F11, using the Fullscreen API on the page. The icon (corner brackets / inward brackets), title and `aria-pressed` follow the real state, also when the browser itself is in F11 full screen (no `fullscreenElement`, the window is as big as the screen; a page cannot leave that one, F11 does). The "Full screen" switch of the settings pop-up was removed (`settings.js`); `shortcuts.js` mentions the button in the F11 text.

### Font of the move bar and the log: Nunito Sans

The move bar (`.movepill`: 700 / 20px; also the Results switch text `.rsswitchtext` inside it, 600 / 19px) and the log (`.sidebar .moves`: 600 / 18px, the current line and the move numbers 700) are set in Nunito Sans (soft terminals, built for running text), no longer in Barlow. Barlow stays the base font of the rest of the page. The faces are `@font-face` rules next to the Barlow ones at the top of `parkposter.css` (weights 600 and 700, Latin subset, `web/fonts/nunito-sans-latin-{600,700}-normal.woff2`, from the fontsource package). The engine line in the bar still uses Park Label. Compared with Barlow, Nunito Sans is wider, so the log wraps a little earlier; the sizes were not changed.

The coloured player name in the move bar ("X must choose an action card", `.who` + `b` in `.movepill .actionbar`) used to be followed by the bar's flex column gap (.7rem, 11px) instead of a word space (about 5px). `parkposter.css` pulls the name back (`.who:has(+ b) { margin-right: calc(.27em - .7rem) }`), so the gap is a normal space; nothing changes where the name and text are inline (the draft pop-up header).

### Player names in colour in every step text (move bar and log)

`labelNode` in `web/js/log-labels.js` (the one function that builds the step text of the bar and every line of the log, also after a POV change) now wraps each player's name in `<span class="pname">` with that player's text colour (`seatText`, the same dark-enough-on-paper colour the decisions of the move bar use). The names come from `S.replay.players`; the match is on whole names (a name inside a longer word is left alone, longer names first, regex characters escaped), so an icon in the middle of a line does not hide a name. Before this only the decisions (`.who` in `action-bar.js`) were coloured; the step text and the log were plain ink.

### Placement bonus buttons in the move bar are named

A building that covers several placement bonuses makes each one a pending effect of the kind `pbonus` (the player chooses the order; `engine/game.py`). The bar used to print the raw kind ("pbonus") on each button, because `replay/options.py` dropped the nested `bonus` and `effect_name` only knows animal abilities. Now `options.effects` carries `bonus: {type, value}` for `pbonus` effects (every mode takes its options from `step_options`: replay, fork, sandbox, live play and recorded games), and `web/js/bonus-names.js` (`PLACEMENT_BONUS_NAME`, one table for the wording) names them ("Gain 5 money", "Take a card", "Marketing", ...); `effectButton` in `action-bar.js` shows the bonus icon (`ICON_IDS['bonus:' + type]`) and the name, and the tooltip says "Placement bonus: ...". A type that has no entry shows its type with spaces. Recorded live games saved before this change keep their old `options` (the buttons then read "placement bonus").

### Amounts of money, X tokens, reputation, appeal and conservation are icons (move bar, log, all modes)

`labelNode` (`web/js/log-labels.js`: the step text of the bar and every log line, in replay, fork, sandbox, live play and recorded games) turns a number directly in front of the word money / xtoken(s) / reputation / appeal / conservation (points) into the `<MONEY:5>` markup (`amountMarkup`), which draws the counter's icon with the number inside (`.labelamount`, white number with a dark outline, as on the tracker). `XTOKEN` was added to `WITH_AMOUNT`; the X token's number sits right of centre (`.labelx`) because the icon has white squares on its left arm. The same icon is used by the buttons of the bar (`effectButton` in `action-bar.js`: "Gain" + `amountIcon(kind, n)` for gain effects and for the placement bonuses money / xtoken / reputation / appeal). Sizes are in `parkposter.css` (em of the surrounding text, so a log line of 18px and the bar of 20px both fit: 1.42em / 1.5em; money tile 1.3em / 1.38em). Also converted: "pays N for building ..." (BGA leaves the word "money" out there; `PAYS_BUILDING_RE` in `amountMarkup`; 293 lines in the 20 sample games, all building costs). "pays N money for ...", "pays N xtoken for ..." and BGA's own `pays <MONEY:N> to gain <APPEAL:N>` go through the same icon path. Not converted: any other bare number without the word.

### Vertical centring in the move bar: text next to icons, and the engine check mark

Measured on the pixels (2x screenshots, ink of the letters and the check against the middle of the pill). (1) The amount icons (`.labelamount`) were placed with a fixed `vertical-align: -.3em`, which made the line box taller above than below, so the text next to an X-token icon sat 1.75px low. Now the icon's middle is put on the middle of the line's strut: `vertical-align: calc(.33em - var(--ah) / 2)` with `--ah` the icon's height (1.42em in the log, 1.5em in the bar; money tile 1.3em / 1.38em, class `.labelm`); the cap height of the first letter is now within 1px of the pill's middle. (2) The engine check mark (`.engine-badge.engine`) was an inline svg on the text baseline in a block with a line strut: it sat 2.5px above the middle of the pill (the badge box was 35px tall for a 30px svg). The badge and its svg are now `display: block`, so the check is exactly centred. The icon pictures themselves (`web/icons`, cropped to their ink) are centred in their cells.

- Amount icons: the number inside (`.labelamount b`) uses `Park Num` (the counter font of the page) instead of the system font that `replay.css` set; override in `parkposter.css`, size .92em.

- Placement bonus Worker on map 11 is shown as "Extra Shift" (it takes a worker back, the engine already did that): `replay/options.py` sends `bonus.type = extra-shift` for map 11, `bonus-names.js` names it, `icons.js` has the tile icon `bonus:extra-shift` (r5c15).

- Opening discard text (all modes): "X discards 4 cards Â· Y discards 4 cards Â· The display is replenished" (no card names, no first display): `replay/view.py` (`_event_text_pov`, `initial_discard`), `live/bgalog.py` (`initial_discard`). Backend only.

- Replay steps: an animal played / bought, a sponsor bought, a building built are no longer joined with what they bring: the cost is one step, every gain (appeal, reputation, conservation, X-tokens) and every placement bonus its own step, like when the engine plays it (`replay/view.py`, `_combine_action` is no longer called, animal gains no longer merged). Backend only; more steps per turn, so step numbers of a replay shift. The Wave and the skipped-action merges stay.

- Replay steps, all of them: no step is joined to another any more (`_combine_runs` for the Wave and the gains of animals, `_fold_unchanged` for steps that change nothing visible are no longer called); every log line / effect is a step. Texts of more than one line in the move bar went from about 310 to 20 distinct ones (all the action card draft + starting hand step). Backend only.

- Action card draft texts: the last keep and the starting hands are two steps (`replay/view.py` `_split_keep_and_draw`): "X keeps Money Sponsors and Terrain Build" (the action card variant names of `data/action_cards.json`, also in the picks) and "X draws 8 cards Â· Y draws 8 cards" (one step per player in seat order, "X draws 8 cards and 2 endgame cards"; 9 cards on map 14, counted from the log). The step of the first player shows the second player's hand and the decks as before.

- Two steps that say one thing are one (`replay/view.py` `_merge_pairs`, `live/bgalog.py`): "X gains a new Association worker (reputation track bonus)" (was "gets 1 x add-worker (...)" + "gains a new Association worker") and "X pays 5 to buy A from display" (was "buys A from display" + "pays 5 money for playing sponsor from reputation range"). `log-labels.js` shows the money icon for "pays N to buy".

- Numbers in the amount icons: appeal and conservation are black (no outline), money / X-token / reputation stay white with the dark outline, as on the map. All five are centred by the drawn digits (`log-labels.js` `centreInk`, canvas measure of Park Num, `--nx` / `--ny`): measured with the digits in magenta, the middle of the digits is within 0.1 px of the icon's middle sideways and within 0.3 px (0.8 px for money in the log) up / down, before it was up to 2.3 px sideways (X token) and 1 to 1.6 px too high. The extra shift of the X token to the right was removed.

- X token without the white squares wherever a number lies on it: `web/icons/r5c6_ns.webp` (plain) and `r3c2_ns.webp` (bordered), made from the sprites by painting the four squares in the body colour. Used by the amount icon of the move bar / log (`log-labels.js`), the player box (`side-panel.js`, `pic('xtoken_ns')`), the X counter of the action bar, the bonus slots with a number (`shared.js`, `association.js`, `map_editor.js`). The originals stay for bonuses without a number (placement bonus, reputation track bonus). Registered in `icons.js` (`names.xtoken_ns`, `ICON_IDS['bonus:xtoken_num']`) and `icons.json`.

- "X plays ANIMAL for 14 and places it ...": the price is now the money icon with the number (`PLAYS_FOR_RE` in `log-labels.js`, move bar and log), like the donation text.

- The action badge (action card icon + strength) of the move bar is left out when the step has no action card (a building from a bonus / effect): it was an empty player-coloured chip (`actionBadge` in `action-bar.js`).

- Step texts from the log's private lines ("You draw ...") read "X draws ...": `_you_to_name` in `replay/view.py` gives the verb its -s (draw, discard, keep, pouch, dig, store, sell, increase, give, get).

- "X increase the size of a a size-2 enclosure" (BGA's template) reads "X increases the size of a size-2 enclosure" (`_fix_grammar` in `replay/view.py`).

- The orange "Engine: Normal turn starts (backend state 7)" line under "X starts turn N" is no longer shown (`main.js`); other engine flags still are.

- The "X starts turn N" steps (`_normal_history` in `replay/view.py`) are no longer inserted into the replay: the turn number still comes with every step (`turn_context`) and `normal_turns` is unchanged. `tests/test_turn_history.py` adjusted.

- Log (`#moves`): the amount icons are centred on the lowercase text (`vertical-align: calc(.27em - var(--ah) / 2)` in `parkposter.css`), before they were centred on the capitals and looked high. The move bar keeps its own value.

- Log amount icons: the offset is .18em (was .27em in the note above): the first value moved them by about a pixel only.
- Log: the step number, the text and the engine badge are centred vertically in the step (`align-items: center` on `.moves li`); the amount icons keep their original alignment (the two notes above about #moves .labelamount are withdrawn).
- Log: the step number and the engine badge line up with the first line of the step text, also when an amount icon makes that line taller: `align-items: flex-start; line-height: 1.5` on `.moves li` (replaces the `align-items: center` of the note above).
- Numbers on the amount icons inside the decision bar (`.movepill .actionbar`, e.g. the Gain buttons) lost Park Num: `.movepill .actionbar b { font: inherit }` (0,3,2) beat `.labelamount b` (0,2,2) and gave them the bar's Nunito Sans 700. Two rules with the bar's prefix put Park Num, the stroke and the white / black colour back (`parkposter.css`).
- Move bar buttons (`.movepill .abtn`) all have the height of a button with an amount icon (`min-height: 2.04em`, 40.8 px at 20 px): plain buttons were 33.8 px, money 38.4, reputation / appeal 40.8.
- The step "X gets 1 x take-in-range-or-deck (reputation track bonus / map bonus space)" is no longer shown (`_ANNOUNCES_TAKE` in `replay/view.py`): it only announced the card the next step takes. The other "gets N x ..." announcements (size-2, Snapping, conservation, X token, upgrade, money ...) are still steps.

## Action strength: "strength N" is the strength icon with the number (log-labels.js `STRENGTH_RE`, `labelIcon`, `web/icons/strength.svg`, parkposter.css `.labelstr`)
In every step text (move bar and log) "with strength 4" is now the BGA strength icon (black square with four perforation holes on its left) with the number inside, like the X token / money icons (`<STRENGTH:4>`, in `WITH_AMOUNT`). The word "strength" is gone from the text. Only a number directly after the word counts.
- The icon is redrawn as a vector, `web/icons/strength.svg` (viewBox 118 x 118, `#211e21`, four white holes 13.15 x 13.2 at y 13.13 / 39.29 / 65.58 / 91.74, measured from `r5c5.webp`; it differs from the bitmap by 0.5 grey levels on average, so it looks the same but stays sharp at any zoom). The sheet cell `r5c5` (`names.json` "strength") is still there for anything that wants the bitmap.
- The number is white in Park Num like the other counters; `.labelstr b { left: 10.6% }` centres it on the dark part of the icon, not on the holes.
(Action strength: "places action card X at position N" (also "(Clever)") shows the same strength icon with the slot number instead of "position N" (`POSITION_RE` in log-labels.js).)

## Action cards in step texts are icons (log-labels.js `actionMarkup` / `actionCardIcon`, icons.js `VARIANT_NAMES`, parkposter.css `.acticon`)
The name of an action card in a step text (move bar and log) is now its icon, with the descriptive name and side as the tooltip ("Sponsors (I)", "Money Sponsors (II)"): "chooses action card SponsorsII with ...", "places action card X at position N (finishing action)", "places Build at position 1 (Clever / Boost effect)", "upgrades AnimalsII", "adds a multiplier token on action card ...", and the special cards named by number: "(Sponsors2 effect)", "(Animals3 ability)", "(Build4)", "Cards4 effect". `labelNode(text, step)` takes the step now (main.js, playback.js, pov.js pass it).
- Icon: exactly the action card of the player box (`.ppactions .ac`, side-panel.js) in the em of the text: white tile, blue (`--act1`, side I) or magenta (`--act2`, side II) frame, the type's icon (`ACTION_ICON`) and, for a special card of Marine Worlds, its silver effect badge (`/action_icons/<type>_<variant>_<side>.webp`) top left. No separate I / II chip any more (the frame colour says the side; the tooltip names it). CSS `.acticon` in parkposter.css.
- Which card: the side and the variant a text does not give are those of the card the player has in that step's state (`ownedCard`: the player named at the start of the text, else the step's actor); "Sponsors2 effect" gives the variant itself. Variants only in Marine Worlds games.
- Tooltip names are `VARIANT_NAMES` (the names of `data/action_cards.json`, with "Pavilion" spelled right; Supply Association was missing in cards.js `VARIANT_NAME`, not changed there).
- "position N" after an action card is the strength icon with N (`POSITION_RE` now also takes the icon marker, for the Clever / Boost texts without "action card").
- Left as text: "max strength Animals" (a rule name, not a card), the action card draft texts ("picks Money Sponsors from ...").

## Money / appeal / conservation number in step texts: tighter digit spacing (log-labels.js `NUM_LS`, parkposter.css `.labelamount.labelm b`, `.labeldk b`)
The number inside the money, appeal and conservation icon of a log / move bar text has a letter-spacing of -.04em, so that two digits (10, 12, 18, 22, 33) sit a little closer. Only these three, only step texts; the tiles of the zoo map, the conservation track and the association strip are unchanged. `inkShift` / `centreInk` take the spacing into account (canvas `letterSpacing`) so the digits stay centred on the icon.

## Two-digit money numbers on tiles: smaller, tighter, centred (icons.js `moneyTwo` / `MONEY_TWO`)
A money number of two or more digits (10, 12 ...) on a money tile is drawn at 90 % of the single-digit size with a letter-spacing of -.04em and an outline 90 % as wide, centred on the tile by its ink both ways (the vertical centre is measured with a canvas, `moneyTwo`). Used by the placement bonuses of the zoo map (board.js, also the side area tags), the conservation track rewards (shared.js `bonusTile`) and the bonus slots of the association board (association.js `slotGlyph`). Single digits are unchanged; the old `moneyDx` is no longer used by them (kept in icons.js).

## Replay: decision frames only at the start of a turn (frames.js `framesOf`, `turnStart`)
A replay step used to have a second frame with the pending decision ("X must place a building", "X must discard ..."). The decision has already been taken in the next step, so a replay now keeps only the decision frame at the start of a turn ("X must choose an action card", also the log-built fallback in the phases `turn` / `final_turns`). A live game (`PLAY`) keeps every decision frame; fork and sandbox show the bar next to the text as before. The draft frames are not decisions and stay.

## Action card icon in step texts: smaller horizontal margin (parkposter.css `.acticon`)
`margin: 0 .08em 0 .22em` (was `0 .5em 0 .4em`): the gap around the icon is like that of the other step icons; the variant badge still hangs into the left margin.

## Player box: the five counters are spaced evenly (parkposter.css `.pp .ppres`)
`display: flex; justify-content: space-evenly` instead of five equal grid cells: the gap between the icons and to the box edges is the same everywhere (11.9 px at 1920 wide, measured on the icon images; the icons are 35-55 px wide, so equal cells gave unequal visible gaps).

## Player box pills: income gold, appeal red, conservation green (parkposter.css `.rt.ov small`, `small.eg`)
The income pill on the money tile is gold (#F2B53A), the endgame live count is red (#D9503F) under appeal and green (#6FD3A8, the former income colour) under conservation, matching the ticket and shield icons. The violet is gone.

## Income pop-up (new `web/js/incomepop.js`; side-panel.js, parkposter.css `.incpop` / `.incslip`; server `replay/view.py` `income_lines`)
Hovering the gold income pill shows a torn paper slip to its left instead of the browser tooltip: heading "Income at the next break", a segmented share bar, one line per source (coloured tab, dotted leader, amount) and a double-ruled Total. Every sponsor is its own line. Order: appeal track, zoo (kiosks, map ability), sponsors, anything else (map bonuses); inside a group the biggest amount first; no group names. Colours follow the line position in the cycle `#D9503F #3B7DD8 #F2B53A #8E63D2 #2E9E5B #E86FA6 #8B5A3C #1FA3A3`, the bar uses the same colours. Data: `players[i].income_lines` = `[category, label, amount, card key|null]` (new, next to `income_parts` / `income_total`, which are unchanged; the lines sum to `income_total`). Without `income_lines` (old data) the slip shows the total as the appeal line. The slip is `position: fixed` on `body`, coordinates divided by `S.scale` like the other fixed pop-ups.

## Conservation pop-up (incomepop.js `showConservation`; side-panel.js `egPill`; server `replay/view.py` `endgame_lines`, `live/projection.py`)
Hovering the green endgame pill under the conservation tile shows the same slip as the income one: "Conservation at the final scoring", one line per sponsor with endgame conservation points, then a thin rule (2 px, 45 % ink) and one line per endgame card that counts, in the same colour cycle. The card lines show only where the cards are shown (own seat / open eye), like the pill's total. Data: `players[i].endgame_sponsor_lines` = `[[sponsor key, points]]` (public) and `endgame_card_lines` = `[[card key, points]]` (private; the live projection sets it to `[]` for the other viewers), biggest first, zero lines left out.

## Conservation pop-up: hidden endgame cards (incomepop.js `showConservation(chip, p, withCards, hidden)`, side-panel.js `nEgNow`)
For a player whose endgame cards are not shown, the slip gets one grey line "Endgame card (hidden)" per card that will count (same count as the little card on the player box: 1 normally, 2 at the start, more with Resistance), a grey hatched tab, a "?" for the amount, no share-bar segment, and the total reads "<sponsors> + ?". Own seat / open eye: the real card lines as before. The pill itself still shows the sponsor total and only exists when that is above 0.

## Appeal pop-up, same as conservation (incomepop.js `showEndgame(chip, p, kind, withCards, hidden)` replaces `showConservation`; side-panel.js; server `replay/view.py` `endgame_appeal_lines`)
Hovering the red endgame pill under the appeal tile shows "Appeal at final scoring": one line per sponsor with endgame appeal (Arcade and Mascot Statue included, same sums as `endgame.live_scoring`), then the thin rule and the endgame card element exactly as on the conservation slip (own seat / open eye: real card lines; otherwise one grey "Endgame card (hidden)" row per counting card with "?", total "n + ?"). The cards give no appeal today, so for the own seat the card part is empty; a future card with appeal would come as `players[i].endgame_card_appeal_lines` (private like `endgame_card_lines`, the live projection must clear it then). The conservation slip title is now "Conservation at final scoring" (no "the"). Data: `players[i].endgame_appeal_lines` = `[[sponsor key, points]]` (public).

## Neutral cube colours (association.js `BLOCKED_COLORS`)
The cubes that cover the unused donation spaces and project slots in a 2 player game (`unusedColor()`) now come from six vivid BGA colours in this order: purple, orange, yellow, red, green, blue (the first one that neither player is close to). Brown (was first) is gone, and so are grey, white and black, which players often use themselves. The cube art is unchanged: the fan site's lossless 48 px cells in BGA's colour order (`scripts/build_cubes.py`), no recreation needed.

## Player box: three-digit numbers (side-panel.js `tile`, parkposter.css `.rt.ov b.d3`)
A tile number with three digits (appeal over 99, rare) gets the class `d3`: letter-spacing -.09em and 22 px instead of 24 px, so it stays inside the ticket / shield. One and two digits are unchanged.

## Sponsor cards: printed bottom strip (card images)
The generated sponsor cards painted their text panel over the lower half of the printed card and lost the strip with the instant effect chip (yellow), the endgame chip (brown) and the reputation / conservation / appeal chips. `scripts/build_sponsor_strips.py` cuts that strip out of the printed scans (`scripts/cardgen/site/img/sponsors/`) into transparent PNGs (`site/img/sponsor_strips/<bga id>.png`, the paper is removed by flood fill so white parts inside chips stay), `BaseSponsorCard.tsx` puts it back flush with the card's lower edge (`.sponsor-strip`, `uniform.mjs`). All 82 sponsor images (`web/cards`, `web/cards_large`) were rebuilt. The earlier chips drawn by the component for the 9 sponsors with a bonus were dropped: the strip already shows them. Cardgen now needs `zod` and `lucide-react` (added to `scripts/cardgen/package.json`).

## Cards: card number and long names (card images)
`uniform.mjs` now sets one style for the card number on every kind of card (animals, sponsors, projects, endgame cards): black, MyriadPro-Cond 16 px, 30 x 50 px box, vertical text at the right end of the name stripe (project cards had a white number before). On the large card the names of sponsors, projects and endgame cards get 42 px padding on both sides (centred) and balanced wrapping, so a long name (Bavarian Forest National Park) breaks into two lines like on the small card and never touches the number. Animals keep their layout (scientific name) and only get the number style. All 300 card images were rebuilt.

## Animal names on the large card (card images)
`shoot.mjs` (`fitNames`): on the large animal card the name stays on one line (the Latin name lies below it) and clear of the card number (305 of 373 design px). A name that is too wide gets -0.02em letter spacing and the next smaller of 19.5 / 18 / 16.5 / 15 px that fits: Northern Plains Gray Langur, Ecuadorian Squirrel Monkey, American Whitespotted Filefish, Southern Blue-Ringed Octopus, Golden Snub-Nosed Monkey 19.5 px, Panamanian White-Faced Capuchin 18 px; all other animals stay at 21 px. The small card keeps its own layout. Fixed the spelling "Squirell" in the card text (`Animals.ts`, locale file; the data files and the card key `A467_EcuadorianSquirellMonkey` are unchanged, they are part of the engine data hash). The card number is hidden again on the small cards (a rule of the number style had shown it).

## Animals: name and Latin name centred (card images)
On the large animal card the name and the Latin name below it now sit centred in the name stripe (`uniform.mjs`: the name's `pt-1` is removed and it is moved up 1 px; before, the pair sat about 5 design px too low). Small cards unchanged. All animal images were rebuilt.

## Cubes in higher resolution, lighter appeal chip
`scripts/build_cubes_hd.py` redraws the ten cubes of `web/cubes` at 192 x 192 px (the fan site's sheet has 48 px cells; the board draws them at 74 px, so they were soft): the shapes of the red cube are split into outline / face / underside / soft shadow, smoothed and recoloured per cube, same look with crisp edges. `parkposter.css`: the endgame appeal chip (`small.eg`) is a lighter red, #F5A99C instead of #F08A7A.

## Addresses by BGA move number, rose endgame appeal chip
`playback.js` `moveHash` / `stepOfHash`: the address of a replay position is BGA's move number: `replay.html?table=930258897#641`; a move that has several steps gets `.1`, `.2`, ... (`#641.1`), and a frame inside a step a third number (`#641.1.2`); trailing zeros are left out. `main.js` writes it (`history.replaceState`) and reads it at the start; links of the old kind (the step index) now mean the move with that number. The Fork button passes the address (`&at=`) so the fork page's link back to the replay opens at the right move (`fork.js`). `parkposter.css`: the endgame appeal chip (`small.eg`) changes hue from salmon #F5A99C to rose #F49FB4, so it differs from the salmon appeal ticket it sits on.

## Victory Column picture: skÃ¤lding03
`data_manual/emu_cup_photos/04-skalding.png` is now skÃ¤lding03.png (requested by the winner). `scripts/build_victory_column_art.py`: `SIGN_X` 0.405 (the face), the cover scale no longer depends on the sign, `SMALL_SHIFT` -105 (negative = the photo moves down; the sky above is the mirrored, blurred top rows) so the face is clear of the income box. The enlarged card has the normal proportions again: its separate art (`S274_VC_large.jpg`), its own layout rules in `mw.css` and `TAB_SCALE` / `LARGE_PHOTO_H` / the `income_box` option are removed; one face art serves both sizes. Thumbnail focus `[0.40, 0.3]`. Rebuilt `S274_VC`.

**Victory Column, enlarged card: winner row.** Only the large card `S274_VC` (`body:not(.compact)` in `scripts/cardgen/site/mw.css`) has a 5 % winner row of its own: photo 50 % + winner row 5 % (the photo element is 55 % = 286 of 520 design px; the caption `.emu-caption` is at top 50 %, height 5 %) + name stripe 10 % + description 35 % (182 px; the other cards 50 / 10 / 40). The face art (`build_victory_column_art.py`, `PHOTO_H` 572) covers the photo element, one image for both sizes.

**Name centring in the stripe (all card types).** Measured on the ink of the names and numbers (screenshots of 57 sample cards with and without the element, both sizes): the numbers are centred within 1 px on all types; the Espera capitals sat above the middle of their line box. `uniform.mjs` now moves the names down: sponsors and endgame cards 2.25 px and projects 1.5 px on the large card, projects 1.75 px on the small card (design px, `position: relative; top`). Animals keep their name + Latin name block (tuned before); the small sponsor / endgame names were within 0.5 px. Rebuilt all cards (the large sponsors, projects and endgame cards and the small projects changed).

**Icons in the sponsor effect texts.** `uniform.mjs`: every `.icon-container` in the effect lists of the sponsors is `vertical-align: middle` (the appeal icon was `text-bottom`, about 4 px (design) too high) and moved up (`top`) until the icon's ink centre is on the middle between the cap top and the baseline of the text: measured on the pixels (text rendered with the icons hidden and the other way round, `scripts/cardgen` probe, not kept): before, the icons sat 1.4 px (appeal, money), 2.75 px (conservation), 1 px (reputation) below that line; now the medians are 0-0.25 px (`top` -2px, conservation -3.3px, reputation -1.6px, strength -0.85px). The digit in the icons lay 0.25-1 px above the icon's centre: `padding-top` of 0.75 px on `.arknova-icon` (conservation 0.9, reputation 0, strength 0.25). Not changed: icons in animal, project and endgame texts. All 83 sponsor cards rebuilt.

**Sponsor strips: tall chips were cut at the top.** `scripts/build_sponsor_strips.py`: the crop starts at y = 790, but the yellow instant-effect chips of Release of Patents (S222), Basic Research (S207), Aquarium, Cable Car, Baboon Rock and Amazon House rise to y = 765-782, so their rounded top edge (and the microscope circle of S222) was cut. A strip with an opaque pixel in its first rows is made again from `TOP_HIGH` = 740 (kept only when the first row of that crop is transparent: on scans where the paper filter fails, the whole crop would become a chip). The six strips are 300 px high (the others 250), the page code is unchanged (bottom anchored, height auto). Cards rebuilt.

**Effect texts next to the instant-effect chip (Okapi Stable S253, Spotted Hyena Compound S252).** BGA shows no text there, so we flow it ourselves: `uniform.mjs` `chipFloat` ({data-id: width}): a left float on the effect list (`::before`, width = the chip's right edge + 10 px, 224 px high, `shape-outside: inset(94px 0 0 0)` so that its top 94 px stay free) makes the lines that would run into the chip start to the right of it, as on the Okapi example; the bullet and the lines above the chip are unchanged. Another card with the same problem needs only its id and width in `chipFloat` (chip right edge: `sponsor_strips/<id>.png`, opaque columns / 2).

## WAZA cards (S227 / S228), large card
- S228: no hard line break in `s228_desc1` (site copy of `common.json`, `scripts/cardgen/site/locales/en/common.json`).
- S227: the enlarged card (not the small one, which keeps the printed chip) is laid out in two columns like BGA (CSS at the end of `site/mw.css`): the instant effect in a yellow panel at the left (texture `scripts/cardgen/site/img/waza_yellow.jpg`, tone of the printed instant chips, about #F5E25B; the bolt `waza_bolt.png` instead of the bullet; the 2- / 4+ card kinds as small icons in the text: `harness/entry.tsx` injects `img.waza-card`, `waza_2minus.png` / `waza_4plus.png`), the passive effect at the right. The card data gives both effects `fontSize: 'sm'`; both use the normal sponsor size (17 px, 25.5 px lines), and S227 is exempt from the shrink step of `shoot.mjs` (the list `['cS227', 'cS215', 'cS218', 'cS270', 'cS222']`).

## Breeding Cooperation (S215) / Breeding Program (S218), large card
- The endgame text had two hard line breaks (site copy of `common.json`, `s215_desc2` / `s218_desc2`), so the build's shrink step zoomed both texts to 0.79. The breaks are gone, both cards are exempt from the shrink step (`shoot.mjs`) and use the normal 17 px with 22 px lines (`site/mw.css`); a 1 px top margin on the first effect keeps the first letters 12 px under the stripe like every other card; the passive text wraps with `text-wrap: pretty` (no lone "icon." line), the endgame text's last word sits left of the printed chip.

## No-snake variants follow the cards
- The no-snake images (`web/cards*/<key>_ns.webp` / `_sw.webp`, `scripts/build_nosnake_images.py`) are copies of the 11 snake cards with another photo. `build_cards.py` remakes them by itself whenever one of those cards (`SNAKE_CARDS`) is drawn again, so they never lag behind.

## Release of Patents (S222), Marine Research Expedition (S270), Publications (S273), Arcade (S281), large card
- S222: the card data says sm (14 px); the text is now 15.5 px with the normal 25.5 px lines (`site/mw.css`), the largest size at which it fits in two lines above the tall printed chip without flowing around it; exempt from the shrink step (`shoot.mjs`).
- S270: one hard line break removed (site copy of `common.json`, `s270_desc1`); its printed strip has no chip and was an opaque scan that hid the end of the text: not shown on the large card; exempt from the shrink step (was 0.79), so the text is the normal 17 px.
- S273: `{ScienceIcon}` was not a token of the fan site's parser and showed as text: now `{ScienceTag}` (the science icon), site copy of `common.json`.
- S281: the redundant "2" before the appeal ticket in `s281_desc2` is removed; two-digit numbers in an appeal ticket (the 10) get `span.icon-num-2` (`harness/entry.tsx`) and are drawn at 0.8 em, 1 px higher (`site/mw.css`).

## Management Plans (P134-P139, Marine Worlds)
- Remodelled after the printed cards (`scripts/cardgen/harness/mw.tsx` + the Management Plan block of `scripts/cardgen/site/mw.css`): three slots of 82 design px in one row (x 17 / 115 / 213, 16 px apart), the outer two on a lighter panel (`.mwp-panel`, 140 px high), the middle one without; in every slot a green cube square (`.project-card-slot-cube-holder.mwp-cube`, 52 x 46) with the conservation shield over its lower edge (all shields on one line) and the reward below (abilities at 2.2x, the reef at 1.25x; the science / reputation reward has a real colon of two dots); the binoculars (Tutor) with a black ring and a black handle; the placement bonus on a tab like the printed card's (58 x 70, attached to the bottom edge, top corners 9 px, icon 1.9x): warm grey (about #b2ab9e) with a faint texture (`scripts/cardgen/site/img/mwp_stone.jpg`, the plain lower part of the grey tab of `img/zoo-card-bonuses.png` at 60 % contrast), darker towards its edges (inset shadow) and a shadow to the right.
- The slots keep the class `project-card-slot` and the cube square the class `project-card-slot-cube-holder`, so `build_cards.py` (`build_strips`) still finds them for the project strips (`web/project_strips/P134-P139.webp`, `cubes.json`); the lighter panels are left out of the strips (`body.bare`).

## Text fixes in the card texts (site copy `scripts/cardgen/site/locales/en/common.json`)
- Endgame cards: Architectural Zoo (F004) "Gain [CP] for building of your zoo." -> "Gain [CP] for the layout of your zoo:" (introduces its list); Designer Zoo (F012) "different shaped buildings" -> "differently shaped buildings"; Naturalists Zoo (F006) "zo0" -> "zoo"; Sponsored Zoo (F008) "sponsors cards" -> "sponsor cards"; Conservation Zoo (F005) full stop after "conservation projects" (inside the bold, `**projects.**`, the parser only bolds whole words); Architectural Zoo (F004) "boarder spaces" -> "border spaces".
- Sponsor Magnet (animal ability, A437 / A441): "sponsors cards" -> "sponsor cards".

## Endgame cards, small card: text and table centred under the stripe
- On the small endgame cards the text sat almost on the name stripe: the body starts LIP (10) design px under the stripe (the stripe's negative margin), so its top padding is now 6 + LIP (`uniform.mjs`, `body.compact ... .scoring-card ... .ark-card-bottom`), and the fitting step of `shoot.mjs` (`fitBody`) leaves at least 13 design px free above and below on the small endgame cards (the gaps of the large card), zooming the body down where needed (0.74-0.96). Measured: about 14-18 image px free under the stripe and the same above the card's lower edge on every small endgame card. The large cards are unchanged.

## Catered Picnic Areas (F015): the kiosk + pavilion pair
- The fan site's `KioskPavilionPair` component relies on Tailwind margins and a 0.5 scale that are not in our build, so the two icons were small and staggered. In the score table's top left cell they are now arranged like on the printed card: kiosk top left, a free-standing plus (two 3.5 px bars, 16 px long) in the middle, pavilion bottom right, in an 88 x 68 box (icons at 21 px, `site/mw.css`, `[data-id^="F015_"] .score-map th`).

## Endgame cards: Diverse Species Zoo / International Zoo centred, conservation icons in the text
- The descriptions of the cards without a score table (F009, F017) began with empty spacer elements of the fan site (`div.mb-4:first-child` and the empty `div.text-sm` after it) that pushed the icon + text down; they are hidden (`site/mw.css`), so the content sits in the middle of the free space on the large and the small card (measured: equal gaps above and below within 2-4 image px). The mb-4 of Architectural Zoo (between the sentence and its list) is not first and stays.
- The conservation icons inside the endgame texts sat 1.75 design px low; `top: -1.75px` (`.scoring-card .project-card-description .icon-container-conservation`) centres them between the cap height and the baseline of the text (measured on the ink, now within 0.25 design px).

## Seahorse on the name stripe of the Marine Worlds-only cards (large card only)
- Cards that exist only in Marine Worlds (source "Marine World" in the fan site's data: A529-A560, S265-S280, F012-F017; and our own P133-P139, also S274 / S274_VC) get the class `mw-only` (`scripts/cardgen/harness/entry.tsx` `MWO`, `harness/mw.tsx`), and `site/mw.css` draws a pale seahorse at the left of the name stripe of the LARGE card, like BGA: `::before` of `.ark-card-middle`, 16 x 28 design px at left 15, vertically centred; the small card (`body.compact`) has none. The picture is `scripts/cardgen/site/img/mw_seahorse.png`: BGA's own seahorse cut from `img/icons-2.webp` (the sprite sheet, row 6 next to the water tile), made white at 80 % opacity. The Marine Worlds variants of base cards (`_MW`) have no seahorse.

## Reveal lightbox: Perception, Scavenging, Hunter, Scuba Dive (`web/js/reveal.js`)

The cards of a reveal-and-keep effect no longer just fly into the hand: they lie large in the middle of the screen on the dimmed window, in the layer of the action card draft (`#draftbox`, `.draftpop`, `.draftcard`, the seal `.draftcheck` of the kept action cards, `.passed` for the cards not kept; extra rules in the last block of `css/parkposter.css`, "the reveal lightbox"). A card the effect cannot take (anything but an animal for Hunter, anything but a sponsor for Scuba Dive) is grey and dark (`.unavailable`) and cannot be chosen; The lightbox is only for the player whose point of view it is (and in a live game only for yourself): what the other player reveals is not seen, so `revealAt` returns nothing for it and that step is shown as a plain step (the `.faceless` card back in the CSS is unused). While the lightbox is on show the revealed cards are not in the hand yet, visually: `frameState()` in `frames.js` returns the state of the step with those cards taken out of the hand (all drawn cards on the draw frame, the kept ones on the keep frame), so the dock and the hand counter in the player's box do not count them; the data and the engine, like BGA, count them as drawn at once, and the frame after the lightbox has them in the hand. The fly-into-the-hand animation after the lightbox closes is not built yet.
- **Replay headline**: "X reveals N cards for <effect>" on the draw frame and on the keep frame alike; the keep frame only adds the seals (kept) and the faded cards (not kept).
- **Effect icon on the headline.** `head(seat, text, effect)` in `reveal.js` returns a `.headrow` (flex, `fit-content`) holding an `<img class="fxicon">` and the `.drafthead` ribbon: the effect's icon tile (`FX_ICON`: Perception `r7c10`, Hunter `r7c7`, Scavenging `r4c10`, Scuba Dive `r4c3` = the sheet's `diving`) is pinned over the ribbon's left tip, 84 px, tilted -6 deg, gold-rimmed with drop shadows (last block of `parkposter.css`). It is a sibling of the ribbon, not a child, because the ribbon's `clip-path` would cut it; the ribbon gets `padding-left: 78px`. It fades in with the ribbon (`lbIcon`, same delay, `--el`). Used by both the replay reveal and `forkReveal`; the action card draft headline has no icon.
- **Action card draft headline.** `draftBar` (`action-bar.js`) always writes "Action card draft" in `.drafthead.actionhead` (no player name in the text, it is the tooltip; the former "must select ..." / "selected ..." wording is gone for all draft steps, replay and fork). The ribbon is the reveal ribbon in harbour teal `#1f5f6b` with the same gold edges and cream text (last rule of `parkposter.css`); font, size, shape, spotlight and cards are unchanged.
- **Replay**: a reveal is two consecutive steps of the log and needs no engine state: "X draws A, B, C for <perception | hunter | scuba dive | scavenging> effect" (the cards are the ones the hand of X gained in that step) and the next step "X keeps ... and discard ..." / "X discards ... (no animal | no sponsor)". `revealAt(i)` finds them; `frames.js` gives both steps the frame kind `reveal` (like `draft`: `main.js` keeps the step text in the bar above the lightbox, `action-bar.js` draws the lightbox with the Back / Next buttons of the draft). The first frame shows the cards, the second the kept ones with the seal and the others faded.
- **Fork / sandbox / live game**: `forkReveal(o, acts, play)` draws the lightbox from the engine's pending effect: `options.effects` of kind `reveal` (Perception / Hunter / Scuba Dive: `filter` any / animal / sponsor, `n` cards kept, 1 when absent) or `scavenge`, with `cards` (the revealed cards: `replay/options.py` `_revealed_cards` = the top `x` cards of the deck, or the cards drawn from the discard pile; the live `decision` carries them for the acting seat only). Click the cards to keep (as many as `n`; another click moves the choice), the chosen ones carry the seal, then Confirm plays the matching `choose_effect` action (`args.keep`); an optional effect (Hunter Animals) has a Skip button (`skip_effect`); when nothing can be kept the button says "Keep nothing". The layer is cleared at the start of every `actionBar()` call and drawn again while the effect is pending.

### Lightbox look "Spotlight" (action draft + the four reveals)
One look for every `#draftbox` use, in the last block of `web/css/parkposter.css` (all rules `body.viewer ...`): no panel and no frame, the cards lie in a warm radial spotlight (`.draftbox` background) with an ink outline and a drop shadow, the picked card gets the orange (`--sun`) outline and the orange star seal (they were gold for a while and went back to the orange of the "Ticket frame" / "Sun seal" above), Back / Next are gold round buttons with a paper label, the Confirm / Skip buttons are Bowlby One pills (orange / ink). The headline (`.drafthead`: player name + sentence, for the draft and for every reveal) sits on an ink (`--ink`) ribbon with gold top and bottom edges and notched ends (`clip-path`, Bowlby One 30 px, cream text, the player name in gold; the seat colour is not used there). The earlier look (cream panel, Park Label heading) and the alternatives that were tried (banner stage, other headline styles, orange / clay / other coloured ribbons, a shelf under the cards) are gone.

### Opening animation of the lightbox (`openFx` in `web/js/reveal.js`, last block of `parkposter.css`)
When the lightbox (action card draft, the four reveals) opens it plays for about 1.7 s: the dim fades in (0.35 s), the cards fall from above one after another (0.1 s apart, a small bounce when they land), the ribbon settles after them (fade in from 110 % size, no rotation: deliberately not a hard stamp), Back / Next and the Confirm / Skip buttons fade in. The dim is now the `::before` layer of `.draftbox` (so it can fade on its own; the box has `isolation: isolate`). `actionBar()` (action-bar.js) calls `noteLightbox()` before it clears the layer and `openFx(box)` after the cards are in (`reveal.js` `open()` and the draft path): the class `lbopen` is added only when the lightbox was not open on the frame before, so Back / Next between two frames of a reveal and re-draws (selecting a card in the fork) do not replay it; a redraw within the 1.7 s carries on where it was (`--el` = seconds already played, a negative `animation-delay`; `--d` = number of the card). Fill mode is `backwards` so hover rules of the cards still work afterwards. `prefers-reduced-motion` switches it off. The animation of the kept cards flying into the hand (and the others being discarded) is the next step and not built.

### Kept cards are dealt into the hand (`web/js/handfly.js`)
When the lightbox of a reveal (Perception, Scavenging, Hunter, Scuba Dive) closes, the lightbox and every card in it vanish at once (no flight from the large card to the small one: that looked awkward), and the kept cards are dealt into the hand like the starting hand (the same numbers and keyframes as `dockIntro` in `dock.js`): in the floating tray they drop in from above one after the other (`DROP_MS` 560, gap 100 ms, alternate tilt), in the fan they rise from below the screen edge and settle into their tilt (`DEAL_MS` 520, gap 110 ms). Only the dock cards that match the kept cards are animated; the others stay where the new layout puts them. `setPending()` (called by `noteLightbox()` in `reveal.js` before the layer is cleared) remembers the kept keys of a lightbox that is on show; `afterRender()` (end of `render()` in `main.js`) plays the deal when the lightbox has just closed and the dock has those cards. Not played for reduced motion or when going backwards. Nothing changes in the data.


### Player choice dialog "Split stage" (`askSpectated` in `web/js/pov.js`, last-but-one block of `web/css/parkposter.css`, replaces the cream box of sections above)
The first-visit dialog "Choose which player to spectate" is no longer a cream box with two chips: the whole window is cut in two halves, one `button.povhalf` per player (left half seat 0, right half seat 1), each tinted with the player's colour (`color-mix(var(--pc) 62%, transparent)` over the dimmed board, the same radial spotlight dim as the lightbox), the name in Bowlby One 54 px with an ink shadow, no icon. A gold notched ribbon `.povrib` (the shape of the lightbox ribbons, gold with ink text and ink edges) carries the title at the top, an ink divider `.povdiv` between the halves, the orange Confirm pill `.povok` at the bottom (grey while nothing is picked). Picking a half only selects it (`.on`: gold inner frame, `.povbox.chosen` darkens and desaturates the other half); Confirm or Enter applies it, as before. `#povChoice` is the id the draft waits behind (`body:has(#povChoice) .draftbox`). The old `.povchip`, `.povdot`, `.povchips` and the `modalbox` wrapper are gone.

### Settings: drawer is the default, fixed top edge (`web/js/settings.js`, `state.js`, `zoo.js`, last block of `css/parkposter.css`)
- **Played cards default** is now "Slide-out drawer" (first entry of `PLAYED_MODES`, listed above "Below the map"): `S.playedMode` starts as `'drawer'` (`state.js`), an unknown or missing stored value gives `'drawer'`, and every fallback that used to be `'below'` (the tray without the floating hand, "beside" outside the side layout: `settings.js` three places, `zoo.js` `playedMode()`) is `'drawer'` too. A stored explicit choice is kept; mini games still use `'below'`.
- **Settings panel position.** `#settingsModal` no longer centres its box vertically (`align-items: flex-start; padding-top: 14vh`): the top edge and the tabs stay put, and the box grows or shrinks downwards when a tab has more or fewer settings. The panels no longer keep the height of the tallest tab (`.setpanel { min-height: 0 }`).

### Settings panel (`web/js/settings.js`, last block of `web/css/parkposter.css`)
The settings pop-up is a cream panel (690 px, horizontally centred, ink frame, hard ink shadow) under a fixed deep teal-grey header (`#2F4A4F`, never changes with the tab). The title "Settings" is centred (Bowlby One 32 px); the close button is absolutely placed at the right, vertically on the title's centre line, cream with an ink cross (not orange: orange is the Control tab colour). The three tab pills straddle the header's lower edge (`.settabs` has `margin-top: -28px`, the header `padding-bottom: 38px`), so the header itself separates tabs from rows. The active tab colour is set by `box.dataset.tab` (`showTab`) through `--tc`: Control orange (`--sun`), Board green `#3F9A4E`, Cards blue `#2d6cb5`; it drives the active tab pill, the "on" switches and the filled slider track. Every row is exactly 74 px high (`.setrow`), starts with a pastel icon chip (`chip(k)` / `ICONS` in `settings.js`, `.setico` 42 px) and is separated by dashed lines. Controls: dropdowns 330 x 48 pills with an ink chevron (see below), switches 66 x 38 (knob turns cream with an ink ring when on), slider track 330 px filled up to the knob (`--f` set on `.settrack` by `knobSlider`'s `showVal`) with an ink knob carrying the value. Dropdowns: the settings use no native `<select>` list (Chrome's list scrolls and cannot be styled). `dropdown(sel)` in `settings.js` keeps the `<select>` hidden as the model (options, `value`, `onchange`; a `sync` event redraws the label after a programmatic change, e.g. `syncPlayedOptions`) and draws a `button.setselect` plus a `.setmenu` list (cream card, ink frame, `.setopt` rows 50 px, selected and hovered ones filled with the active tab colour `--tc` (copied onto the menu in `open`) and cream text as full-width bands (menu has no padding, `overflow: hidden`, entries 50 px, square); while the mouse is inside the list only the hovered entry is filled (`.setmenu:hover .setopt:not(:hover)`), like the browser list but in tab colours, disabled ones dimmed) as a child of `#settingsModal`, positioned from the button's screen rectangle divided by the viewer zoom, opening upward when there is no room below. It never scrolls (all lists have 2-3 entries); outside click closes it, arrows move, Esc closes only the list. The sandbox and the play lobby still use native selects.
Icons: the Hand display row shows a raised hand with five fingers (lucide 'hand', `ICONS.hand`, thinner stroke via `.ico-hand`); each chip gets the class `ico-<key>` so single icons can be sized (`.ico-panda` 28 px, `.ico-hand` 26 px). The No-snake mode row uses a drawn panda face (`ICONS.panda`, community inside joke; an icon entry may carry a third element, extra filled shapes drawn in ink). Help: the "Map placement preview" row has a `?` chip (`.qm`: sand with an ink glyph, ink when hovered, default cursor) after its label that shows `.pvnote`, a gold tilted sticky note (burgundy tape strip, title and bullets, colours are the `--nbg/--ntape/--nink/--nacc` variables) with a centred title and two bullets. Its tape touches the `?`: `showNote` measures the `?` in screen pixels, divides by the viewer zoom and positions the note in `#settingsModal` (the box clips its overflow, so the note is a sibling of the box). The old browser `title` tooltip of that row is gone.
The block overrides the older settings rules; the panel keeps its fixed top edge from the section above.


### Reef Dweller icon on animal cards (`scripts/cardgen/uniform.mjs`, cards A529 A531 A533 A535 A536 A541 A542 A544)
The coral icon in front of an ability of a Marine Worlds animal (`isReefDweller` in `Ability.tsx`) hung above the name's line (the fan site's `-ml-1 -mt-1` pulled it up, its box stretched over the whole text column) and nearly touched the text. `uniform.mjs` now gives the icon's box the height of the ability name's line (large card 28.5 px, small card 48 px with the name's 10 px top margin), centres the icon in it (`align-items: center`, `align-self: flex-start`), puts a clear gap before the text (`REEF_GAP`) and fine-tunes the vertical position per size (`REEF_TOP` / `REEF_TOP_S`, measured on the ink of the icon against the ink of the name in both finished images: centres within 0.5 px). Only these 8 cards show the icon; their `web/cards` and `web/cards_large` images were rebuilt with `python scripts/build_cards.py --only A529,A531,...` (set `CHROMIUM_PATH` where Playwright has no matching browser); every other card image is unchanged.

### Enclosure gap and borders (`drawBuilding` in `web/js/board.js`, setting "Show enclosure borders" in `web/js/settings.js`)
Adjacent buildings run into each other because the sprites have no edge.
