Every building sprite is drawn inside an SVG `<mask>` made of its hexes (white, plus a 1.5 px stroke so no seam shows inside a multi-hex building) minus a black stroke of its outline (`outlinePath`, the edges that have no neighbour of the same building) that is `2 * ENC_GAP` wide with round caps: the outer `ENC_GAP` = 3 px of the building are cut off, so a thin channel of the map shows between neighbours. The gap is always on. The setting "Show enclosure borders" only adds the border: off (default) = gap only, on = gap plus border (`S.encBorders`, saved as `encBorders` in `localStorage.settings`, default off, Board tab, hexagon icon, event `encborders` redraws the board) the same outline is stroked again (only then), `ENC_BORDER` = 8 px wide in `#A68731` (measured on BGA's border, which is that golden brown), clipped to the building's hexes (`<clipPath>`) and inside the same mask, so the border shows as an 8 px band along the outer edge only (no lines between the hexes of one building), joined at the corners (`stroke-linejoin` / `linecap` round). Mask and clip ids come from the module counter `maskCount` and must stay unique in the whole page: the two zoos are drawn by separate calls, and with a per-call counter the second zoo's `url(#bm1)` resolved to the first zoo's mask and its buildings vanished. The gap and border apply to every call of `drawBuilding` (placed, changed and gone buildings, the fork placement ghost, the preview).

### Unique pattern id of the association strip (`associationStrip` in `web/js/association.js`)
The grid pattern of the strip had the fixed id `stripgrid`, so with two zoos on the page there were two elements with one id and `url(#stripgrid)` of the second strip pointed at the first one's pattern. The id is now `stripgrid<n>` from the module counter `stripCount`.

## 115. Draft lightbox toggle, forks see both hands, features restored after the polish merge
- The action card draft lightbox of the replay has a button (`.drafthide`, under the panel) that hides it and brings it back (`S.draftHidden`, class `lbhide` on `#draftbox`): the dim covered the sidebar, the Fork button included.
- A fork and the sandbox play both seats: `pov.js` `seesAll()` makes `hides()` / `povState()` and the dock's seat lock ignore the eye there. Before, a fork showed only the eye's player's hand and draft offers, so after the first pick of the draft (or whenever the other seat had to choose a card) no control was left to play.
- The frontend polish merge (PR 6, a copy of the frontend from before 2026-10-14..31) overwrote parts of other work in `action-bar.js` (Hypnosis cards, Multiplier icon and `multiplierMode`, Marine Research Expedition `effectOption` buttons), `board.js` (H hexes of maps 8 / 8a), `state.js` (Ark360 study state), `playback.js` (`ownChoice`) and `frames.js` (`verb`): restored by a three-way merge of the original commits onto the new files (the new code of the polish was kept where the two touched the same lines).

## 116. Base projects and 5 / 8 / 16 bonuses hidden during the draft

web/js/shared.js (draftOn): while the action card draft is running (state phase setup with a draft that is not done, and step 0 of a replay) the three base project slots are drawn empty and the bonus tiles on the 5 / 8 conservation spaces and at 16 reputation (Marine Worlds) are not drawn. They appear when the draft box closes. Forks and live games use the same rule; the sandbox (no draft) is unaffected.

## 117. Fork: swap between the seats' hands and endgame cards in the tray

web/js/dock.js (drawTray): the round buttons on the tray (setting Hand display = Floating container) now cover every seat that is not hidden (hides): hand + endgame cards (+ stored / played) of each, in the seat's colour, so a fork and the sandbox can switch between the seats. A replay with an eye open still shows only that player's buttons. The fan already had one group of buttons per seat.

## 118. Fork: the enclosure to play an animal into can be clicked anywhere on it

web/js/board.js (zooBoard): the click target of an enclosure offered to the selected animal (.encpick) was the outline path, which is only loose edge segments and has no area, so only the 4 px border could be clicked (a puffer could not be put into a large aquarium). The target is now a closed path of the building's hexes (illPath, no stroke) and the border is drawn apart from it (pointer-events none); both are appended after all buildings so no sprite covers them.

## 119. Live game: own dock only, page scrolls clear of the tray, action order at the starting hand

- web/js/dock.js: in a live game (PLAY, seated) the dock (fan and tray) has only the player's own hand / endgame (/ stored / played) buttons; the opponent's group is not drawn (hides wraps the point of view's hides). A spectator still sees both. The tray branch of 
enderDock now calls itDockSpace (it returned before it), and itDockSpace also measures the dock's children, so the page has room under the zoos and the whole map can be scrolled clear of the tray.
- web/js/action-bar.js (draftOpen): a live game or fork has no frames, so after the last keep the state is the starting hand selection, no longer the 'draft closing' frame; rameState therefore no longer sets ctions_hidden and the drawn action card order is visible while the starting hand is chosen.

## 120. Replay: the starting-hand draws are log-only

- `replay/view.py` (`_split_keep_and_draw`): the two steps "X draws 8 cards and 2 endgame cards" (one per player, after the last keep of the action card draft) carry `log_only: true`: they stay in the data and in the log (move list), and `go()`, the timeline and the jump box still reach them; stepping skips the other player's one (see below).
- `web/js/playback.js` (`skipFrame`): a frame of a step with `log_only` is skipped by `stepBy` (arrow keys, buttons, autoplay) when it is the OTHER player's draw (the label does not start with the followed player's name); the followed player's own draw stays, because it is the step that shows the 8 starting cards before the discard (skipping both made the replay jump from the draft to the 4 kept cards). With both hands open (`S.pov === null`) both draws are shown. So the second player no longer steps through the opponent's hidden draw first, but still sees their own hand dealt.

## 121. Layout: the display's fit no longer jitters from step to step

- `web/js/layout.js` (`fitDisplay`): the association width (`--assocw`) and the zoom of the display box are computed from measured sizes, which differ by a hair from one step to the next (fonts, the content of the display). An icon close to a half pixel then flipped by one pixel on every step (seen on a tester's screen: the X token under reputation 12). Now the last values are kept (`lastFit`) while the window and the column layout are the same (`key` = `S.scale`, `S.scaled`, the width of `#shared`, the number of grid columns) and a new value differs only by noise: at most `FIT_TOL_W` = 1 design px for the width, `FIT_TOL_ZOOM` = 0.25 % for the zoom (`steady`). A resize, another layout or a larger change of the content is applied as before. Over a whole replay at 1670 x 940 and 1366 x 768 there is now one zoom value instead of several; the display and the association element still end at the same height (within 0.2 px).

## 122. The special enclosure bonus: hex outlines that fill the pentagon

- `web/js/association.js` (`slotGlyph`, bonus type `special-enclosure`, the choice of a large bird aviary / reptile house, + large aquarium in Marine Worlds; shown in the association strip and the bonus panel of a zoo): the three small sprites (`r9c9`, `r9c11`, `r8c14`, cut from a sheet, the aquarium truncated) and the slashes are gone. The buildings are drawn as vector polygons (`SPECIAL_ENCLOSURE` for the base game, `SPECIAL_ENCLOSURE_MW` for Marine Worlds, chosen by `S.replay.marine_worlds`): brown `#6b4a22` for the aviary and the reptile house, blue `#1b8fc4` for the aquarium, white edge 1.1 (round joins), in the 72 x 71 box of the pentagon picture (the `<g>` is translated to the pentagon's corner `74, y - 36`).
- The outlines are the real building shapes: each is 5 flat-top hexes (fitted to the enclosure pictures in `web/enclosures`, union of the hexes, so the edges are straight), turned and nested by a packing search so that they use the whole safe area of the pentagon (the yellow part, 1.8 px in from its border) with a gap of 1.6 px: base game = aviary above the reptile house on the centre line, both turned 180 degrees, 34 px wide each; Marine Worlds = three shapes in a loose triangle (aviary right, reptile house upper left, aquarium lower left), 30 px wide. The coordinates are final (18 / 20 points per shape); to change the layout, redo the packing and replace the two arrays.

## 123. Association strip: the worker bonus uses the reputation track's picture

- `web/js/association.js` (`slotGlyph`, bonus type `Worker` on a pentagon space; not on a purple income square): the worker is drawn with the HD picture of the reputation track and the conservation track (`/icons_hd/Worker.webp`, its box from `BONUS_HD.icons.Worker` in `bonus_hd_data.js`, scaled from the track's 80-high pentagon to the 71-high pentagon of the strip) instead of the sprite `r5c13` at 50 px, which almost filled the pentagon. The picture is about 14 % smaller and sits with the same margins as in the track.

## 124. Move bar: the action card buttons are smaller

- The five action card buttons of the move bar (`.abtn.acbtn`; the class is set in `action-bar.js` for the turn-start choice and for `actionBadge`, in `fork.js` for the Multiplier choice) are 31 px high (`min-height: 1.52em` at 17 px, padding 2 px 8 px, icon 22 px; were 41 px high with a 26 px icon), so they are as high as the chosen card's tile in the log line (30 px, `.acticon`). The variant badge on them is 21 px (`parkposter.css`, `.movepill .abtn.acbtn .variant`). The other buttons of the bar (X tokens, `gen`, `deckbtn`, `gainx`) keep the 41 px of `.movepill .abtn`. The chosen card's icon and the strength tile are unchanged.

## 125. Player box: placeholders for the action cards during the draft

- Before the action cards are shuffled (`st.actions_hidden`, the draft and the frame that deals the starting hand) the `.ppactions` row of the player box is no longer invisible. `side-panel.js` draws slot 1 as the real Animals card (the same code path as every card: `pic(ACTION_ICON.animals, 40)` in `.ac`, side I, no variant badge) and slots 2 to 5 as `.ac.ghost` (`parkposter.css`: 38 px content + 4 px dashed border = the 46 px of a real card, transparent, the slot number in Bowlby One 24 px, ink at 38 %). The old `.ppactions.unseen { visibility: hidden }` rule and the class are removed.

## 126. Draft and reveal lightboxes: Back / Next use the control box's arrows

- `draftNav()` in `action-bar.js` (the Back / Next buttons at the sides of the action card draft lightbox and of the Perception / Scavenging / Hunter / Scuba Dive reveal lightboxes, `reveal.js` gets the same builder) draws the double arrows of the control box's Back / Forward buttons (the paths of `#prev` / `#next` in `replay.html`, 20 x 20 viewBox, `svg.dnglyph`, filled ink, 36 px) instead of the chevrons. The gold circle stays. The BACK / NEXT words and the arrow key caps are only drawn in the action card draft (`draftNav(d, labels)`; `reveal.js` calls it with `labels = false`). `centerNav(wrap)` in `reveal.js` (called for both lightboxes, again by a ResizeObserver) shifts the buttons with `--ny` (`.draftnav { position: relative; top: var(--ny) }`) so that the middle of each button element (button + words) is the middle of the rows of cards, not of the panel with its title ribbon; it measures with `offsetTop` (the cards fly in with a transform), so `.draftwrap` is `position: relative`.

## 127. Reveal lightboxes (replay): the cards can be highlighted with a click

- `showReveal()` in `reveal.js` (Perception, Scavenging, Hunter, Scuba Dive in the replay) makes every card clickable like the cards of the action card draft: a click toggles `.marked` (the green tag, `parkposter.css`), kept in `draftMarks` (`action-bar.js`, key `r:<seat>:<card>`, cleared by `go()` at every step). Cards the effect cannot take (greyed out: anything but an animal for Hunter, anything but a sponsor for Scuba Dive) cannot be highlighted and keep the not-allowed cursor. The pop-up has the class `.replayreveal`. Fork and live reveals (`forkReveal`) are unchanged: there a click chooses.

## 128. Lightbox opening: the ribbon comes in earlier

- In the opening animation of the draft and reveal lightboxes (`.draftbox.lbopen`, `parkposter.css`) the ribbon (`.drafthead`) and the effect icon (`.fxicon`) start at 0.45 s and take 0.25 s (were 0.95 s / 0.4 s, after the cards had landed): they appear as the first card touches down, while the others are still falling. Cards (0.1 s + 0.1 s per card, 0.75 s), dim and Back / Next are unchanged; `FX_MS` (1.7 s) in `reveal.js` still covers the whole animation.

## 129. Resistance spotlight (replay)

- The endgame cards of a Resistance effect (elephants) use the reveal lightbox of Perception (`reveal.js`, effect `resistance`, icon = the hourglass of the endgame cards, sheet id `r7c2`, headline "X reveals 2 endgame cards for Resistance"). Only the followed player's effect is shown (`hides(seat)`; with no point of view both seats' lightboxes show, as for the other reveals).
- Data: the endgame cards are in no step state until one is kept (the discarded one never is), so `view.py` (`_resistance_reveal`, called per step in the move loop) puts `step.resistance` = `{seat, cards}` on the draw step and `{seat, cards, kept}` on the keep step; the card keys are added to `cards` (the catalog). `revealAt()` reads it before the label-based reveals.
- Frames (`frames.js`): the draw step is one `reveal` frame; the keep step has two frames, `reveal` (the chosen card sealed, the other faded, the card NOT yet among the endgame cards: `frameState` removes it from `endgame_hand`, `endgame_card_lines` and `endgame_cards` of that seat in a virtual state) and then a plain `text` frame with the lightbox closed and the card added. One log line, two frames, so Back / Next steps through both.
- The effect icon of Resistance is `web/icons/hourglass.svg` (sharp, redrawn as a vector, own proportions): `head()` in `reveal.js` gives it the class `tall` (`.fxicon.tall`, height 92 px, width auto) instead of the square of the other icons.
- Live points on the cards: `step.resistance.points` = `{card key: {conservation, appeal}}` (`_card_live` in `view.py`, `endgame.card_points` for the state of the step; `appeal` is always 0 today, the slot is there for endgame cards that score appeal). `face(key, hidden, points)` in `reveal.js` draws the strip `.egpts` across the picture of the card (`parkposter.css`, last block; a translucent stone band, `rgba(217, 214, 206, .75)`, the number in ink 52 px beside the icon of the kind, 38 px): conservation always (0 too), appeal only when not 0. Nothing else (no tier icons). Other reveals pass no points and show no pill.
- Hide / Show button on every replay reveal lightbox (`showReveal()` in `reveal.js`): the same button and CSS as the action card draft (`.drafthide`, `.draftbox.lbhide`), labelled "Hide Cards" / "Show Cards". Its state is `S.revealHidden` (own flag, not the draft's `S.draftHidden`); it stays while stepping between frames, the button always remains so the cards can be brought back. `lbhide` is cleared at every render (`action-bar.js`) and set again by `showReveal`. Not in the fork / live game reveal (`forkReveal`).
- Live count on the endgame cards of the hand: the small cards of the dock's endgame tab (`dock.js`, cards of the row `kind === 'endgame'`) carry the same strip (`#dock .card .egpts`, smaller numbers, last block of `parkposter.css`); the enlarged hover preview is a separate picture and has none. The strip code moved to `web/js/egpoints.js` (`pointsBadge`), shared with `reveal.js`. Data: `player.endgame_card_pts` = `{card key: {conservation, appeal}}` for every card in `endgame_hand` (`state_view`, also the card that would be discarded; empty once the final scoring has begun); the live projection empties it for the other seat (`live/projection.py`). The zoo's "Endgame cards" section of the mini games has no strip.
- The Hide / Show button of the lightboxes (`.drafthide`, `parkposter.css`; the action card draft and every reveal) is restyled so that it is seen over the bright boards too: big gold pill (21 px label, 4 px ink outline, hard ink shadow) with an eye icon (`::before`, ink). Resistance words it "Hide endgame selection" / "Show endgame selection" (`showReveal` in `reveal.js`); the other reveals keep "Hide Cards" / "Show Cards", the draft "Hide Draft" / "Show Draft".
- Hide / Show button position: `bottom` 42 px (was 22). The button is always under the lightbox, so `.draftbox` has `padding-bottom: 110px` (last lines of `parkposter.css`): the draft / reveal panel is centred in the room above the button, not in the whole window (the layer is still fixed, full window, flex centred).
- `.drafthide` has `word-spacing: .20em` (the Park Label font sets the words of "HIDE ENDGAME SELECTION" too close together); one rule, so every lightbox button gets it.

## 130. Endgame scoring-card discard: the dock follows (replay)

- `web/js/egdiscard.js` (new): `endgameDiscardAt(i, seat)` says whether step `i` lies inside the scoring-card discard of the followed player. A window starts at the step whose label matches `triggers scoring card discard` and ends at the step where that player's own label starts with `<name> discards ` and contains `(scoring card)` (searched within 14 steps; the opponent's discard in between belongs to the window). A player with a single endgame card has no own discard line, so no window. Cached per replay and seat.
- `web/js/dock.js`, `autoEndgame()` (called first in `renderDock`; replay only, off in fork / sandbox / play / mini games and with no POV): on entering a window the dock switches to the POV player's endgame cards and remembers the previous selection in `S.autoEg` (`prev` is null when that view was already shown, so nothing is switched back). One step after the window the previous selection is restored, unless the viewer changed the dock by hand meanwhile. Nothing is saved (`saveDock` is not called), so the user's own dock setting is never overwritten.

## 131. Runs of draws are one step for the other player (replay)

- BGA logs the draws of an upgraded Cards action one per move. `replay/merge.py` `mark_merges(steps, names)` (called in `replay/view.py` after the step indices are set) finds the runs of draws of one player ("X draws N card(s) from the deck", the count from the hidden `label_pov` text) and gives every step of a run `merge = {seat, kind: "draw", id, last, from}`; the last step also has `merge.label` ("X draws 4 cards from the deck", the counts added) and the same text in `label_pov[1 - seat]`. The steps stay in the list (indices, forks, engine states unchanged).
- Only draws. Digs, snaps and the takes in the reputation range are never merged (they change the display, which is replenished step by step; the money of a mark is a step of its own). A draw in the same BGA move right after a dig from the hand (Digging Cards: "X digs 1 card from their hand", then "X draws 1 card from the deck") is not part of a run (`_after_dig`): game 800966139 move 232 stays two steps, the draws of moves 233-236 are one ("draws 4 cards").
- Client (`pov.js` `mergedAway(step)`, `mergedFrom(step)`): only for a viewer who follows the OTHER player (`S.pov !== null`, not the actor's seat, not fork / sandbox / play). The actor's own point of view and "all" keep every step. The steps of the run before the last are skipped by the keys / buttons / autoplay (`playback.js` `skipFrame`), hidden in the Log (`buildMoveList`: `display: none`), a jump (`go`, `stepOfMove`) into one leads to the end of the run, and the number of the last step is the run's first move (`moveNumbers`: `show`, `cur`). Changing the eye fires `povapplied` (`main.js` builds the Log again and calls `go(S.step, S.phase)`).
- Tests: `tests/test_move_numbers.py` `test_merge_runs_unit`. Examples: 760993552 move 285 (4 draws, seen from hardyzhao), 800966139 move 233 (4 draws after two Digging Cards digs).

## 132. X token in the Log and the move bar: height and the single token

- `.labelamount.labelx` (the X token with a number, `log-labels.js` `labelIcon`) is as high as the money icon: `--ah` 1.3em in the Log, 1.38em in the move bar (it was 1.42 / 1.5em and looked too large next to the other icons). Rules at the end of the `.labelamount` block of `parkposter.css`.
- A single X token (`<XTOKEN:1>`, "pays 1 xtoken", "gains 1 xtoken", a bonus of 1) shows the plain icon without the 1 (`labelIcon`: the amount is dropped, the picture is `S.iconNames.xtoken` with the perforation dots, class `labelxplain` only without a number, same height; `--lh` is HALF the icon height, .65em in the Log and .69em in the bar, it puts the icon's middle on the text's middle: measured with `getBoundingClientRect`, the offset to the text is 0.1 px in both places); 2 and more keep the icon with the number on it (`r5c6_ns`).
- Live count on the endgame cards of the hand stays after the scoring: `state_view` (`replay/view.py`) fills `endgame_card_pts` in every phase (it was empty in `scoring` / `over`), so the small numbers on the dock's endgame cards are still there on the last steps (they then show what each card scored).

## 133. Replay: no stale "take" greying while the break has not started in the log

- Bug (game 941298700, move 91): Science Lab has an immediate take AND an income take in the break. After the player's take, the engine ends the action and starts the break inside it, so the options of the step ("what is pending next") held the income take of the break, with the range computed from the display as it is after the break (one card, which is another card than the one in the first slot of the display the step shows). The display then greyed everything but that card. `replay/options.py` `step_options` now sets `in_break` when the pending effects resume into a break (`resume.kind == "break"`); `replay/view.py` drops `take` from the options of a step whose log state is not in the break yet (`turn_context.phase != "break"`). Steps inside the break keep their take options.

## 134. The break sentence is gone from the log texts

- BGA's line that moves the break token onto the last space ends with "At the end of the turn, all players must take a break". `render_log` (`replay/view.py`, `_BREAK_SENTENCE`) cuts the sentence from every text it renders, so the replay shows "X advances break token of 5 space(s) and reach the last space of the Break track". The live game log (`live/bgalog.py`) never had it; fork and sandbox texts come from the same renderers.

## 135. Info box chips: no tile tooltip, the appeal line only when it can score

- The income chip (`+26`) and the endgame live-count pills (`+X`) get `title = ''` (`side-panel.js`), so the tile's own tooltip ("Money", "Appeal", "Conservation points") does not show while the mouse is on the chip; only the slip does. An empty `title` on a child switches off the tooltip inherited from the ancestor; `notips.js` leaves it alone (the chip has text).
- Appeal endgame chip, hidden hand: the grey "Endgame card (hidden) ?" line is only shown when an endgame card can score appeal. `replay/view.py` `_endgame_has_appeal()` (cached) looks for an appeal key in `endgames.json`'s `scoreArray` (none today) and sets `p.eg_appeal`; `side-panel.js` passes the hidden count to the appeal slip only if it is true. When such a card is added to the data the line comes back by itself (the key name must contain "appeal"; check `_card_live`, which has an `appeal` slot, and `egpoints.js`).

## 136. Info box: the score is an ink chip, the waiting player has a faded name

- Score: the old flip board (one plate per digit, `.flip .ft .fc .ff`) is replaced by `.chip` (`web/js/side-panel.js` appends `<b class="chip">` with the number to `.ppscore`; `parkposter.css`): a rounded rectangle (36 px high, radius 9, a 2 px light inner rim) with the number in white Park Num 24 px. It is as wide as its digits need (`min-width: 36px`, 7 px side padding): the type size and the shape never change for three characters (112, -14, -105). No animation (the flip swing, `shownScore`, `flipScore` are gone); the `flash-up` / `flash-down` tint of the `.ppscore` span stays. Tried and dropped: a ring that fills to 100, an ink chip (invisible on a black header).
- Chip colour: the player colour darkened (`color-mix(in srgb, var(--pc) 60%, #000)`: 45 % looked too dark on blue, rim `rgba(255, 255, 255, .35)`). Three colours get their own chip, chosen by `chipTone(hex)` in `side-panel.js` (a class on the `.pp` box): `chip-black` (max channel < 16 %: `#14201F`, rim .5; a darkened black is black), `chip-amber` (a light colour with hue 20-45: orange, `#5A2E00`, rim `rgba(255, 200, 150, .5)`) and `chip-gold` (a light colour with hue 45-75: yellow, `#7A6600`, rim `rgba(255, 246, 170, .55)`; a darkened yellow turns olive green). BGA's white (shown grey `#767c82`) and grey use the darkened colour. Player colours seen in the sample logs: `#1863a5 #b91b1b #ffffff #000000 #30a638 #d1c81c #c028d3 #5a5856`; orange is not among them (`#ee8a2b` was used to test).
- Space: at the 300 px sidebar the name has about 245 px next to a two-digit chip and 230 px next to a three-digit one (after the 8 px gap; measured with the 38 px chip: 233 / 218). At the full 20 px size that holds 17 lowercase letters, most 15-17 character names, but not an all-capitals 17-character name; `squeezeName` still shrinks such a name (down to 16 px, then an ellipsis) as before.
- Turn marker: the glowing gold line under the header (`.pphead.on`, `pp-turn-glow`, `pp-turn-glow-light`) is removed and nothing replaces it yet. A faded name for the player who waits was tried and dropped: the name stays untouched, and there is no room for a dot before it. The `on` class of `.pphead` and `active` of `.pp` stay (no style).

## 137. Bonus spaces: university and partner zoo share one fit, animal magnet fits the pentagon, snapping 2 px bigger

- University (`Fac`: placement bonus on maps 6 / 6a, bonus space of the association panel on 4 / 4a) and partner zoo (`Partner-Zoo`: placement bonus on 2 / 2a, bonus space on 8 / 8a) are one drawing in both places: `FIT_H` and `FIT_DY` in `web/js/icons.js` (icon height = 0.54 / 0.62 x the height of the pentagon, as big as fits without touching the border; centred 0.02 x its height below the middle because the pentagon's mass lies low). `board.js` (pentagon 80 px high) and `slotGlyph` in `association.js` (pentagon 71 px, `PENT_H`) both use them, so the two look the same. Before, the board drew them at 48 px (off-centre, the university grazed the pentagon's lower edge) and the panel at 50 px (overflowed). The yellow spaces only: a purple square keeps its sizes.
- The same fit on the HD pictures of the tracks (5 / 8 conservation random bonuses, 16 reputation): `fitted()` in `bonusart.js` replaces the box of `Partner-Zoo` / `Fac` by one of height 80 x `FIT_H`, centred at 40 + 80 x `FIT_DY` (aspect and centre x kept), so all four places (board hex, association space, strip under the zoo, track tile) show the two icons at the same share of the pentagon.
- HD pictures everywhere: the placement bonuses (`board.js`) and the bonus spaces (`slotGlyph`) draw the university and partner zoo from `web/icons_hd/Fac.webp` / `Partner-Zoo.webp` (the 4x upscales of the tracks, 464 x 376 / 428 x 336) instead of the icon sheet sprites (116 x 94 / 107 x 84), through `fitImageAttrs(type, cx, cy, h)` in `bonusart.js` (width from the aspect of `BONUS_HD.icons[type].box`, `preserveAspectRatio: 'none'`, the shadow below). The size and centring are unchanged (`FIT_H`, `FIT_DY`).
- Partner zoo contrast: its grey blob is close to the yellow, so the sprite gets a soft dark shadow, `iconShadow(type, h)` in `icons.js` = svg `filter: drop-shadow(0 .06h .06h rgba(0,0,0,.85))` on the `<image>` (h = the icon's height in the svg's units), used by `board.js`, `slotGlyph` and the HD image of `bonusart.js`. Only the partner zoo; the university has its own dark outline. Tried and dropped: recoloured blobs (cream / ink / blue / brown), a dark outline, a white halo.
- `SLOT_FIT` (`association.js`): the animal magnet (map 12) is 38 px high (its sprite is 54 px wide; it overflowed at 50), the snapping icon (purple square, 24 maps) 46 px instead of 44. `slotGlyph` is also what the bar under the zoo (`assocbar.js`) draws. Checked on all 17 maps (a versions) in the sandbox.

The 1 conservation of map 11's third set sits on a yellow pentagon (`bonusHex`, 92 high) like the other conservation bonuses of the strip (it reaches past the left edge of the strip's viewBox: the wide strip svg has `overflow:visible; position:relative; z-index:3`, so it is drawn above the board next to it instead of being cut). The ends lie in the margin (x 15 left, 336 right), clear of the frame of the partner zoo / university region (x 24 to 327); the control points keep the bonus in the middle at x 0 / 351 (-12 for the third set; control points at x -15 / 366, -39 for the third). Tried and dropped (mock): colour rings on the slot pairs, numbered pairs, straight lines on both sides, one dark ink line, ink outline with colour core, arrowheads.

The strip's view box is now `-78 0 507` (`stripUnits` 507, was 451) so the right-hand pentagons are not clipped by the zoo panel; the strip svg has `overflow:visible`, z-index 3, so the left ones may reach into the board.)

`PLAYS_FOR_RE` also matches `buys`: "X buys Sand Tiger Shark from display for 32 and places it in ..." shows the price as the money icon with the number, like "plays ... for 14".

## Special enclosures in "places it in ..." (log-labels.js, `placesText`)

BGA's text for an animal put into special buildings ("X plays A for 5 and places it in the large aquarium, the underwater tunnel") is reworded in the move bar and the log (`amountMarkup` calls `placesText` first; texts of the live game come through the same way): the special buildings are capitalised (the Large Aquarium, the Small Aquarium, the Underwater Tunnel, the Large Bird Aviary, the Reptile House, the Petting Zoo); several buildings are joined by "and" ("the Large Aquarium and the Underwater Tunnel"; three or more: commas and a last "and"); both aquariums together become "both Aquariums" ("both Aquariums", "both Aquariums and the Underwater Tunnel" / "and the Reptile House"). Lists with anything else ("a size-2 enclosure", "a pavilion") and the Flocking text stay as BGA writes them.

An empty storage zone of the dock (`stored`) shows just the empty container: its `cardRow` gets an empty text (no "none"; the endgame zone keeps it). The player info box shows the number of stored cards next to the hand for map 11 players: the store icon (`r5c2`, the one of the dock button) and `X/3` (`.rt.hd.stored`, `flash` on the count, tooltip "Cards stored under the notepad (Caves) / capacity").

## Player info box: overlapping bonus tokens (parkposter.css)

Several bonus tokens (`.btoken` in `.ppres2 .bons`) no longer wrap: the row is `nowrap`, every token stays 36 px high, the later one lies on top. Two tokens overlap by 18 px and sit diagonally (first 5 px up, second 5 px down); three or more overlap by only 10 px on one line (no vertical offset). A single token stays centred. Known limit: on map 11 (Caves) the storage counter takes room, so two or three tokens can run past the right edge of the box.

## Discounted animal prices (price.js, cards.js, shared.js, settings.js, view.py, cardgen/shoot.mjs)

Setting "Apply discounts to animals" (Cards tab, second row, under "Hand card size", `S.applyDiscounts`, default on, saved as `discounts`; switching it redraws every card like the no-snake mode). With it on an animal's price badge shows the price after the discounts, in the card's own look, on the small card and in the enlarged preview; with it off the printed price stays. `price.js` `discounted(seat, key)`: card price - 3 per partner zoo icon of the animal's continents (a continent icon counts once per icon on the card), - 3 with Expert in Small Animals (S229, size 1-2), - 4 with Expert in Large Animals (S230, size 4-5), never below 0 (the engine's `animals_action.cost` without the display folder and without the bonuses of an action in progress). `card(key, extraClass, seat)` takes the seat whose discounts count: `cardRow` passes the owner for the zones `<seat>:hand` and `<seat>:stored` (stored cards too, whenever the partner zoo came), the display passes 'display' = the player whose point of view is shown (`S.pov`; the player to move when there is none); played cards and other lists show the printed price. The new price is the badge image `web/price_badges/<n>.webp` (n = 0-40, BGA's money icon, 100 x 100) laid over the printed one as an `<img class="cprice">` in % of the card (box = `pbox` of the card catalog entry, from `web/price_badges/geometry.json`: the three requirement cards are scaled to 0.86 and have their own box); in the enlarged preview `withPrice` draws the same badge into a canvas copy of the large card (the preview src gets `#price=N:x,y,w,h`). The catalog (`view.card_catalog`) now also carries `tags` and `pbox` of every animal. The badges and the geometry come from `scripts/cardgen/shoot.mjs` (the end of the script: `out/price/*.png`, `out/price_geometry.json`; copy to `web/price_badges/`, webp, geometry from `large`).

## No-snake images keep the requirement badges (scripts/build_nosnake_images.py)

The animals' `_ns` / `_sw` images used to lose the red requirement bars and their badges under the price panel (everything below y = 260 of the large card was repainted with the new photo). `bars_bottom(key)` now reads the last red bar of the original large card (column x = 12, from y = 262, only the run that starts under the panel counts because the photos are reddish) and the kept block under the grey panel reaches down to it (`BARS`; 0 = no requirements, A487 is unchanged). Remade: the images of A470, A474, A475, A482, A483, A485 and A492 in `web/cards/` and `web/cards_large/`.

## Player info box: stat icons on the action row's grid (parkposter.css)

`.ppres` (money, X tokens, reputation, appeal, conservation) is a grid of five 59 px columns, centred, with no padding: the same spacing as the five action icons of `.ppactions` below, so the columns line up. The endgame chip (`small.eg`) and the endgame card tag (`.egcard`, `right: -15px` on the conservation shield) fit inside the row.

## Player info box: chosen action card with a gold glow (parkposter.css)

`.ppactions .ac.chosen` keeps the 5 px lift and gets a 3 px `--gold` ring plus a soft gold halo (`box-shadow`), so the card of the running action stands out by colour too. The other action cards are unchanged.

## Assertion lightbox (reveal.js, frames.js, action-bar.js, parkposter.css, view.py, parser)

Assertion (take a base project) has its own lightbox, like Perception / Hunter. **Replay:** the step "X keeps P with Assertion" has three frames, `reveal` (the base projects on offer, headline "X reveals N base projects for Assertion"), `reveal1` (the one taken carries the seal, the rest are faded, "X keeps P with Assertion") and `text` (the step text, the card is in the hand; in both lightbox frames it is not, `held` in `revealAt`, `frameState`). The offer is BGA's own list: the parser keeps the private `cardIds` of the choice state (`Move.offers`, `parser/log.py`), `view.py` `_assertion_reveal` puts `{seat, cards, kept}` on the step as `assertion` (offer looked up in the move and the 3 before it; a log without it falls back to every base project not in play). It is shown to both points of view (the choice is public). The icon of the ribbon is the base project badge (`r11c6`). **Fork / live game:** the pending `assertion` effect (its `choose_effect` actions carry `card`) is the same lightbox with Confirm (`forkReveal`); the effect's button is not drawn (`effectButton`). **Layout** (`.assertpop`): two rows, the first the larger half (8 = 4 + 4, 9 = 5 + 4, 10 = 5 + 5, 11 = 6 + 5 ...); `--per` is the number in a row; cards are 190 px wide up to 5 in a row and narrower after that (`--aw`), so a row never gets wider than the 5-card row (1230 px). Test: `tests/test_assertion_offer.py`.

## Lightbox ribbon: gold edge on the right end (parkposter.css)

`.headrow::after` draws a gold line (4 px, `#F4B63F`) along the swallowtail notch of the ribbon, so the notch has the same edge as the top and bottom. It is on `.headrow`, not on `.drafthead`, because the ribbon's `clip-path` would cut it. Applies to every reveal lightbox headline.

## Horse Whisperer lightbox (reveal.js, frames.js, action-bar.js, parkposter.css, view.py)

Horse Whisperer (S275: gain 2 money, take a Petting Zoo animal from the discard pile) opens the whole discard pile in a lightbox (`.hwpop`, `showHorse` / `horseFork` in `reveal.js`), headline "X plays Horse Whisperer" (ribbon icon: the goat badge `/badges/Pet.webp`, in `head()`: a `div.fxicon.pet` whose background is the badge cropped to the 246 px square around the goat, so the goat is in the middle of the tile, with a 5 px gold rim instead of the 3 px of the other ribbon icons). **Layout:** the Petting Zoo animals lie large on top (`--hwc` = 14 % of the window height, 96-160 px wide, wrapped and centred, newest first); below them is the rest of the pile in a tray (`.hwtray`, gold scrollbar), greyed out (`grayscale(1) brightness(.8)`, opacity .92), 112 px cards, 10 per row (`.hwrest` is exactly 10 wide, newest first). **It fits any window height:** `.hwpop` is a flex column with `max-height: 100 --vh - 160 px` (the draft box keeps 110 px under it for the Hide button), the animals shrink with `--vh` and the tray takes the rest of the height and scrolls (min 150 px), so the ribbon is never cut off; the page is laid out at 1920 px wide, only the height varies, and the bin tile (`.hwtile`: a tilted green gold-rimmed tile with the bin icon and the number of cards of the tray, like the ribbon icon) on the top left corner of the tray. The rest cards have the hover preview (`bindPreview`) and cannot be chosen. **No animal in the pile:** the greyed goat badge (`/badges/Pet.webp`) with "× 0" in a dashed strip (`.hwnone`) in place of the animals; the tray is still there (players play it just to look at the pile). **Replay:** `view.py` `_horse_whisperer` puts `horse = {seat, pile (oldest first, before the draw), pets, kept}` on the step "X draws A from discard for Horse Whisperer effect"; a play with no animal has no draw line, so its `horse` (`kept: null`, `pets: []`) is on the step "gains 2 money (Horse Whisperer)" (or the play step). Frames: with an animal `reveal` (pile), `reveal1` (the animal taken has the seal, the other animals are faded) and `text` (card in the hand; in both lightbox frames it is not in the hand and still in the pile, `frameState`); without: `reveal`, `text`. Shown in every point of view (the pile is public). **Fork / live game:** the pending `search_discard` effect (its `choose_effect` actions carry `card`) is the same lightbox: click one animal, "Take the card" plays it (the existing "impossible to undo" confirmation of secret effects still comes first); the effect's button is not drawn (`effectButton`). **Not done:** the engine only has the pending effect when the pile holds an animal, so in a fork / live game a play with no animal opens nothing (it would need an optional pending effect that the differential test skips; engine change, new release). Test: `tests/test_horse_whisperer_view.py`.

## Badges: Pet2 removed (web/badges, scripts/build_badges.py)

`web/badges/Pet2.webp` (a round goat badge from BGA's stylesheet, `data-type=Pet2`) was not an official badge and nothing used it; the file and its `badges.json` entry are gone and `build_badges.py` skips it (`SKIP`). The Petting Zoo animal badge is `Pet.webp` (the side panel's icon rows).

## Lightboxes on short windows (parkposter.css)

Checked Perception-type reveals (Hunter), Assertion and Horse Whisperer at 1920x1080, 1366x768, 1280x720 and 1600x700: the ribbon and the panel stay above the Hide button in all of them. Only the Assertion needed a change: its card width `--aw` is also limited to 17.5 % of the window height (`--vh`), so its two rows shrink on a short window (no change at 1080 px).

## Adapt 3: the "gets 3 x bonus-scoring-cards" line is not a step (view.py)

The log words an Adapt 3 (the 5 / 8 conservation and 16 reputation bonuses, the map 13 project bonus) as "X draws A, B, C for adapt effect", "X gets 3 x bonus-scoring-cards", "X discards A, B, C for adapt effect". The middle line only announces the discard and changes nothing visible, so `_ANNOUNCES_TAKE` (`view.py`, the filter of announcement lines like "gets 1 x take-in-range-or-deck") now drops it in every replay: the draw and the discard are consecutive steps. The engine state that sat on that step (the adapt choice pending after the bonus was chosen) is no longer a fork point; the step before it still is (the bonus choice).

## Adapt lightbox (reveal.js, frames.js, action-bar.js, parkposter.css, view.py, options.py)

Adapt 1 and Adapt 3 (draw endgame cards, then discard some) open a lightbox (`.adaptpop`, `showAdapt` / `adaptFork` in `reveal.js`) with the hourglass ribbon icon (`tall`, as Resistance). One row of the player's endgame cards: the ones held before are plain, the ones just drawn have a gold glow and a diagonal "NEW" sash (`.fresh`, `.sash`); no lift. Every card carries the live conservation strip (`pointsBadge`, the Resistance one). **Replay:** `view.py` sets `step["adapt"]` = `{seat, cards (held, then drawn), drawn, discarded?, points, from?}` on the draw step ("X draws ... for adapt effect") and on the discard step (`_adapt_reveal`, `_adapt_pools`: the discard step shows the draw step's pool with `discarded` marked by a red ✕ at 74 % of the card height so the points stay readable; points are the draw frame's). Both steps have frames `['reveal', 'text']` (frames.js; `frameState` leaves the state as it is for `adapt`). Only the drawing player sees it (`hides(seat)`, as Resistance). **Fork / live:** the pending `adapt` effect (`choose_effect {discard: [...]}`): the pool is the union of the offered discards, held = the seat's `endgame_hand`; click the `n` cards to discard, then "Discard (k/n)"; `options.py` `_revealed_cards` adds `pts` (points of every card in the pool, including the ones about to be drawn) to the effect; `effectButton` hides the pending-effect button. Test: `tests/test_adapt_view.py`.

## Adapt: no empty frame; ribbon centred on the cards (frames.js, parkposter.css)

The Adapt draw step has only the `reveal` frame (no closed-lightbox `text` frame), so Next goes straight from the drawn cards to the discard lightbox; the discard step keeps `['reveal', 'text']`. All reveal lightboxes: `.headrow` is shifted 24 px to the right (`left: 24px`), so the ribbon together with the icon that overhangs its left tip (48 px) is centred on the card row (measured 952 / 952 at 1920 px for Adapt, Horse Whisperer; same CSS for Perception, Hunter, Scuba Dive, Resistance, Assertion).

## Log search bar (log-search.js, main.js, parkposter.css)

The Log tab has a search bar at the top of its box. `setupLogSearch()` (called in `init` after `buildMoveList`) wraps `#moves` in `div#logBox.logbox` and puts `div.logsearch` above it; the bar and the list form one box (the bar has the top border and radius, `.logbox > .moves` loses its top border and top radius). Look: a near-white field (`#FFFCF0`) with a 3 px ink line under the bar (it had a hazard stripe along the top edge for a while, removed). Row: magnifier, input `#logQuery` ("Search the log"), the counter `.ls-cnt` ("2/5"; a pale grey "0/0" when nothing matches), up/down buttons `.ls-btn` (shown only while there is a hit, disabled with 1 hit), a separator and the funnel button `.ls-fun` with a count `.ls-fk` (`FILTERS.active`; the filter bar it will open is not built yet, the button does nothing so far).
Find mode: case-insensitive substring over the label of each line (`li.children[1]`); only lines on show count (not hidden by the spoiler rule, not merged away), so the search never shows moves ahead of the current step. Hits are wrapped in `mark.hit` (faint gold wash), the current hit's first mark gets `.cur` (solid gold, no ring), its line `.hitline` (faint gold tint). Typing selects the last hit (nearest the move on show) and centres it; Enter / ▼ next, Shift+Enter / ▲ previous (wrapping); Esc clears, a second Esc leaves the field. `refreshLogSearch()` runs in `render()` after the lines are hidden/shown, so the counter follows the playback and a rebuilt list (point of view switch) is highlighted again. Not on the mini game pages.

## Log filter bar (log-filter.js, log-search.js, main.js, parkposter.css)

The funnel of the log search bar opens the filter bar `div.logfilter` (built by `setupLogFilter()`, after `setupLogSearch()` in `init`, inserted after `.logsearch` inside `#logBox`; closed by default). Design "two dropdowns": a row of two fields, **Players** (two small squares in the seat colours; an off player is a hollow grey square) and **Actions** ("All" in grey, or a gold "3/7" badge when narrowed); a click opens that field's menu under the row (one at a time, the field turns gold with the chevron up; click outside or Esc closes it). Menu rows are tick box + colour square / action icon + name + a count of the lines it would show (counts follow the other field). A row is two buttons: `.lf-main` (toggles the row) and `.lf-only` (the count; while the row is hovered or has keyboard focus the count is replaced by a small bordered "ONLY" button, 11 px / 800 / uppercase / letter-spacing .1em, gold on hover) which ticks only that row and unticks the rest. On touch devices (`hover: none`) the count stays a count and `.lf-only` is inert. A footer ("Showing 85 of 201 lines" + "Select all", which ticks everything again) appears only while something is narrowed. The funnel turns solid gold with a number (`FILTERS.active` = how many of Players / Actions are narrowed, 0-2; the number is ink, the funnel `.on`); while the bar is open and nothing is narrowed it is a pale gold.
Rules: everything ticked = no filter. Players and Actions combine with AND, the ticked actions with OR. Lines that do not match get `li.fout` (display none; the line on show is never hidden, so playback and `followLog` keep working); `refreshLogFilter()` runs in `render()` before `refreshLogSearch()`, and the search skips filtered lines (`shown()`), so the counter and the highlights follow the filter.
Ownership and action of a line are derived in the frontend from the step labels (`groupsOf`, cached per steps array, `[{act, owner, x}]` per step). A turn is every line from "chooses action card <Animals|Build|Cards|Association|Sponsors>" up to the next such line or the next break: all of it belongs to the player who chose (`owner`, whoever the line names: the display refill and the "discards their Constriction token" after the action are part of the turn) and to that action (`act`); a "pays N xtoken for increasing card strength" line before the choice belongs to the turn that follows. **Neutral** (`owner` null; no menu entry: neutral steps show while no player is unticked and are hidden as soon as one is, Actions > Break shows the break alone; the log marks them with a grey stripe, see below): everything before the first turn (the action card draft, the first draw and discard), the whole break from "Starting a new break" to "End of the break" (income, discards, refills, whoever acts), and, once "End of game triggered" has been seen, every line after the last "places action card" (final scoring). Break lines are also the action Break; **X-back** (action key `xback`, menu name "X-back", the X-token icon) is a turn without a chosen action: the player puts the action card back and gains an X-token, logged as "X gains N xtoken" (no reason) directly followed by "X places action card Y at position 1 (finishing action)"; both lines (and the lines after them up to the next choice) belong to that player and to X-back (a plain "gains N xtoken" that is not followed by that place line, e.g. from a bonus, is not X-back); lines that belong to no action (draft, setup, scoring) are hidden when actions are narrowed. Players is narrowed when one of the two is unticked. A per-step tag from the server would be more exact (BACKEND NEEDED, optional).

## Log: grey stripe for neutral steps (playback.js, log-filter.js)

`buildMoveList` colours the 5 px left stripe of every log line with the acting player's colour; a **neutral** step (`groupsOf(steps)[i].owner === null`: the action card draft and setup, the whole break, the final scoring) gets `NEUTRAL_COLOR` (`#B5B2A8`, exported by `log-filter.js`) instead: a light warm grey, clearly lighter than the mid grey a white player is shown in (`WHITE_AS` `#767c82`, util.js `seatColor`) and than a black player's colour. The `seatN` class of the line is unchanged. Lines of a turn keep the colour of the player the line names (a Pilfering payment by the other player in a turn keeps that player's colour).

## Log filter: nothing outside the filter is shown; "only" closes the menu; the funnel icon (log-filter.js, sidebar.js, log-search.js, parkposter.css)

- The step on show is hidden like any other line when it does not match the filter (no `fghost` any more, it was tried for a moment). `followLog` (sidebar.js) skips hidden lines: it follows the last line before the current step that is shown (a step whose line is `display: none` has no offset), and does nothing when there is none.
- The "ONLY" button of a menu row closes the dropdown (`openMenu = null`), a click on the row (toggle) keeps it open.
- The funnel button draws the filter icon of Google Sheets: three rounded bars, long to short (`M3 6h18M6 12h12M10 18h4`, stroke 3, `.ls-funnel` is stroked, not filled).

## Log search bar: the hazard stripe is gone (parkposter.css)

`.logsearch::before` (the gold/ink stripe) and the `--hz` variable are removed; the bar is 52 px high (was 60) and centres its row (`display: flex; align-items: center`).

## Action cards under the board (zoo.js `actionStrip`, settings.js, main.js, side-panel.js, parkposter.css)

- Settings > Board has a switch "Action cards under the board" (`S.actionStrip`, saved as `actionStrip` in `localStorage 'settings'`, **off by default**, never on in the mini games). The `actionstrip` event redraws the boards (`main.js`).
- `actionStrip(st, seat)` (zoo.js) adds `div.actstrip` as the last child of the zoo box, in all three Player boards layouts. It is **outside the frame**: `position: absolute; top: 100%` below the cream box, so the frame stops where it always did and the cards hang in the free space under it. `fitPlayed` (also observed by `rowWatch`) gives the box `margin-bottom` = the strip's height + 4 px so nothing below (the other board in Top / bottom) is covered; the card images carry `aspect-ratio: 744 / 1039` so the height is known before they load.
- Five columns, one per slot in the **real slot order 1 to 5** (`players[seat].action_cards`), each the **whole action card**, straight (no tilt): `/action_cards/<type>_<variant|0>_<1|2>.webp` (the variant badge is part of the art; Venom / Constriction / Multiplier tokens are drawn over its bottom right like in the player box, `CARD_TOKEN_ICON` / `CARD_TOKEN_TEXT` are exported by side-panel.js). Hover shows the big preview (`bindPreview`).
- Each card is held by a black **binder clip** (inline SVG `CLIP`, `.aclip`) on its top edge with the slot number on it; only the clip body is drawn (no wire handles).
- The card chosen for the running action (`st.current_action`) only gets a thin yellow ring and a yellow glow (`.asl.chosen .acard` box-shadow); it is not lifted and its clip stays black.
- Before the shuffle (`st.actions_hidden`): slot 1 is Animals, slots 2 to 5 are empty outlined slots with their clip.
- The strip adds about 200 px of height per board (side by side the page scrolls a little at 1080 px). The floating hand tray still floats over it (not handled).
- With a drawer open (`.zoo.drawer-open`) its association bar hangs 58 px under the box: the strip is pushed down to just under it (`top: calc(100% + 50px)`, `padding-top: 8px`, and `fitPlayed` adds the 50 px to the box's bottom margin), so the cards stay visible. A box with a strip is `align-self: start` in `#zoos`, so the margin does not stretch the neighbouring frame.
- The number on each clip is the slot's **strength number**, not always 1 to 5: `state_view` (replay/view.py) gives every player `slot_numbers` (five numbers, `slot + map_rules.strength_bonus`), which differs from 1 2 3 4 5 only on map 12, where the black squares conceal numbers (a covered number becomes the next visible one: e.g. 3 3 3 5 5, and 3 3 3 6 6 with all four). `actionStrip` reads `players[seat].slot_numbers` and falls back to the position when it is missing.

## Map 12: action strength in the player box (side-panel.js, parkposter.css)
- Only for a player on map 12 (`p.map_id === '12'`, not before the shuffle): every action icon of `.ppactions` gets `span.acstr` with the real strength icon (`amountIcon('STRENGTH', p.slot_numbers[i])`, log-labels.js: black perforated square, Park Num font) at 20 px on its bottom left (`left/bottom: -8px`), so the Venom / Constriction / Multiplier tokens on the right stay free. The variant badge is top left.
- The icon's size is forced to 20 px (`.acstr .labelamount .labelicon`, the `--ah` rule of the log would make it 28 px); the number is `.9em` with a 1.1 px outline. `slot_numbers` comes from `state_view` (see the action strip section).

## "Sun Bathing" is "Sunbathing" everywhere
- Every text, key and data entry spelled "Sun Bathing" now reads "Sunbathing": `animals.json` (ability name), `animals_action.py`, `replay/options.py`, planning data / `gen_planning.py`, `docs/engine_design.md`, and the card generator (`IconName.ts`, locale `common.json`, `harness/out.cjs`, `site/cards.html`). `scripts/import_data.py` writes the new spelling when the data is regenerated from upstream. The ability line of the 7 animal cards that have it (A422, A454, A471, A472, A473, A478, A481) was redrawn: `build_cards.py --only ...` (`web/cards/`, `web/cards_large/`).

## Iconic Animal: "Iconic Animal: Africa" (cardgen `Ability.tsx`, locale `common.json`)
- The ability line of an animal with a long value (Iconic Animal) reads "Iconic Animal: Africa" (a space after the colon), and the continent words `africa` / `asia` / `australia` of the locale are capitalised, so the description reads "for each Africa icon in all zoos". Redrawn: A418, A436, A452, A476, A517 (`build_cards.py --only ...`), both sizes. Sea Turtle Tank (S250 / S250_MW) already reads "(Sunbathing 2)" after the Sunbathing fix.

## Icon followed by punctuation in card texts (cardgen `ParseDescription.tsx`)
- A word like `{Appeal}.` / `{ConservationPoint},` / `{Money-3}.` lost its punctuation (the parser replaced the whole word by the icon). It now draws the icon and keeps the trailing punctuation: the full stop after the appeal icon of Pilfering 1, the comma of Pilfering 2, the full stop of Jumping, and the other animal texts with an icon at the end of a sentence. Full card rebuild (`build_cards.py`): 53 images changed (about 26 cards plus the no-snake variants).

## The endgame card counter goes away with the final scoring (side-panel.js)
- The small terracotta card with the number next to the conservation shield of the player whose cards are hidden (`egCardIcon`) is only drawn while the game is not scored: it disappears from the first step of the final scoring (`st.phase` `scoring` / `over`, the same moment the engine's live count `endgame_live` goes to 0 because the points are in the score).

## Endgame card counter: counts the unscored cards (view.py `_endgame_unscored`, side-panel.js)
- Replaces the "gone from the first scoring step" rule above. Every replay step has `players[i].endgame_unscored`: the endgame cards of the player that are not scored yet. The log scores a card in a step of its own ("X gains 2 conservation (Climbing Park)", name matched case-insensitively with the card catalogue), so the count goes down in exactly that step: 2 cards 2 -> 1 -> 0, one card 1 -> 0. From the final scoring on it is 0 (a card worth 0 points has no line). The little card of the hidden player shows `min(cards that count, endgame_unscored)` and disappears at 0. Forks, sandbox and live games have the field too: `state_view` gives `endgame_unscored` = the cards of the hand until the phase is `over` (the engine scores all cards in the one step that ends the game), then 0; the page's fallback for a state without the field is the same rule.

## Map-specific rows of the results table + sponsor statistics (mapstats.py, results.js, sponsor_stats.py, sponsor-stats.js)
Replay only. `replay/mapstats.py` counts the map ability statistics from the raw log (`map_stats`, one dict per seat, keys of that seat's map only; `a` variants share their map's keys) and `view.py` ships them as `map_stats` in the replay (`null` for the mini games' cut views). `results.js` shows them: `MAP_OVERVIEW` rows in the Overview category right above "Thinking time" (Tower appeal, Sponsors marketed with map ability, Overall points of marketed sponsors, Animals played with map ability, Cards pouched with map ability, Continent markers removed, Reputation / Appeal (x2) / Hunter from quadrant effect, Shark Attack points, Person sponsors played with map ability, Cards discarded for action strength); the exceptions are placed by hand in `groups()`: "Gained from map ability" (map 4 Commercial Harbor, the sale money) and "Gained from map income" (maps 5, 7, 11; map 13: "Income from quadrant effect" = 3 money per money-quadrant trigger) below "Gained from income", "Clever from map ability" (map 6) below Clever. A row appears when either player's map has the key (the other player shows an en dash and no pointer).
Rules (all from the history specs): income = `getBonuses` source "map income"; Tower appeal = `getBonuses` source "Map N bonus"; marketing = `gameStateChange id=82` source "map bonus space" followed by that seat's `playSponsor`, its points are the final `sponsor_stats` of that sponsor (appeal buckets + conservation, later points included); Harbor = `id=84` followed by the seat's "sells" `discardCards`; pouches = `discardCards.pouch == "mapPouched"`; cubes = `discardTokens` with `continent`; T1 = the public "for Map T1 effect" `discardCards`; Clever = `id=65` source "map bonus space" + the "(Clever effect)" cleanup; person sponsors = `id=125` source "map bonus space" + `playSponsor`; shark points = the appeal of a `getBonuses` "Shark Attack" whose selector (`id=111`) came from a "placement bonus" (also an Excavation copy) or the Archaeologist (source-less selector after a `takeBonus` SharkAttack); quadrants = `mapStatus` false -> true (any event) + the explicit "Map 13 quarter bonus" once per (seat, move, quadrant) + `takeBonus` MAP_13_INCOME; Research Institute = a `buyAnimal` whose latest private status has `conditions.ignored` > 0, no ignore token discarded just before, and hex (0, 11) already covered (from the replay steps' buildings; "applicable" semantics, the same as the ignore spec).
Sponsor statistics: Excavation Site counts only the extra copy of a placement bonus (a 5-money space is +5, not +10: `sponsor_state_gains` and the unknown-map fallback of `sponsor_sources.py`, which emits every second activation of one placement); Archaeologist counts a source-less `takeBonus` only when its (bonus_type, bonus_n) was offered by the selector (`_archaeologist_offer`: `choices`, `allChoices` only as the fallback, Pass offers nothing; one resolution uses one offer; the offer is replaced, not summed, when a state is re-emitted); Waza Large Animals Program has the new counter `ignores` ("Large animals played with an ignored condition": a large animal by the `sizes` delta, ignored > 0, no token, Waza in play; `waza_large_events`, wired through `logged_gains`); Sea Turtle Tank adds `money` (4 per card sunbathed); the Waza Small counter `third_small` is labelled "Extra small animals played". Tests: `tests/test_mapstats.py`; the Excavation expectations of `tests/test_sponsor_stats_rest.py` were halved.

## 138. Other player's card flights

For the seat that is not followed, only counts are known, so the flights come from `otherPlan` (card-motion-plan.js): hand-count change minus pilfered cards, display cards that left the display (public), and new `main_discard` cards.
- Draw from the display: the real card glides to the right edge of that player's info box (`.pp[data-seat]`, set in side-panel.js) with the same glide as the followed player's display take, duration scaled by distance (650 ms per 850 px, 650-1500 ms). It keeps its size until the last 24 % of the flight, where it shrinks and fades (the vanishing part).
- Draw from the deck / discard: card backs only (`web/card_back.webp`, 420x588, cut from the 4th panel of `scripts/cardgen/site/img/card-backgrounds.jpg`, inside the frame so no light edge) fly between the invisible deck (`deckPoint()`) and the info box, 560-800 ms, tilt alternating 9 deg, 100 ms stagger.
- Pilfering (`parsePilfer` reads "X gives N card(s) to Y from Pilfering effect"; money pilfers are ignored): the card we give leaves our hand as a back to the receiver's info box; the card we receive flies from the giver's info box as a back and turns face-up in our hand.
- Not in a pure fork (both seats are played there), minigames or with reduced motion.
- Tests: `node --test tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs` (16 tests).

- Sunbathing (own and other seat): a ray from the invisible deck to the card (to the player's info box for the other seat), held for the whole animation (`.cm-ray`, `sunRay`); the card or back is beamed up along it. Sunbathing also covers the Sponsors action card's level 3 discards ("discards X to gain money" and, on the upgraded side, "discards X to increase Sponsors action's strength by 2"). The level 4 (Marketing) discards ("to play a Sponsor card for money", "to snap a Sponsor card") are normal discards, not sunbathing.
- Gliding (own and other seat): the card flies to the invisible deck in a trail of bubbles (`bubbleTrail`).
- Other seat: sunbathe, glide and pouch are counted (`discardKinds` reads the log lines of that player, pouches come from the new `under` / `pouched` entries) and shown as that many backs; the pouch backs slide out of the info box towards the zoo and are tucked away.
- The backs are as wide as a card of the display (`backWidth`).
- Money tile flash: the ring rule of replay.css targeted `.rs.money` but the tile is `.rt.money`, so the ring never showed; fixed.
- Resource tile flash (appeal, conservation, reputation, X tokens): the background flash was hidden behind the opaque picture; it is now a green / red glow that follows the picture's shape (`glow-up` / `glow-down` in parkposter.css, 1.5x the flash duration, held for the first third), the number stays above it.
- Glide: the bubbles are bigger, rimmed and glowing so they show on every background (20 along the flight); the card starts moving sooner. The log line no longer says "for a total of N <icon>" (move bar, log, every mode: `labelNode` in log-labels.js): "X discards A, B (Glide effect)".
- Pilfering is read from the per-card lines ("X gives <card> from the Pilfering effect" shrinks the giver's hand, "Y gets <card> ..." grows the receiver's); the "gives 1 card to ..." summary line changes nothing and is ignored, so it no longer triggers a stray deck draw. Examples: table 930781276 steps 169 / 170, 853899676 steps 244 / 245.
- Pilfering is one step now (`_merge_pilfer` in replay/view.py): "A gives B <card> from the Pilfering effect" replaces the give / get / summary lines (the state of the last one stays), the card animation runs in that step (`parsePilfer` reads the one-line form). "X chooses A and A to Pilfer" shows the name once (`labelNode`).
- Playing a conservation project (replay, live, sandbox, fork; the followed player's hand or the display; `playProject` in card-motion.js, `movementPlan(...).projects` finds the project in `projects_in_play`, the strip is `.projstrip[data-key]`): the card glides to the open spot of the association board (650 ms per 850 px, at least 650 ms, eased in and out), then flips: it turns edge-on around its middle (200 ms, `scaleX` to 0.001, then removed) and straight after the real strip unfolds from its middle (220 ms, `scale` 0 to 1.04 to 1). No gold ring or other glow. The slot (`.projslot`) itself is never moved or scaled (its neighbour in `.projpanel.pair` and its own `--shift` stay put); until the strip unfolds it keeps the look of an empty place: its outline is the `.projempty` inset box-shadow (parkposter.css), read from a hidden probe clone, and the green base (`background-image`) is withheld, both switch to the real values at the moment the strip starts to unfold. The other seat's projects: see the next entry.
- Card flights no longer "blink": `animate()` starts every animation paused and releases them together two frames later, so a render that blocks the page for a few hundred ms cannot eat the first part of a flight; a delayed follow-up animation on the same element must use `fill: 'forwards'` (with `'both'` its first keyframe covers the earlier flight, which made the played card sit at the spot from the first frame).
- Other player's projects (`otherPlan(...).projects`, `playProject(p, i, g, emerge)`): from the display the card flies like ours; from their hidden hand the real card fades in at their info box and flies to the spot, then the same flip; the played card is no longer counted as a discard of that seat. General rule for every animation of the non-POV seat: the info box in the sidebar (`.pp[data-seat]`, `boxPoint`) is their hand. Test added (16 in `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`).
- Money rows of the results table (`countStats` in results.js): new global rows "Gained from kiosk income" (right below "Gained from income": `getBonuses` money with source "kiosk income") and "Spent overall" (right above "Spent on animals": animals + buildings + donations + every payment that is a negative-money `getBonuses`: buying a sponsor card, the range cost of a sponsor or project, Venom ...; the animal price `amount` of a `buyAnimal` already includes its range cost, so the display cost row is not added again).
- Results table labels may contain a line break (`\n` in the label, `.gslab { white-space: pre-line }` in parkposter.css): "Sponsors marketed / with map ability" and "Overall points of / marketed sponsors" "Animals played / with map ability" and "Cards pouched / with map ability" break there.
- "Gained overall" also counts the money of `gainMarked` ("for their mark on <card>") and of `discardCards` whose log says "sells" (selling cards), which come without a `getBonuses` event. Pilfered money (`pilferingMoney`: `player_id` gives, `player_id2` receives) counts as spent by the giver ("Spent overall") and as gained by the receiver ("Gained overall").
- The results (post-game stats) have their own address: one sub-step after the last step (`#456.12` when the final board is `#456.11`; `moveHash(step, phase, results)` in playback.js, called with `S.results` in main.js `render`). `stepOfHash` returns a third value `results` (the sub-index is one past the steps of the last move); boot sets `S.wantResults` and renders, `syncResults` (results.js) opens the results as soon as `resultsAvailable()` (a conceded game needs the log read first) and drops the flag if the step is not the last.
- The log keeps what was revealed: `render` (main.js) tracks `S.seenStep` (the furthest step shown in this replay, reset for a new replay) and hides only the lines after it (`list.children[i].hidden = i > S.seenStep`; `log-filter.js` uses the same limit). Going back to move 40 after move 100 keeps moves 41-100 listed; the current line stays highlighted and the spoiler rule still holds for steps never reached. A reload starts again from the address that is opened.
- Map statistics of legacy maps: tables played before the 6a fix have the map id `6a-legacy` (hyphen, `data/map_quirks.py`), but `replay/mapstats.py` only stripped `_legacy`, so these tables got no map rows at all. `_base_map` now strips `-legacy` too (`6a-legacy` counts like map 6: "Animals played with map ability" and "Clever from map ability" appear, `tests/test_mapstats.py`).
- Folded floating container: (the unfold-play-fold workaround described here was removed, see "Folded tray: card motions play in place".)

## Single card into the floating tray: frame opens with the flight
`card-motion.js`: `frameWidth()`, `capture.frameW`, `openWith(flight, fromWidth)` called by `run()` for exactly one arrival without departures / projects in tray mode. `arrive()` returns `{delay, duration, ease}` of the card's flight (deck: slide + delay, `ease-in-out`; display take: `EASE`; targeted: after the 1220 ms hold, glide curve) and the frame's `width` is animated old -> new with the same timing while `overflow: hidden` is set inline (restored when done). The CSS has no width transition on `.trayframe` (only height), so nothing else changes. Details: docs/animations_v01.md.

## Targeted draw: magnifying glass (replaces the Species spotlight)
`card-motion.js` `searchFlight({ front, goal, angle, fan, delay, backGoal })`: dim (`.cm-dim`), a row of 7 face-down cards (`.cm-flip`, two faces for the 3D flip), the lens (`.cm-lens`, `--u` = page scale) on one pendulum path (`x = c - R cos(theta)`, cosine-eased theta, ends over a random card 0-5), hops, ring flash (`.cm-flash`), the others drop, the chosen card is pulled out, flipped (own seat, `front` = the hand card's image) and glides to `goal` (650 ms; tray `EASE`, fan `glideEase` with the upright rotation); returns `{ done, glide }`, `glide` is what `openWith` follows. `arrive()` uses it for `plan.kind === 'targeted'` (the old spotlight / species badge / 1220 ms hold and `spotlight()` are removed, `.cm-spotlight` / `.cm-species` CSS removed). Other seat: `runOther` calls it with `front: null, backGoal: true` for `plan.targeted` (the card stays a back, glides into the info box and fades). `card-motion-plan.js`: `targetedCount(text, name)` (University, Monkey Gang, species Management Plans, map 8 sponsor, map 14 person sponsor for the 1st / 2nd / last worker bonus but not `Map 14 effect`, Waza Special Assignment), `movementPlan` marks the first arrival of such a step `targeted` (the `species` field stays when the text has a `<SEARCH-...>` tag), `otherPlan` has `targeted`. Tests: `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs` (22 passing). docs/animations_v01.md section 7.

## Buildings glide from / to the invisible deck
`card-motion.js` `runBuildings` / `buildingFlight` (called from `afterRender`, state captured in `beforeRender` as `buildingCapture`): a building that is new between the previous and the shown state flies from `deckPoint()` to its place on the zoo map, a cut-down one flies from its place to the deck (forward steps only, not with reduced motion). `card-motion-plan.js` `buildingChanges(before, after)` tells the arrivals / removals per seat by type + anchor hex. `board.js`: every building group has `data-bseat` / `data-bid`, and a building that left since the last drawing is drawn invisible as `g.cm-leaving` (source of the copy). The flying piece is a clone of the group in an overlay svg (`#card-motion .cm-building`) placed with `getScreenCTM()`; the clone gets fresh mask / clip-path ids. Details in `docs/animations_v01.md` ("Buildings"). Test: `buildingChanges` in `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`.

## Display leave / replenish, face-up targeted draw of the other seat, slower buildings
`card-motion.js`: `runDisplay` (cards leaving the display by wave / natural discard glide into the invisible deck, replenished cards glide out of it into their slot; `displayChanges` in `card-motion-plan.js`), `shotOnScreen` (display cards are measured on screen, not by offsetWidth), `runOther` gets the step text and shows the other seat's targeted card face-up (`targetedNames`), building flights are 25 % slower (812-1375 ms). Details in `docs/animations_v01.md`. Tests in `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`.

## Display cards that are given to a player glide to the info box
`card-motion.js` `givenTo` / `displayTake`: a card that leaves the display into the discard pile of the state but is given to a player by the log ("X digs <card> from the display") glides to that player's info box instead of into the deck (`runDisplay`); `runOther` uses the same `displayTake`. See `docs/animations_v01.md`.

## Display cards slide into their new slots (domino)
`card-motion.js` `runDisplay`: cards that change slot in the display glide from the old to the new position, left to right with 70 ms between cards (`420 + 110 * slots` ms), then the replenished cards come out of the deck. Details in `docs/animations_v01.md`.


## Wave: one breaking-wave motion through the display (replay only)
New `web/js/wave.js` (`createWave(parent, box, Hw)`: a procedural canvas wave, no images; `timeAt(px, local)` = when the foot of the wave reaches a point, `burst` = spray, `run(clock)` draws until the clock returns null). `card-motion.js` `waveNext` / `runWave`: a removal "due to Wave" whose next step (same `move_id`) is "The display is replenished" is played as one motion on the removal step: the wave rolls right to left; cards that leave are thrown off over the crest, cards that stay are lifted and set down in their new slot (small bounce), new cards rise out of the water behind it. The viewer then moves on to the replenish step by itself (`go(step + 1, 0, false)`, only if the step was not changed meanwhile). The wave's clock is an animation on the canvas, so it follows `S.animationSpeed` and `stop()`. Other display discards (first two cards, natural) and fork / live / sandbox / mini games keep the glide of `runDisplay`; if the slots are not one row or the card counts do not match, `runWave` returns false and the glide runs.

## Starting hands: one player after the other
`card-motion.js` `sequenced(by, fn)` + `shift` / `spanEnd` in `animate`: when the own seat and the other seat both deal or discard in the same step (the starting hands, the discard of 4), seat 0 animates first and seat 1 after it, whoever the move bar follows (the folded tray's 480 ms opening is taken into account). No extra steps.

## Starting hands: tray, fan and display follow the followed player's turn
`card-motion.js` `afterRender`: when both players' cards move in one step, each player's part gets a start time (`doOwn(by)` / `doOther(by)` return when they end). The followed player's tray no longer reacts early: in `run()` a part that starts late (`shift > 0`) animates the tray frame's width from the old width itself (starting when the last discarded card is gone / over the length of the deal) and delays the tray's slide-in (`.handtray.enter` gets `animation-delay`); The display's replenish (`runDisplay`) is started after both players' parts (`handsEnd`) instead of during the second player's discards.
`beforeRender`: when the followed player's hand goes from empty to its first cards (the starting deal, stepping forward) the hand is unfolded for good (`S.dockHidden = false`, `dockSel` = that hand, saved to localStorage `dockHidden`): tray and fan.

## Buildings: a new building shows itself at the deck first
`card-motion.js` `buildingFlight` (appearing buildings): the first 26 % of the flight hold the building at the deck while it fades in and grows a little, then it flies to its place; 30 % longer in total (about 1.05-1.8 s). Buildings that are cut down are unchanged.

## Targeted draws are public for everybody
`replay/view.py` `_PUBLIC_DRAW` now also holds Map 8, Map 14, Waza Special Assignment and Monkey Gang (the visibility sheet says search effects and Monkey Gang are public; before, the viewer who followed the other player read "X draws 1 card" for them, so there was no card in the log line and no search animation). The starting hands (`_split_keep_and_draw`): a card found by a search (Map 14's person sponsor) is no longer counted in "X draws 8 cards and 2 endgame cards" but named on a line of its own ("X draws <card> with <SPONSOR-PERSON> (Map 14 effect)"). `card-motion-plan.js`: the Map 14 line is a targeted draw now (it was excluded), so the starting person sponsor gets the search animation, own seat and other seat. Live games: `replay/fork.py` `narrate` names the sponsor found by Map 8 (`search_sponsor`) like it names a category search. Tests: `tests/test_targeted_public.py`, `card-motion-plan.test.mjs`.

## Targeted search: the move bar waits for the reveal
`card-motion.js` `holdBar` / `releaseBar`: when a search animation starts (`searchFlight`, own seat and other seat, replay only) the move bar (`#current`) keeps the text of the step before (`previous.bar`, a clone of the bar's content taken at the end of every `afterRender`) and switches to the step's own text about 700 ms after the glass settles, when the card is turned over (scaled by the animation speed). `stop()` (a new step, resize, a click on the dock) releases it at once. The Log tab in the sidebar is not delayed.

## Info box flights also work on the Log (and Chat) tab
`card-motion.js` `boxPoint(seat)`: the info boxes (`.pp[data-seat]`) live in the Control pane, which is `hidden` while the Log or Chat tab is open; they have no size then and every flight to / from a player's info box (draws and discards of the other player, display cards given to a player, pilfering, targeted draws, back flights) was skipped. Now `boxPoint` shows the Control pane for a moment (`visibility: hidden`, the other pane hidden, measured, restored in the same task, nothing is painted in between) and returns the spot where the box is on the Control tab, y clamped to the viewport. The cards fly to / from that spot even though it is not on screen (on the Log tab it is where the log is).

`boxPoint` (follow-up): the spots of the info boxes are kept (`boxSpots`, per window size and zoom). With the Log tab open the Control pane is shown only once to measure both boxes, not once per card (the log is a long list, every toggle was a heavy relayout during the start of an animation).

## Log search: clear button; display folders before the first replenish
`log-search.js`: a small X (`.ls-clear`, hidden while the field is empty; `parkposter.css` needs `.ls-clear[hidden]` because `.ls-btn` sets `display: grid`) between the field and the hit counter clears the search and puts the focus back in the field; Escape uses the same `reset`. `shared.js` `renderShared`: the display always draws 6 folders (`Math.max(6, display.length)`); before the first replenish the display is an empty list, so no folder was drawn at all until the cards came.

## Hand / endgame flights on every tab, Resistance and Adapt endgame draws
