`card-motion.js`: (1) **Zones that are not on show.** The followed player's cards of a zone whose tab is not open (the other tab is open, or the tray / fan is folded) are no longer left out: `tabFlights(old, st, seat)` makes the drawn cards fly face-up from the invisible deck to that zone's round button (`.traydisc` in the tray, `.dockbtn` in the fan; the fan buttons now carry `data-seat` / `data-kind` like the discs) and the discarded ones from the button to the deck; the button blinks as each lands (`blinkTab`, the keyframes of the played-cards tab). The tab is not switched and no card is added to a visible row. `tabPrev` is the state before the step (set in `beforeRender` for any forward step). A folded tray on its hand / endgame tab plays its own row in place (see "Folded tray: card motions play in place"). (2) **Endgame cards.** `card-motion-plan.js` `endgamePlan(before, after, seat)` (arrivals `deck`, departures `discard`, `after: true`, nothing dealt in the setup). When the endgame tab is open its row flies like the hand (`visibleZone()` / `zoneKind()` replace the hand-only check; `previous.kind` keeps a tab switch from counting as a draw). With `plan.after` `run()` plays the departures first and starts the arrivals when they are over (`lead`, read by `arrive` through `plan.lead`): Adapt discards, then draws. (3) **Other seat.** `otherPlan(..., eg)` also returns `egDeck` / `egDiscard` (counts of endgame cards; Adapt passes `{out, in}` from the step's `adapt` data via `adaptCounts`, so a card drawn and discarded again animates nothing); `runOther` flies them as backs of the endgame card (`BACK_EG` = `web/card_back_endgame.webp`), discards first, the draw 900 ms later. `backGhost(x, y, w, src)` takes the back image. (4) **Lightbox.** `frames.js` `frameState` (Adapt): both lightbox frames show the endgame cards held before the effect, so when the lightbox closes the real state differs and the discard / draw play; Resistance already hid the kept card until the lightbox closed. After the lightbox is gone the kept card arrives with the normal deck draw.
**Asset:** `web/card_back_endgame.webp` (840x1176) is redrawn from the user's screenshot of the endgame back (terracotta gradient, light inner border, hourglass); no original file was found. Replace it with the real back under the same name if one turns up.

`card-motion.js` follow-up (single drawn card did not fly in): `arrive` (floating tray, deck draw) now draws the flight in the overlay (ghost, the real card hidden until it lands) instead of animating the real card. The tray frame is held to `overflow: hidden` while it widens for a lone card (`openWith`), which clipped the card, so it only showed once it was inside the frame; drawing in the overlay avoids the clip. `tabFlights` takes the display shots: a card taken from the display with the other tab open starts from its slot in the display and flies to the tab button (it used to fly from the deck after the display card vanished).

`card-motion.js` (endgame cards): the points strip (`.egpts`) of a newly drawn endgame card has no delay: it is part of the card and shows the moment the card lands (a short-lived fade-in that was added for it was removed again). Flights to a tab button (`tabFlights`) keep the size of a card of the display (`backWidth()`, or the display card's own size for a display take) until they are close to the button, then shrink into it; they used to shrink early and looked small.

`card-motion.js` `arrive` (opening deal): the overlay flight introduced for deck draws into the tray is used only when the tray is not sliding up. A tray that has just been drawn (`.handtray.enter`, the starting hand) carries its cards with it, so the cards fly inside the tray again (the overlay copies were aimed at where the tray stood when the flight began and landed in the wrong places).

## Starting hands: deal and discard, cleaned up
The start of the game is three steps of the log: seat 0 draws 8 cards + 2 endgame cards, seat 1 does the same, then both discard 4 (one step, with the first replenish of the display). In a player's point of view the other player's draw step is skipped (`log_only`, playback.js), so the viewer jumps from one frame to the next over it. Before, the jump netted the other player's deal and discard into "4 cards drawn" and played it after one's own discard (seat 0's point of view: own deal, own discard, then 4 backs, then the display). Now the order is always: seat 0's deal, seat 1's deal, seat 0's discard, seat 1's discard, the display.
- `card-motion.js` `openingSplit(before, after, seat)`: when the viewer jumped over steps from a setup state, the other player's hand and endgame cards are walked step by step through the skipped states; each step that changed them is one part for `runOther` (`{ step, plan }`). `ownStep` finds the step in which the followed player's own cards changed. `afterRender` sorts the parts by step, then by seat (seat 0 first), and plays them one after the other (`sequenced`); the display's replenish waits for all of them (`handsEnd`).
- The two endgame cards of the starting hand are dealt too: `endgamePlan` / `otherPlan` no longer skip the setup. The followed player's fly to the endgame tab after the 8 cards (`tabPlans` / `tabFlights(tabs, shots, lead)`, now run inside the followed player's part with `lead` = when the open row's flights end), the other player's are endgame backs after the 8 backs.
- `dock.js` `drawTray`: no tray while the shown seat has no cards in the setup (before the deal). The tray is drawn with the deal and slides in when the followed player's cards start (its slide is delayed, `run`), and until then it is fully transparent (an opacity hold on `.handtray`; the round buttons on its top edge used to peek out of the bottom of the window). The empty dark tray that waited during the other player's deal is gone.
Checked on 791369985 and 833146203 from both points of view, tray (open / folded) and fan, manual steps and autoplay.

### Starting hands: one step for both deals (playback.js)
The log has one step per player for the starting draw (`log_only`). The viewer now always shows them as ONE step, whoever the point of view is (or none): `dealMerged(n)` skips the first draw of the pair (and `go()` leads to the second), whose state has both hands. Order of the opening is therefore always: step A = seat 0 dealt, seat 1 dealt; step B = seat 0 discards, seat 1 discards, then the display's first replenish. The point of view only decides which animation (open cards or backs) and what is visible.

### Waiting card backs invisible (card-motion.js, `runOther` `back()`)
The other player's backs (endgame backs included) that wait for their turn in a sequenced deal sat visible at the deck point (first keyframe opacity 1 with fill backwards). They now start at opacity 0 and become visible 2 % into their flight.

### Move bar of the merged starting deal (frames.js `barLabel`)
On the merged deal step the move bar says "A and B draw 8 cards and 2 endgame cards" (different counts: "A draws ..., B draws ..."); before, the line of the other player replaced the followed player's. The move list keeps one line per player. One line fits up to about 34 + 34 characters (narrow letters) or 23 + 23 (all wide capitals) of player name at the 1920 px layout.

### Move bar: the two starting discards in one sentence (frames.js `discardsOf`)
"A discards 4 cards · B discards 4 cards · The display is replenished" is shown as "A and B discard 4 cards · The display is replenished" (names in seat order; different counts: "A discards …, B discards …"); the move list keeps the original lines. `barLabel` applies it to every step label.

### Log: the starting discards on separate lines (log-labels.js `stepLabelNode`)
The Log entry of the discard step shows one line per player and one for the display refill ("A discards 4 cards" / "B discards 4 cards" / "The display is replenished"); still ONE entry (one number, one click target, `#moves` stays one `li` per step because other modules index it by step). The move bar joins them (frames.js `discardsOf`).

### Starting discards: three real steps (replay/view.py `_split_discards`, `merge_group`)
Like the starting draws: the move of the first discards is three steps in the data ("A discards 4 cards", "B discards 4 cards", "The display is replenished"; states: first discarder done / both done / display refilled). All of them are `log_only` with `merge_group: "discard"` (the draws: `"deal"`); `playback.js dealMerged` skips a step of a run whose next step has the same group, so the viewer shows the last one, and the move bar says "A and B discard 4 cards · The display is replenished" (`frames.js barLabel`). The Log has one row per step. Steps after it are numbered 2 higher than before (`setup_steps` counts them via `len(steps)`; restart the server).

### Live endgame points travel with the card (card-motion.js `shot` / `ghost`, parkposter.css)
The strip of an endgame card (`.egpts`) is copied onto the flying copy of the card (`shot` keeps a clone, `ghost` appends it, zoomed like the page because the overlay sits outside the zoomed body; css rules `#card-motion .cm-card .egpts`): a card that arrives (Adapt, Resistance, any draw into the open endgame row) has its points from the first frame of the flight, and a discarded one (Adapt) keeps them until it has left.

### Narrow played-cards drawer: the strip goes under the map again (parkposter.css)
With a zoo's drawer open (side-by-side boards, not expanded) the map slides left over the partner zoo / university strip, which is covered (the bar under the box shows the same). The wide strip of a map with upgrade sets (`association.js`, inline `position:relative; z-index:3` so its pentagons reach over the board) stayed on top of the map: `.zoo.nrw.drawer-open .zooRow > svg.strip { z-index: 1 !important }`. (The rule that hid the bar under the box while the hand tray is open was a misreading and is removed.)

### Room to scroll the bottom clear of the hand, measured (dock.js `fitDockSpace`)
The page's bottom padding is measured instead of estimated: how far the visible frames of the dock reach up from the window's bottom (folded tray = its round buttons, unfolded tray, fan at rest; the cards themselves are not measured, a card in flight is moved by a transform) plus how far anything hangs below the page's last row (`.zoo`, the bar of an open drawer `.assocbar`, the action cards `.actstrip`), + 16 px. Measured again (`queueDockSpace`, one per frame) on every dock drawing, every animationend / transitionend, a size change of `#zoos` or the dock (ResizeObserver), resize and load; skipped while a fan is raised or a new tray is still sliding in. The two CSS `:has(.zoo.drawer-open)` padding rules are gone (the inline padding always overrode them).

### Folded tray: card motions play in place (card-motion.js, card-motion.css)
The workaround that unfolded a folded floating tray for a hand animation and folded it again (`trayFolded`, `measureUnfolded`, `openAndRun`, `foldBack`, `estimate`, `autoOpen` / `autoTimer` / `foldTimer`, `capture.auto`, the `html.dock-nofx` CSS rule) is gone. A folded tray keeps its cards in the page (the frame is only short: the cards reach below the bottom of the window), so `visibleZone()` / `visibleHand()` count a folded tray (`S.handMode === 'tray'`) as a shown row and the normal flights run against the cards' real, folded positions: deck draws, display takes, targeted draws, discards, sunbathing, glide, pouch, pilfer, played cards and projects, the endgame row. The overlay `#card-motion` is cut at the window's edges, so a flying card is cut exactly where the folded tray cuts its cards: it sinks into the tray / rises out of it showing only the part above the edge. Nothing changes for the open tray or the fan (the fan has no folded state). The starting deal still unfolds the hand for good (`beforeRender`, on purpose).
- The frontend polish merge (PR 6, a copy of the frontend from before 2026-10-14..31) overwrote parts of other work in `action-bar.js` (Hypnosis cards, Multiplier icon and `multiplierMode`, Marine Research Expedition `effectOption` buttons), `board.js` (H hexes of maps 8 / 8a), `state.js` (Ark360 study state), `playback.js` (`ownChoice`) and `frames.js` (`verb`): restored by a three-way merge of the original commits onto the new files (the new code of the polish was kept where the two touched the same lines).

### Lone card into the tray: survivors slide with the flight (card-motion.js)
**Survivors close up with the flight (6 November 2026).** When the lone card is the only change of the hand (no cards leaving, no projects), the cards already in the tray no longer slide to their new places *before* the new card appears (when the row was at its widest this sent them left, out of the picture, while nothing had arrived yet). They now slide during the card's flight, from 40 % of it to its end (60 % of the flight time, `ease-in-out`), so the row closes up as the frame's edge opens and the card lands. Everything else (several cards, cards leaving, fan, projects) keeps the old timing (`run()` in `card-motion.js`: `lone`, `slideAt`, `slideMs`).

**The row stays put while the frame opens (6 November 2026, the real cause).** The cards still jumped left when a lone card came in (table 941429583, move 57: the first card cut off at the left edge, then the whole row drifting back right). The survivors' slide was not the cause (here no card changes its slot). The row of cards is centred in the frame (`parkposter.css`, `.handtray .cards { justify-content: center }`) and has its new, wider layout at once, while the frame's width is animated from the old width: a frame narrower than its row lets the row overflow on both sides, so every card stood half the added width to the left and moved back as the frame opened. Now `pinRow()` (`card-motion.js`, called by `openWith()` and by the frame animation of the starting hands) holds the row to the frame's left edge (`justify-content: start` on the row) until the width animation ends or is cancelled; at its end the row fills the frame, so left and centred are the same place. Skipped for a frame that was held at its minimum width (few cards, the round buttons are wider: there the old cards really were centred and the survivors' slide moves them) and for a multi-row tray.

### Played cards with the drawer closed: flights to the "Cards N" chip (card-motion.js, card-motion-plan.js)
With the played-cards drawer closed (setting "Played cards" = drawer, the side-by-side boards) a played card used to fly to the right-middle of the board, often at the height of the hand where it was hardly seen. It now flies to the board's "Cards N" chip on the top edge (`.drbar .drtab`, `drawerChip()` in `card-motion.js`; both players, the other seat's card comes out of its info box as before) and fades out there. The number in the chip (`<b>`) stays at the old count until the card has landed, then shows the new one and pops once (`chipCount()`, `popNumber()`: one pulse of scale and brightness, 600 ms). An open drawer is unchanged (the card flies to its slot).
A card that leaves the played cards (release, expedition; `playedRemoved(before, after)` in `card-motion-plan.js`, test in `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`) comes out of the chip when the drawer is closed, glides to the invisible deck in the trail of bubbles (the same flight as a glided discard) and the number counts down with a pop as it leaves. Nothing flies for a leaving card when the drawer is open, in the tray or under the map (as before). Table 941429583: move 55 (play) and move 208 (release of the White Rhinoceros).




## 139. Saved maps, My maps page, custom map lobbies

- web/map_editor.html / js/map_editor.js / css/map_editor.css: a name field (#mapName), the id of the open map (#mapId), Save map / Save changes and Save as new (save(): POST /api/maps, or PUT /api/maps/<id> when a saved map is open; the url becomes ?map=<id>), a link to My maps; ?map=<id> opens a saved map (openMap, modelFrom turns an export back into the editor's model); errors of the server (a bonus the game cannot play yet, the limit of 3) are shown in the status line; exportObject() is the export as an object. ccount.js is included, so the editor has the login box.
- web/my_maps.html + js/my_maps.js: the list of the account's maps (name, id, date) with Open in the editor, Rename, Delete and Make a new map (hidden at 3 of 3). ccount.js adds a My maps link next to the name.
- web/lobbies.html / js/lobbies.js: the game mode Custom map (added by the script: the options leave it out) shows #mapsel, the account's saved maps (GET /api/maps), and sends map_id; the list rows and the lobby page show the map's name (l.map, iew.map).
- js/board.js (zooBoard): map.custom_terrain (rock / water cells of a map made in the editor) are drawn as the editor's 156 x 135 pieces on map.image (the blank board). js/association.js: map.custom_association (workers / partner zoos / universities) are drawn with onusFill on the strip and under the locked workers instead of the fixed bonuses of the maps of the game. js/zoo.js mapBadge: a custom map's badge is its name.

## 140. Faster start of a replay: the draft opens first, the rest follows

- `web/js/load.js`: the first request asks for a quick view (a replay table: `GET /replay` with header `X-Quick: 1`; a log from the extension / `source=upload`: the upload body has `"quick": true`). The answer holds the action card draft and the starting hand (about 15 steps, 0.1 s on the server instead of the 15-30 s the engine needs for the whole game) and `partial: true`; a replay the server has built already, or the browser has cached (304), comes back whole. `fetchFullReplay()` fetches the real one. Log uploads (replay, fork) are gzipped in the browser (`packed`, CompressionStream; the server unpacks a body that starts with the gzip bytes): 10 MB became about 0.4 MB.
- `web/js/main.js` (`loadRest`): after `init()` of a partial replay, a note "Loading the rest of the game…" shows at the bottom left; when the full replay arrives it replaces `S.replay`, the move list and timeline are rebuilt and `go(step, phase)` keeps the position (the first steps are the same in both). A failure turns the note red.
- `web/import.html`: no `/verify` round trip before the replay opens (the upload verifies the log and the viewer shows the reason when it is refused).

## 141. Step icons paint with their text, Assertion of the other player stays private

- `web/js/log-labels.js`: the icons of a step text arrived a few milliseconds after the text. The line is built anew at every render, so each icon is a fresh `<img>`, and a fresh image is decoded off the main thread even from the cache (an SVG is rasterised on its own too); the text was painted a frame before the picture. Now: `labelImg` asks for `decoding = 'sync'`; `warmLabelIcons()` (called after `init()` in `main.js`) decodes the icon sheet entries, badges and the X token once at idle time and keeps the `Image`s; the strength icon (an SVG file, the slowest one) is inline SVG (`strengthIcon`), which paints in the same frame as the text. `pic()` (icons.js) and the icons of the action bar (`action-bar.js`) ask for `decoding = 'sync'` too.
- `web/js/reveal.js` (`revealAt`): the Assertion lightbox was shown to the player whose point of view it is not (it listed the offered base projects and the one taken). It is hidden information like Perception or Scavenging: `hides(seat)` gives no lightbox, the step is a text frame and the card is drawn face down from the deck into the player's box (the generic hand-count motion, `card-motion-plan.js`). Table 941429583, move 202.
- Engine, `engine/sponsor_extras.py` (Conference on Australia, `enlarge`): an enlarged enclosure that completes an area of map 13 pays the area's bonus, as a new building does. Table 941429583, turn 60 (moves 387-395 were flagged illegal after it). Kept clean by `tests/test_problem_fixes.py`.
- Engine / log translator, map 4 / 4a Commercial Harbor: a card sold at the harbor before the action card is chosen ("card effect (pDiscardCards) outside the Cards action", then "no cleanup"). `replay/actions.py`: the harbor sale (a sale for 3 money of one card) is recognised before the check for card effects outside the Cards action; `replay/differential.py` `_is_free_part`: BGA logs it as a turn of its own before the card choice, so it is merged with the turn that follows like the other free actions; a turn whose action card is skipped keeps the sale in front of the `skip_action`. Tables 627776608 (moves 107, 483) and 688371482 (turn 51; 33 flagged steps before, 0 now), kept clean by `tests/test_problem_fixes.py`.
- Quick view of a replay (`replay/view.py`, `QUICK_MIN_MOVES`): the viewer opens only once at least 20 moves are parsed (it was the draft plus the starting hand, about 10).
- Timeline (`web/js/settings.js` `applyTimeline`): hidden while the replay is partial (the quick view of the first moves), because it would end after a few steps; `loadRest` in `main.js` calls `applyTimeline()` after the full replay is swapped in, so the timeline (if the setting is on) appears with it.

### Unsuccessful Hunter / Scuba Dive (replay)
When the draw step of a Hunter or Scuba Dive reveal is followed by the "discards ... (no animal)" step with nothing kept, the lightbox is shown for 2 s (scaled by the animation speed) and the replay then goes on by itself to the step after the discard (`autoClose` in `web/js/playback.js`). Only when the draw frame was reached going forward; ← and the timeline/log show every frame as before. Not in fork / sandbox / live play / mini games.

### Caribbean Reef Shark: "Shark Attack 1" on one line (small card)
`scripts/cardgen/site/compact.css`: `white-space: nowrap` for the ability title of A544 in the compact rendering (as on BGA). `web/cards/A544.webp` is the current small card edited by hand to the same look until `python scripts/build_cards.py --only A544` is run (the CSS rule was not rendered yet; if the title still wraps after the rebuild, tell the agent).

### Fork "Soon" on the live site (production copy only)
`scripts/deploy_pages.py` `soon_fork` (called by `coming_soon`, so only for the production branch upload): in `replay.html` the Fork button is wrapped in `.soonwrap`, paled out (45 %), not clickable (`pointer-events: none`, no tab stop; `main.js` keeps toggling its `disabled`), and a tooltip opens on hover: "**Soon:** Fork this position and play it for both sides from here" (near-black, light outline, orange "Soon:" label in Park Label). The repository and develop keep the working button; `fork.html` itself is still reachable by its address.

### Enclosure turn (all modes)
A standard enclosure (size-1 to size-5) that stays where it is but changes its side, because an animal moves in (yellow empty side -> green occupied side) or is released (back), turns over on the spot: a turn on a vertical axis through its middle with a small lift and a shadow, 700 ms at speed 1 (the sprite is swapped when the tile is edge-on; a release turns the other way). `buildingFlips` in `web/js/card-motion-plan.js` finds them (same type and anchor in both states, occupied state differs), `buildingTurn` in `web/js/card-motion.js` draws it (called from `runBuildings`, a copy of the building's group in the overlay that holds both sides, the real group is hidden meanwhile). Special enclosures, new / cut-down buildings and size increases are unchanged. Test `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`.

### Building preview hint (replay, fork, sandbox, live)
The black pill "Wheel / R: turn   Right click: stop" is replaced by a night-style tab that hangs from the bottom edge of the map, in the middle of it (`previewBar` in `web/js/zoo.js`, css `.pvbar` in `web/css/parkposter.css`): near-black with a light outline (the look of the Fork tooltip), nothing on the map is covered. One row with two actions: mouse wheel / R turn, right click stops (Shift + R still turns back, it is just not on the tab). The wheel and the right button of the mouse icons are lit in the accent orange and pulse slowly (not with reduced motion). The tab is placed from the measured map, only while a preview runs, and not when the bar under an open drawer is there (that bar has the place). With the played cards under the map the tab covers the top edge of the cards while the preview runs. The error pill for a failed preview is unchanged.

### Sidebar starts on Control
The sidebar remembered the tab that was open last (Control or Log), so a loaded game could open on the Log. It now always starts on Control (`setupSidebar` in `web/js/sidebar.js`); switching afterwards works as before. The Chat tab of the play page is still remembered.

### Sunbathed and pouched cards are private
The Log of the other player's point of view named the sunbathed card ("seowy sells Secretary Bird cards for 4 money"). `_SECRET_DISCARD` in `src/ark_nova/replay/view.py` now also covers the private "You sell ..." line, so a viewer who follows the other player reads "seowy sells 1 card for 4 money" (the number only, as for pouching). The count in the hidden text is grammatical for one card ("pouches 1 card", it was "1 cards"). The card animation of the other player's sunbathing and pouching already shows card backs (the ray / the bag); `listCount` in `web/js/card-motion-plan.js` also reads a plain number ("sells 2 cards") so a hidden line counts right. Test: `tests/test_secret_sell.py`. The server has to be restarted (Python change). The discard pile still lists a sunbathed card like every other discard (unchanged).

### Map badge tooltip: main line only
The tooltip of the map badge (`mapBadge` in `web/js/zoo.js`) shows the title and the main line only. The notes that follow the first sentence in the map data are switched off for every map (`SHOW_MAP_NOTES = false`, the code stays for the overhaul). The main line is now normal weight and 2 px larger (`.mt-main` in `web/css/parkposter.css`: `font-weight: 400; font-size: calc(1em + 2px)`). `MAP_TIP` words the main line of three maps ourselves: 4 / 4a (Commercial Harbor): "If Harbor is connected: Once during your turn, you may discard up to 1 hand card for 3 Money (at any time)." and 13 (Drawing Board): "If you completely cover a quadrant on your map, gain the depicted bonus immediately and as break income." (the card data has the longer text, and "mao" for "map" on map 13; `src/ark_nova/data/maps.json` is generated, so the override is in the viewer).

### Preview picker roomier; Top / bottom slides the lower map down
The picker of the enclosures (`.bpmenu` in `web/css/parkposter.css`) is about 15 % bigger so the pictures are less cramped: tiles 134 x 126 (were 114 x 104), 11 px gap, more padding around each picture (5 px / 10 px), labels 18 px, title 23 px. In the Top / bottom layout the zoo box that shows the preview tab gets `.pvroom` (`margin-bottom: 36px`, set in `renderZoo`) while the preview runs, so the box below slides down and the tab does not cover its name; the other layouts are unchanged.

### Tokens and reduced strength on the action cards of the move bar
The action card buttons of the move bar ("X must choose an action card", `action-bar.js`, all modes) now show what the tracker shows: the tokens that lie on the card (Venom, Constriction, Multiplier; same icons as the tracker's `.actoken`, smaller: `.movepill .abtn.acbtn .actoken` in `web/css/parkposter.css`) at the lower right of the button, and the strength number is reduced by 2 for each Constriction token, never below 1 (as `engine/venom.py strength_penalty` / `engine/game.py`); the tooltip names the strength and the reduction. The hypnosis list (the opponent's first three cards) shows the tokens of those cards too.

### Round / Turn pill and break marker animations
The Round | Turn pill of the top bar never animates (no flash when a new round begins: `flash('round', ..., quiet)` only remembers the number). The break pill no longer changes colour while the marker advances or when a new round resets it (the dark blink is gone; `.hdrstats .flash-down` is removed, the old green wash `.hdrstats .brk.flash-up` stays and is only added when the marker reaches 9). Instead the marker hops (`hopBreak` in `web/js/side-panel.js`, `.pips .mk` in `web/css/parkposter.css`, every mode): one small arc per step (230 ms at speed 1, shortened by `S.animationSpeed`), each dot it leaves turns gold as it lifts off, the number ticks (`.bn.tick`); the green wash starts when the marker lands on 9. A new round puts the first dot back with a pop. Not with reduced motion, and a jump of more than 5 steps (timeline) is drawn at once. The marker is drawn over the dots, which `headerStats` draws in their final state first.

### Break marker: every step is one hop; clearer break-token log line
The marker no longer skips the hops on long moves (the limit of 5 steps is gone): every step is one hop of the same length, so 2 to 9 is seven hops and the green wash starts when it lands on 9. The log line is now "X advances the break token 7 spaces to the last space of the Break track" (below 9: "... 3 spaces (now at 5/9)", singular "1 space").

### Keyboard pop-up shorter
The hint line under the white box is gone. The grey detail line of every key is switched off (`SHOW_KEY_DETAILS = false` in `web/js/shortcuts.js`, the texts stay). The white box sits 24px below the keyboard with the same 24px (padding 20px + border 4px) to the bottom edge. New titles: Tab "Switch between tabs in hand", S "Open settings", R "Rotate building during map preview", H "Show / hide hand", / "Open keyboard tooltips (= this window)", arrows "Go back / forward one step in replay", up/down "Switch to table / graph (post-game only)", Home / End "Jump to first / last step". Shared by the replay and the mini games.

### Keyboard pop-up: light grey and orange, prompt in the title row
The pop-up is a light grey window (#E2E6E7, softer than white) with ink-outlined keys; the keys with a function are a lighter grey (`--keyon`), the picked key and the cursor are orange (`--acc` in `.kbbox`), the picked key keeps ink text. The text of the picked key is a prompt line in the title row, typed letter by letter (instant with reduced motion; a screen reader gets the full text), in JetBrains Mono Bold (`web/fonts/jetbrains-mono-700.woff`, Latin subset, font `Park Mono`). The white box under the keyboard and its CSS are gone. The close button is a key of its own: square keycap with an ink outline, fills and presses down on hover. Shared by the replay and the mini games. The Space key has the normal key font size again (only long labels on narrow keys, like Home, are smaller). A bar separates the key from its text in the prompt line ("Home | Jump to first step"), except for the four arrow keys.

### Break marker: a break from 0 hops once per step, the number waits for the landing
A break that started on 0 (a new round) lost its first hop: 0 to 2 showed one hop, so it looked far too fast next to 2 to 9. Now every step is one hop of the same length, also the first one of a round, which drops onto the first dot from the side of the number (`hopBreak` in `web/js/side-panel.js`). The number in the pill no longer changes at the start: it shows the old value while the marker hops and changes (with the tick) when the marker has landed; the green wash at 9 starts then too.

### Post-game bar: score as 127-125, bold text
The bar at the last step reads "Game over · Xiao Tsong wins 109-104": a hyphen instead of " : ", the winner's score first (a tie shows the same score twice), in the font of the move bar (Nunito Sans bold 20px). The play-mode end line says "Game over: X wins 109-104." instead of "109 to 104" (`resultsSwitch` in `web/js/results.js`, `web/js/play.js`, `.rsswitchtext` in `web/css/parkposter.css`).

### Post-game bar: plain paper bar, indigo Final board button
The bar at the last step has the usual paper colour of the move bar again (the wash of the winner's colour and `--win` are gone). Game stats keeps its green, Final board (on the game stats) is indigo #3F4FA0 (hover #5262B8), a colour used nowhere else in the viewer (`.rsflap.rsgo.rsback`).


## Endgame points in the game stats header, coloured winner in the move bar (replay)
- `web/js/results.js`: `endgamePoints()` = the final score minus the score after the last normal turn (`replay.normal_turns`, the End column of the graph), per seat; `panel()` wraps each score in `.gsw` and hangs `.gseg` under it: an hourglass in the seat colour mixed 70% with white (white #FFF, grey #ADA9A5 and black #6F7B81 are set apart, `hourglassColor`) (`HOURGLASS`, less sand in the lower half) + `+N` between two thin lines, centred under the score and on the line of the Elo chips. Nothing is drawn when the split is unknown (conceded game, no `normal_turns`).
- `web/css/parkposter.css`: `.gsw`, `.gseg` (32px high, 1px lines rgba(214,207,182,.32), hourglass var(--hg) 12x19, value Park Label 500 20px #D6CFB6), `.gshg`.
- The post-game bar text: the winner's name is in the seat colour (`.rsswinner`, `resultsSwitch`).

## The Multiplier is BGA's own picture everywhere
- `web/js/bonusart.js` `multiplier()`: the blue x2 disc on the conservation track and the reputation track is now BGA's icon (`ICON_IDS['bonus:Multiplier']` = r6c8, the same as the placement bonus and the action card token) instead of a drawn gradient disc with text.
- `web/css/parkposter.css`: the Multiplier token on an action card (`.actoken[data-action-token="Multiplier"]`, move bar and tracker) lost the cream circle, outline and shadow: only the disc. Venom and Constriction keep their frame.

## 142. Without BGA premium the maps are read off BGA's replay (extension 1.5.0)
- BGA hides the end-of-game stats from players without premium: the table info's `result.stats.player.map` reads `*masked*` and the replay build crashed (`KeyError: '*masked*'`, 500 on `/api/tables/{id}/upload`). `storage/tableinfo.py` `maps_from_tableinfo` leaves unknown maps out, `maps_masked` says when BGA hid them, `replay/config.py` `game_from_log` ignores any map id that is no map (the log then tells the maps).
- Extension 1.5.0 (`extension/button.js`): when the table info masks the maps, the helper tab (BGA's replay page of a player, as for the base projects) reads each player's map from `.zoo-map-board-background[data-map]` (the player is found from an id / data attribute of the zoo around it), stores it as `ark:maps:<table>` and the popup waits for it (`isReady`). `importer.js` / `button.js` send `maps: {player id: map}` with the log; `web/import.html` keeps it in `LogStore` (`maps:<table>`), `web/js/load.js` sends it with the upload and the fork, `api/main.py` `_clean_maps` / `_record_for_upload` use it as `player_maps` when the table is not indexed. The extension also lost `http://localhost/import*` from its content script matches.

## 143. Who is on the site: an anonymous heartbeat (web/js/presence.js, web/online.html)
- `web/js/presence.js` (started by `main.js` after `init()`, so replay, fork, play, sandbox and the mini games): a random id in `localStorage` (`ark:visitor`) is sent with the page kind to the Worker (`POST <worker>/presence`, found through `GET /api/presence/config`) every minute, only while the tab is on show and the person did something (pointer, key, wheel, touch) in the last 15 minutes; coming back to the tab beats at once. `window.ARK_NO_PRESENCE` switches it off.
- `cloudflare/src/presence.ts`: the `Presence` Durable Object (migration v4 in both wrangler files) keeps when each id was last seen, counts the ids of the last 3 minutes and takes a sample every 5 minutes while anybody is there (kept 14 days). Cloud Run is not involved in a heartbeat, so it neither wakes nor restarts it, and a deploy loses nothing.
- `GET /api/admin/online?hours=` (header `X-Admin-Secret` = `INTERNAL_SECRET`) -> the Worker's signed `/internal/presence/online`; `web/online.html` shows it (not linked from the site). Tests `cloudflare/test/presence.test.ts`, `tests/test_presence.py`.


## 144. A kept log opens without the upload; a review page opened directly (extension 1.6.0)
- `GET /api/tables/{id}/kept` says whether the log is already in GCS (`all_games_logs/<table>.txt`, `Contributions.has/read`). `web/js/load.js` (`keptOnServer`) asks before an `source=upload` replay or fork; when it is kept, the body of `/upload` and `/fork` has `kept: true` and no log (the server reads the copy, which was verified when it was kept). A 409 `not_kept` makes the page send the log as before. Nothing is kept again from a kept open.
- Extension 1.6.0: the button also shows when the review page (`/gamereview`) was opened directly, so no table info (`tableinfos.html`) was captured. `button.js` `loadPair` then has `index: null` and `needsMaps: true`: the maps are read off BGA's replay page like for `*masked*`. `importer.js` and `web/import.html` accept a missing table info (an earlier `index:<table>` in the LogStore is cleared).

## 145. A kept table opens like a logged one (cached); the Elo of a review page opened directly (extension 1.7.0)
- The BigQuery appendix (`freestyle-190711.ark_nova.base_projects_appendix`) has the columns `maps`, `elos`, `elos_after`, `elo_changes` (JSON text) and `same_map`, `marine_worlds` (added with `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN`; rows from before have none). `Contributions.keep` writes them with the log; a log that is kept already gets a newer row when its table has none with the maps (the rows are append-only, `record()` reads the newest one that has `maps`).
- `GET /api/tables/{id}/kept` answers `{kept, complete}`; `complete` = the log and a row with the maps exist. `GET /replay` and the fork then take such a table like a logged one (`_kept_record`, `gcs_path` `kept:<id>`): the same replay cache and ETag (the tag carries a digest of the row), nothing is sent by the page (`web/js/load.js`). A table kept without the maps goes the upload way as before (`kept: true`, no log in the body).
- Without the table info (the review page was opened directly) the extension also reads the players' Elo off BGA's replay page (`#player_elo_<id>`, falls back to `.gamerank_value` in the player panel; checked on a real rated and a friendly page, scripts/recon/bga_recon.py; the replay opens without it after `ELO_GRACE` 8 s). The server brings the number to the index's scale (minus 1300), and `view.py` works out the change of the game with K=20 from the result (`storage/tableinfo.py estimate_elo_changes`; `elo_estimated: true` in the view). Measured on a real game: the replay page shows the Elo from before the game (737 before, 739 after, the page showed 737), as the estimate assumes.
- `TableRecord.marine_worlds` is `None` for an upload with maps read off the replay and no table info: the log tells whether it is Marine Worlds.- (Superseded by the next bullet: BGA does show an Elo in a friendly game.) When all the player panels are on the page and none has an Elo after `NO_ELO_AFTER` (2 s), `button.js` stores `{none: true}` under `ark:elo:<table>` and goes on at once (no 8 s wait); the replay then shows no Elo and no change.
- `scripts/recon/bga_replay_elo.js`: paste it into the console of a BGA replay page (a rated and a friendly game); it prints and copies a JSON report of where the Elo is (`#player_elo_<id>`, `.gamerank_value`, anything rating-like in the player panels, short numbers, `gameui.gamedatas`), what the extension's lookup would read, and the zoo boards with the elements around them (the map's owner lookup). Use it to confirm or fix the selectors of `readElos` / `readMaps`.
- A friendly game DOES show an Elo on BGA's replay (its pre-game rating), but is not rated: the replay page's own data has table option 201 "Game mode" (1 = Friendly, 2 = Arena, 0/else normal; `"id":201,"name":"Game mode","value":1` in an inline script of the game frame). `friendlyMode()` in `button.js` reads it; a friendly table stores `{none: true}` and the Elo is not used. `scripts/recon/bga_recon.py` (Playwright, logs in once, saves the review and replay pages and the recon report of tables you name) produced these measurements.
- CHANGED (extension 1.7.0, before release): the Elo is no longer read off BGA's replay page or estimated (BGA keeps decimals, a K=20 guess can be off, and a friendly game shows an Elo although it is not rated). A review page opened directly now opens BGA's table page in a background tab (`/table?table=<id>`, helper tab like the replay one): capture.js sees `tableinfos.html` there, button.js stores it as `ark:index:<table>` and sends `table-read`, background.js closes the tab. The table info (Elo before / after, change, result) is available with or without premium; only the maps may be masked and are still read off the replay. The popup waits `INDEX_GRACE` (15 s) for it at most, then the replay opens without it as before. Removed: `readElos`, `friendlyMode`, the `ark:elo:` storage, `elos` in the import message, `LogStore` key `elos:`, `elos` in the upload / fork bodies, `estimate_elo_changes`, `elo_estimated` in the view. Kept: the appendix columns `elos`, `elos_after`, `elo_changes` (now from the real table info).

## 146. Board Design Workshop maps (BDW1 - BDW4)
- `web/maps/map-BDW<n>.jpg` (1122 x 976, the printed bonuses and flags painted out) and the map view of a BDW map (`replay/view.py map_view`: `custom_association` like a map-editor map, `full_map_appeal`, `bdw: true`): `board.js` writes `map.full_map_appeal` on the full-map plate (5 on BDW3), `association.js` `slotGlyph` draws the new notepad spaces (`bonus-kiosk-pavilion` = BGA's 3x pentagon r6c3, `size-5` = the five hexes, `border-capacity` = a blue square with +1; `project-support` uses the shield card r11c6) and `icons.js` has `bonus:project-support` / `bonus:bonus-kiosk-pavilion`. The map pick of a live game and the sandbox map list include the maps only when the game asked for them (checkbox 'Include BDW map pack' in `play.html` and `sandbox.html`; `S.replay.bdw_maps`; sandbox list entries have `bdw: true` and are named without 'Map ').


Rewording of the mouse view (7 November 2026): the view is titled "Mouse controls"; a tooltip is one text in the mono font (`.rctip p`) whose first words "Right click" are bold and orange (`.rctip p b`), no title line; texts: "Right click on any map to select any standard enclosure and preview its map placement", "Right click on a sponsor building card in hand to preview its map placement", "Right click on any point of the timeline to jump to the beginning of its round". A right click anywhere in the open keyboard pop-up opens the view (`modal` contextmenu in `shortcuts.js`; only a left click on the backdrop closes the pop-up); the map's epicentre lies at the very top of the map.

## Card names as BGA calls them (all modes)
`data_manual/card_names.json` lists the cards whose name in the fan site's data differs from the `card_name` of BGA's own logs (found by comparing every card of the sample logs): A428 NORTHERN GIRAFFE, A439 LLAMA, A467 ECUADORIAN SQUIRREL MONKEY (typo fixed; the `bga_id` keeps BGA's own spelling `...SquirellMonkey`, it is only used to match), A475 INDIAN COBRA (was KING COBRA), A547 MEDITERRANEAN RAINBOW WRASSE, A548 SHORT-SNOUTED SEAHORSE with the Latin name Hippocampus hippocampus (was LINED SEAHORSE / Hippocampus erectus; the photo is still the old one), S221 ARCHAEOLOGIST. The file is applied in three places: `src/ark_nova/data/animals.json`, `sponsors.json`, `animal_play_effects.json`, `sponsor_play_effects.json` (the names the viewer shows), `scripts/import_data.py` (`NAME_FIXES`, so a re-import keeps them) and the card pictures (`named()` in `scripts/cardgen/harness/entry.tsx`, like the S274_VC name). The card images of these seven cards (`web/cards`, `web/cards_large`, and the no-snake variants `A475_ns` / `A475_sw`) are rebuilt (MEDITERRANEAN RAINBOW WRASSE and ECUADORIAN SQUIRREL MONKEY are shrunk a little to fit the stripe by `shoot.mjs`). The server has to be restarted (Python data).

### Brahminy Kite photo mirrored (all modes)
`scripts/cardgen/site/img/animals/A560_BrahminyKite.jpg` was the fan site's photo with the bird flying to the right; BGA's card has it the other way round (wing up on the right, head on the left, away from the icons at the top). The picture file itself is now flipped (re-saved at JPEG quality 92), there is no flip in the code. The card images `A560` (small and large) are rebuilt. A refresh of the fan site's `site/img` would bring the old picture back; flip it again then.

### Guineafowl Puffer photo mirrored; ability icons lifted into the photo (all modes)
- `scripts/cardgen/site/img/animals/A540_GuineafowlPuffer.jpg` is flipped (BGA's fish faces right, the fan site's faced left), like the Brahminy Kite.
- The Venom / Hypnosis / Constriction / Pilfering octagon on the photo of an animal is not part of the photo: the fan site's CSS puts it at `top: 213px` (70 px high, 14 px above the stripe) for its 297 px photo. With our 260 px photo (`layout.mjs` PHOTO) its lower part was under the name stripe. `uniform.mjs` now puts it at `top: PHOTO - 14 - 70` = 176 px (`site/uniform.css` is generated, a rebuild keeps it). 16 cards redrawn (large and small): A449, A456, A458, A463, A470, A474, A475, A482, A483, A485, A492, A534, A537, A538, A540, A555.
- The no-snake variants (`_ns` / `_sw`) do not show the icon, they are unchanged.

### Ability octagon kept in Slow Worm / missing-photo mode (all modes)
- `scripts/build_nosnake_images.py`: the Venom / Hypnosis / Constriction / Pilfering octagon (centre 635, 422, apothem 72 in large-card pixels) is now in the kept shapes (`ICON_OCTAGON`, `HAS_OCTAGON`), so the `_ns` / `_sw` images of A470, A474, A475, A482, A483, A485 and A492 show it (it was painted over by the new photo). Images rebuilt (small and large).
- No strip of the old photo next to the requirement badges any more (A474 and especially A475 in missing-photo mode): `build_nosnake_images.py` no longer keeps a 154 px wide rectangle there but, row by row, the red bar plus the circle of each badge (`bars_shapes`), and the grey panel's right edge is measured per card (`panel_right`, 131 on the Indian Cobra). Needs only Pillow. All `_ns` / `_sw` images of the 7 snakes with an octagon rebuilt; the others are unchanged.

### Pop-ups on very wide windows: no more cut-off Settings, no page scrolling behind a pop-up (all modes)
- Reported on a 34" ultrawide: the bottom of the Settings pop-up was out of reach and the mouse wheel scrolled the page behind it. Reproduced (Board tab: 3440x1300 box bottom 1387 px, 3840x1200 1524 px; the page scrolled 800 px). Cause: the page is zoomed to the window width, so a wide window has a short layout height, and the gap above the Settings box (`padding-top: 14vh`, raw vh) grows with the zoom; the pop-up layer could not scroll itself, so the wheel went to the page.
- `table.css` `.modal` (all pop-ups of the four viewer pages): `overflow-y: auto; overscroll-behavior: contain; align-items: safe center` — a pop-up taller than the window scrolls by itself; while it fits nothing changes.
- `parkposter.css` `#settingsModal`: `padding-top: min(14vh, max(12px, 100 --vh - --settall (604 px: the Board tab of the replay + shadow) - 12px))`: the old 14vh whenever the box fits below it, else the gap shrinks.
- New `web/js/modalscroll.js` (imported by settings.js): while a pop-up is open (`.modal` not hidden, `.modalback`), the wheel scrolls only something inside it that can scroll that way, never the page (done on the wheel event, not with `overflow: hidden` on `<html>`, which would remove the window's scrollbar and shift the page). Ctrl + wheel is left alone. `onScrollbar()`: a click on the pop-up's own scrollbar does not close it (settings.js, shortcuts.js). The dropdown lists and the ? note of the settings add the pop-up's `scrollTop`.
- Checked in Chromium (real replay page): Settings pop-up (3 tabs) pixel-identical before / after at 1280x720, 1366x768, 1536x864, 1920x1080, 2560x1440; shortcuts and point-of-view pop-ups unchanged apart from the usual run-to-run noise of the board behind; the only change there: the wheel no longer moves the page behind a pop-up (it did by 63-127 px). 3440x1300: box now fully visible (top 196, bottom 1257); 3840x1000: the pop-up scrolls, the page stays, dropdown and note sit at their button; backdrop click still closes, scrollbar click does not.

### Extension: no localhost permission in the manifest
- `extension/manifest.json`: removed `http://localhost/import*` from the `importer.js` content script (a development leftover; it made browsers list localhost among the extension's permissions). Only affects the automatic import (`?ark=auto`) against a local dev server; the click path never needed it. Version number unchanged (left to the extension's release).

### An enclosure that grows turns over in place (all modes)
- Conference on Australia (and any other growth of a standard enclosure): BGA and the engine replace the enclosure by a bigger one, often with another anchor hex and rotation (table 941429583: move 284 size-2 -> size-3, rotation 5 -> 0; move 394 size-3 -> size-4, anchor 4|9 -> 4|11). The viewer showed that as "one cut down, one new": the old enclosure flew to the invisible deck while the new one flew out of it. Now it turns over on the spot into the bigger one, the same turn as when an animal moves in. That turn (fill, release and growth alike, `MS` in `turnOver`) now takes 1000 ms at the default animation speed 1x (it was 700 ms; the animation speed setting still divides it).
- `card-motion-plan.js` `buildingGrowths(before, after)`: an enclosure that disappeared, paired with a bigger standard enclosure of the same player that appeared and covers all of its cells (any bigger size: a jump over two growths gives size-2 -> size-4). Tests in `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`.
- `card-motion.js`: `turnOver(wasG, nowG, ...)` is the turn of `buildingTurn`, generalised to two groups (the invisible ghost of the old enclosure, board.js `cm-leaving`, and the new one); each copy keeps its own screen matrix, the turn axis is the middle of both pictures together. Paired buildings are taken out of the flights; anything unpaired (no cells, a board that is not drawn) flies as before.
- Checked in Chromium on the real replay: both growths of table 941429583, and 19 made-up ones injected into it (1 -> 2, 2 -> 3, 3 -> 4, 4 -> 5, every rotation of the old enclosure, with another anchor or rotation for the new one): one turn each, no flights, the new enclosure shown at the end. The turn of an animal moving in is unchanged.

### Setting "Animations" (All / Only standard / None) and a new animation speed list (all modes)
- Settings, Control tab, a new first row **Animations** (above Animation speed): a three-part switch in one frame, green = All (default), yellow = Only standard, red = None, icons sparkles / arrow / barred circle, the words are the tooltips (`data-tip`; css `.setthree`, `.setthree-opt` in `parkposter.css`, built in `settings.js`; radio group, arrow keys move). The row's icon tile is a sky-blue play circle (`ICONS.anim`), so it shares no colour with the switch; the Timeline tile is green now, so the Control tab has sky blue, gold, lavender, green and salmon tiles. The Endgame live count shield (`ICONS.shield`) is drawn after the Ark Nova conservation shield: gabled top, notched shoulders, round body to a point. Saved as `animations` in `localStorage.settings`, kept in `S.animations` (`state.js`).
- **Only standard**: no thematic animations. Sunbathing, gliding and pouching play like a plain discard, the targeted draw like a plain draw from the deck: `card-motion-plan.js` `plainPlan` (the followed player's `movementPlan`) and `plainOther` (the other seat's `otherPlan` counts), applied in `card-motion.js` `afterRender` and in the zone tabs (`standard(...)`). The wave (switched off, `WAVE_ANIMATION`) will be thematic too: add it to the two helpers when it comes.
- **None**: nothing moves, like the system setting "reduce motion": `card-motion.js` `reduced.matches` is now `motionOff()` (new `motion.js`: system setting or `S.animations === 'none'`), also read by the dock intro (`dock.js`), the drawer / zoo animations (`zoo.js`) and the break marker hop (`side-panel.js`); a running animation is stopped when the switch is moved to None. The CSS-only entrance animations of the page are not switched off.
- Animation speed: steps are now .25 / .5 / 1 / 1.25 / 1.5 / 1.75 / 2 (3x removed; a saved 3x becomes 2x).
- Tests: `plainPlan` / `plainOther` in `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`. Checked in Chromium on the real replay (941429583): the Glide step (move 442) shows the bubble trail with All, plain cards with Only standard and no flight with None; the choice survives a reload; the pop-up shows the tooltips.
- The knobs of the Settings sliders (animation speed, autoplay speed, hand card size; all modes) are wider and taller (76 x 38 px, `--knob`; were 50 x 32) so "0.25x" fits. The tooltips of the Animations switch read "All (full animation pack)", "Standard only (no detailed thematic animations)" and "None (all actions happen instantly)"; they had been clipped by an `overflow: hidden` on the switch frame, which is gone.
- Slider knobs: the rail is now drawn by the track (`.settrack::before`) and is `--inset` (9 px) longer than the knob's travel on each side, so the knob no longer touches the ends at the lowest and highest step (the range input is narrower by the same amount, `settings.js` `showVal` adds the inset to the number's position); the number in the knob is lowered 1 px to sit in the middle. The tooltips of the Animations switch are centred and have a line break after the name ("Standard only" / "(no detailed thematic animations)", likewise All and None; `white-space: pre-line`).
- Slider rail: on the highest step the colour runs on to the end of the rail (nothing white is left behind the knob); the tooltips of the Animations switch are centred on their field (the last one keeps to the frame's right edge so it stays inside the pop-up).
- Tooltip of the Animations switch: near-white (#F4F4F2) with the 4 px ink outline and the 5 px offset ink shadow of the switch frame and the knob (it had been black and merged with the black slider knob below it).

### Cubes in special enclosures: as many as BGA shows, also spread over several aquariums (all modes)
- An animal in a special enclosure covers as many spaces with cubes as its special enclosure size says (Humphead Wrasse, Aquarium 2: 2 cubes; Aquarium 0 such as Zooplankton: none; table 944055156 move 37 showed 1), and the aquariums of a zoo share their spaces, so an aquarium animal can have its cubes in several aquariums (African Penguin 853899676 move 465: 1 in the large, 1 in the small aquarium).
- Engine: `Building.cubes` ({card key: cubes in this building}, `engine/state.py`; the animal itself still lives in one building, `animals`) kept by the new `engine/cubes.py`: `place` (playing an animal; without the log the chosen aquarium is filled first, then the other aquariums in the order they were built), `release`, `move` (Bonus move into a new reptile house / aviary), `view`. Called from `animals_action.play`, `project_effects.release_animal`, `bonuses` (move_in), Reconstruction keeps them (`sponsor_extras` placeback, `effects`). The cubes change no rule (the aquariums' spaces were already counted together). A state saved before (live tables, a fork posted by the browser) has no cubes: `cubes.view` gives each animal its size.
- Replay: BGA's `state` of a special enclosure in buyAnimal / releaseAnimal / moveAnimal is its number of cubes after the event (checked: every placement of the 14 sample logs). `builder.cube_totals` reads them; the log-built states (`h_buy_animal`, `h_release_animal`, `h_move_animal`) and the engine (`replay/actions.py` passes them as the log-only argument `cubes`, which `differential._plain` and `animals_action.play` leave out of the legality check) put the cubes where BGA has them. BGA's cubes belong to no animal: a release that BGA shows in another aquarium moves the cubes of the other animals (`cubes.sync`).
- View: `state_view` gives every special enclosure `cubes` = [[card key, cubes], ...]; `board.js` draws one cube per space in turn (an older view without `cubes`: one per animal).
- Checked on all 14 sample logs: at the end of every move, every special enclosure of the view holds exactly BGA's number of cubes (8,845 checks, log-built and engine-played steps, the 5 spread placements included: 714192218 m314, 853899676 m465, 927683584 m489, 931947993 m475, 941429583 m436); the engine replays exactly the same steps as before. Tests `tests/test_animal_cubes.py`.
- Fork / sandbox / live play: the player chooses the aquarium, the engine spreads what does not fit (a split by choice, such as BGA's 1 + 1 when one aquarium has room for both, cannot be asked for).

## Free universities and partner zoos in the post-game stats (all modes)
- Replay stats (`results.js` `countStats`): "Universities" and "Partner zoos" now count every tile that arrives on a player's board: the slide of the tile (`slideMeeples` with a `partner-*` / `fac-*` meeple into `partner_n` / `university_n`; its own log line is empty, the text "takes a new university" belongs to the worker of the task). That adds the free tiles of 5 / 8 conservation points, of the end of the reputation track and the placement bonuses of the map (the 14 sample logs hold 10 of them). "Reputation" stays the 2-reputation task only: the reputation of a conservation / placement / track bonus is a bonus, not a slide, and replaces no action.
- Live stats (`engine/gamestats.py` `assoc`): `association.take_tile` (every free tile) counts the tile as well, the 2 reputation of a bonus does not. Test `tests/test_free_tile_stats.py`.
- Checked in the browser on 944055156 (player 2: partner zoos 1 -> 2) and 853899676: the panel equals the tiles in the log (3 / 3 and 2 / 2; 1 / 1 and 1 / 3).

## Log search bar: the X set apart, count and arrows one block (all modes)
- `log-search.js`: the hit count and the two arrows are wrapped in one `span.ls-grp`; `parkposter.css`: that block is a pale grey-blue (`#E7EEEF`) square-cornered area with a 2px soft inset edge (no pill), the arrows in teal, the clear X in grey (`#9AA4A6`, ink on hover) and outside the block. With nothing typed there is no block; with a query without hits the block holds just the `0/0` (the arrows are hidden as before).

## Shark Attack animation (all modes, Animations = All)
- New module `web/js/shark-motion.js`, called from `card-motion.js` `runDisplay` when the step's text names a Shark Attack (`SHARK_ATTACK`: replay "X discards A for the Shark Attack effect", live "X removes A from the display (shark attack)", fork "X shark Attack 2, cards ...") and cards left the display into the discard pile. Only standard and None keep the plain discard / no motion.
- One card: front-facing jaws come up out of the card's lower edge and bite twice; each bite cuts a toothed half-moon out of the card (`bitePolygon`, a clip-path on the card image), shakes it, throws white-and-ink ripple rings and a few small blue drops; then the card is pulled down into its own slot (the card sits in a clip box the size of its slot: the table is the water). 2.3 s at speed 1.
- Two or more cards: one fin runs along the lower edge of the whole display, from left of the first folder to right of the last, always in front of every card, with a thin blue wake and a trail of ripples; each chosen card is pulled under as the fin passes its middle (`sharkTimeline`). No water surface, no appeal pop.
- Sizes follow the card on screen (`u` = card width / 130.25); the rest of the display (cards sliding left, the refill) waits until the attack is over. `givenTo` no longer reads the live line "... from the display (shark attack)" as a card given to that player.
- Tests `tests/shark-motion.test.mjs` (`node --test`). The mock it was made from: the "Shark Attack: ripples with a touch of water" artifact.

## Shark Attack: fangs on the gum (all modes, Animations = All)

- `web/js/shark-motion.js` `JAWS` (Two bites, Shark Attack with one card): the teeth were placed on a sine curve that is not the mouth's edge, so they hung on the grey head and below it. Now the mouth is the quadratic arch M0 (22, 70) - M1 (60, 18) - M2 (98, 70) of the viewBox 0 0 120 70 and `fangs()` puts 7 teeth on it between t = 0.1 and 0.9: the bases touch each other, sit 1.2 units under the edge, the sides are gently curved (two quadratic curves) and the tips lean towards the middle; the middle teeth are longer (12 x (0.7 + 0.3 sin(pi t))). A thin ink line (2.4) along the mouth's edge, drawn over the teeth, ties them in. Head, eyes, colours and the motion are unchanged. Test: `tests/shark-motion.test.mjs` (the bases are on the arch and touch, the tips point into the mouth).

## Shark Attack: higher leap, taller mouth with a soft point (all modes, Animations = All)

- `web/js/shark-motion.js` (Shark Attack with one card): the jaws were a flat parabola (viewBox 120 x 70) that was also squashed to .55 height at each bite, so the mouth looked squashed. Now the viewBox is 120 x 90 (`JAW_W` / `JAW_H`), the head is a tall arch with a soft point (`M6 90 C9 54 36 20 60 9 C84 20 111 54 114 90`) and the mouth is a tall arch of two quadratic curves (corners (22, 90) / (98, 90), controls (29, 44) / (91, 44)) meeting at the apex (60, 28) at a gentle angle, not a pyramid (`qpt`, `qnorm`); the fangs are spaced along the edge (see "even teeth at the apex"); the eyes sit at (24, 50) / (96, 50). The bite squashes to .74 instead of .55.
- The leap: the jaws rise `jh * 1.42` (was 1.05) of their own height, about 35 % higher: the top of the head reaches the card's name bar. The rise takes 340 ms (was 300), so one bite is 900 ms (was 830) and the whole animation 2420 ms (was 2280): bites close at 450 / 1350 ms, the card goes under at 1800 ms (`sharkTimeline`, `runShark`). The fin pass of Shark Attack 2 is unchanged. Tests: `tests/shark-motion.test.mjs` (8 fangs on the edge; a symmetric tall arch, gentle at the top, steep at the corners).

## Shark Attack: even teeth at the apex (all modes, Animations = All)

- `web/js/shark-motion.js` `fangs()`: with 8 teeth the apex fell between teeth 4 and 5 and their tips, leaning towards the middle, crossed into a thin sliver. Now there are 7 teeth (15 long, the middle ones longer: 0.72 + 0.28 sin), spaced evenly by length along the mouth's edge (`tAt`, an arc-length table); the middle tooth is centred on the apex and points straight down; every tooth points between the edge's inward normal and straight down (`down` .7, a slight lean `curve` .25 towards the middle), so the teeth near the apex stand side by side; each tooth's base follows the edge (through the apex for the middle one), so no gap opens between a tooth and the gum. `JAWS` is exported (to draw it alone). Test: `tests/shark-motion.test.mjs`.

## Shark Attack: the bite marks match the jaws (all modes, Animations = All)

- Timing: the clip-path animation of the bitten card ran for `L + 700` ms while its keyframe offsets were computed for `L` (the jaws' clock), so the first mark appeared ~175 ms and the second ~525 ms after the jaws closed. It now runs on the jaws' clock (duration `L`), so each mark appears in the frame the jaws close (450 / 1350 ms).
- Place and size: the fixed half-moons (`bitePolygon`, removed) were smaller than the mouth and the second one was off to the right (72 % of the card while the jaws closed at 67 %). `biteHole(bites, cw, ch)` cuts the hole from the jaws' real pose when they close (box `left`, `top`, `w`, `h`, translation `tx` / `ty` = up + 8u, `sy` = .74 about the box centre): the mouth's opening up to the gum line (`BITE_ROW`, from the same `teeth()` data the drawing uses), with a small notch (`NOTCH` 4.5 units) above every tooth, down to the card's lower edge. Two overlapping bites merge into one outline (for every x the higher hole wins), so it never crosses itself.
- The head now goes on below its mouth (the head path runs down to y 170, past the card's edge, which clips it; the mouth's bottom is closed by the ink gum line), so no strip of the hole shows under the shark while it bites. `teeth()` returns the tooth data, `fangs()` draws it. Tests: `tests/shark-motion.test.mjs` (the hole follows the gum where the jaws close, a notch above the apex, as wide as the mouth, two bites never cross).

## Shark Attack: only the cone of the head shows (all modes, Animations = All)

- `web/js/shark-motion.js` `runShark` (one card): the jaws rose `jh * 1.42`, so the bottom of the mouth (the gum line closing it) and the grey below it came up into the card. They now rise `jh + 6u`: at the top of the leap and at the bite (`ty` = up + 8u, `sy` .74) the mouth's lower edge stays just below the card's edge (clipped), so only the cone with the open mouth shows; that is still about 30 % higher than the first version (1.05 x the old, shorter jaws). The bite holes follow automatically (`biteHole` uses the same pose): the open cone down to the card's edge. Test: `tests/shark-motion.test.mjs` (the mouth's bottom stays below the card's edge at both zooms).


## Faded cards: solid grey (all modes)

- Faded cards had too little contrast with the (now lighter) dim of the spotlight lightbox: they were 60-75 % opaque, so the dark teal dim and the board lines showed through. They are now fully opaque and plain grey, only a little darker:
  - `web/css/parkposter.css` `.draftcard.unavailable` (reveal: the effect cannot take the card): `grayscale(1) brightness(.82) contrast(.95)`, `opacity: 1` (was brightness .5, opacity .75).
  - `web/css/replay.css` `.draftcard.passed` (action card draft, not kept): `grayscale(.9) brightness(.84)`, `opacity: 1` (was opacity .6).
  - `web/css/replay.css` `.card.dim` (unplayable display / hand cards) uses the same fade, `grayscale(1) brightness(.86)`, `opacity: 1` (was opacity .35), so the board and the lightboxes stay consistent; `web/js/card-motion.js` (the flying copy of a dimmed card) matches.
- Not changed: the dim of the lightbox itself, `.hwrest .draftcard` and `.adaptpop .draftcard.out` (both already close to opaque).

## Reveal lightbox: at most 5 cards in a row (all modes)

- `web/js/reveal.js` `perRow(effect, n)`: Perception, Hunter, Scuba Dive and Scavenging reveals with more than 5 cards are split into rows of at most 5, evenly, the longer row first: 6 = 3 + 3, 7 = 4 + 3, 8 = 4 + 4, 9 = 5 + 4, 10 = 5 + 5, 11 = 4 + 4 + 3. They reuse the Assertion layout (`.assertpop` with `--per`, card width from the row length), in the replay (`showReveal`) and in fork / live play (`revealEffect`). 5 cards or fewer stay in one row as before. Assertion (always two rows), Horse Whisperer, Adapt, Resistance and the action card draft keep their own logic.

## Move bar: a reveal draw names only the number of cards; effect names capitalised (all modes)

- `web/js/frames.js` `shortDraw(text)`, used by the bar in `web/js/main.js`: "X draws A, B, C ... for Hunter effect" (Perception, Hunter, Scuba Dive, Scavenging) reads "X draws 8 cards for Hunter effect" in the move bar, everything else of the sentence is kept; the live owner's "You draw ..." is shortened the same way, an opponent's "X draws 3 cards for ..." (live, already a number) is left as is. The names of the cards are in the reveal lightbox, and the Log (the step's own label) keeps every card. The card motion still reads the full text (`frameText()` is unchanged).
- `web/js/pov.js` `statueName` (applied by `labelOf`, so to the bar, the move list / Log in the replay, fork and live): "for hunter effect" is shown as "for Hunter effect", likewise Perception, Scavenging and Scuba Dive. The text of the step itself (backend, `bgalog.py`) is unchanged.
- The keep line of these effects too: "X keeps Lesser Flamingo and discard A, B, C ... for Hunter effect" reads "X keeps Lesser Flamingo and discards 8 cards for Hunter effect" (the kept card by name, the discards as a count, "discards" with the s; one discard reads "1 card"; a line that already has counts stays). `KEPT` in `shortDraw`; the Log keeps the full text. The live Perception line has no "for ... effect" ending and is not shortened.
- Not touched: Assertion ("X keeps <project> with Assertion"), Horse Whisperer ("X draws <card> from discard for Horse Whisperer effect") and the other draws.

## Side wheel of the mouse steps through the moves (all modes)

- `web/js/main.js`: a horizontal wheel event (the tilt / side wheel of a mouse, a trackpad's sideways swipe, or Shift + the wheel) does what ← / → do: right = `stepBy(1)`, left = `stepBy(-1)`, one step per notch (small deltas are summed to 30 and paced to one step per 140 ms). Ignored while the settings / keyboard / viewpoint dialogs are open, in text fields, in the mini games, with Ctrl / Alt / Meta, and when the element under the pointer scrolls sideways itself (a tray of cards). The event is cancelled so a sideways swipe is not the browser's back / forward. The plain vertical wheel is unchanged. `web/js/shortcuts.js`: the ← / → details mention it.

## Player panel: no 0 on an empty icon slot (all modes)

- `web/css/parkposter.css` `.ppicons .ic.zero b`: the count circle of a continent / animal type the player has none of is hidden (was shown at 55 % opacity); the badge stays at 30 % so the grid of slots does not move. The tooltip (title) of the slot is unchanged.

## Lagoon (map 14): the starting person sponsors are steps of their own (all modes; replay data + viewer)

- How Lagoon's start differs: besides the 8 cards and 2 endgame cards, each player gets a person sponsor searched out of the deck (log: "X draws Spokesperson with <SPONSOR-PERSON> (Map 14 effect)", in the same BGA move as the deal), so the hand has 9 cards and 5 are kept after the 4 discards. Before, the sponsor was folded into each player's deal step: the bar read "A draws 8 cards and 2 endgame cards · A draws Spokesperson ..., B draws ...", and the deal of each player ran together with a search animation that showed the wrong card (the first new card of the hand).
- `src/ark_nova/replay/view.py` `_split_keep_and_draw`: on Lagoon the move is now five steps: the keep, the deal of seat 0 (8 + 2, without the sponsor), the deal of seat 1 (both merged into one viewer step as before, `merge_group` "deal": "A and B draw 8 cards and 2 endgame cards"), then "A draws Spokesperson (Map 14 effect)" and "B draws Expert On Europe (Map 14 effect)", one step each (`merge_group` "person0" / "person1", `log_only`). The states in between have the sponsor still in the deck (at its place in the known order) and not in the hand; the last step has the move's own state (and its engine object). Other maps are unchanged. Restart the server (`Start Ark Nova.bat --reload`).
- `web/js/playback.js` `personChain`: reached forward from the deal with animations on, the replay plays on by itself through the person sponsor steps (each starts once the cards of the step before have landed, plus 350 ms); not during autoplay, not with Animations = None, stopped by any other move. Back, the timeline, the Log and the jump box show each step on its own.
- `web/js/card-motion.js`: a search holds the bar on the text of the step before until the card is face-up; the text kept for the next step is now the step's own text, not the held one (two searches in a row showed the deal text twice).
- `web/js/pov.js` `labelOf`: " with <SPONSOR-PERSON>" is left out of every line (bar and Log): "X draws Spokesperson (Map 14 effect)".

## Lagoon (map 14): the person sponsors in fork, sandbox and live play

- `web/js/lagoon-deal.js` (new) `expandLagoonDeal(before, steps)`: the engine deals the starting hands in the step of the last action card draft keep, with Lagoon's sponsor as the ninth card of the hand. Like the replay, that step is split: the keep step (hands of 8, the sponsors back in the deck), then "X draws <Sponsor> (Map 14 effect)" for each Lagoon seat in seat order (`log_only`, `merge_group` "person<seat>"). Only the last step keeps the legal actions, the options and the engine state; the others are passed through. A sponsor that is hidden from the viewer (the opponent in a live game) reads "X draws a person sponsor (Map 14 effect)".
- `web/js/fork.js` `commitSteps` (fork and sandbox) and `web/js/play.js` `pushLive` (live) run every new step through it; the viewer is put on the first of the split steps, and a push that arrives while the sponsors are still being played on moves the viewer along (`wasLast`).
- `web/js/playback.js` `personChain` no longer skips fork / sandbox / live: the sponsors are played on by themselves in every mode.
- Not split in the engine modes: the deal of the 8 + 2 cards of the two players (the engine has no separate step per player there, for any map).

## Glide: a trail where the bubbles are the focus (all modes)

- `web/js/card-motion.js` `bubbleTrail` (the glide of a discarded card to the invisible deck, own seat and other seat alike): 34 bubbles instead of 20, 1.45 times larger, with the same look; a thin (8 px) faint streak under them (before: none); two ripple rings where the card leaves and, where it vanishes, two rings and a burst of 14 bubbles. Ring and streak are the classes `.cm-ring` / `.cm-wake` in `web/css/card-motion.css`. Everything is in the overlay, nothing is drawn with Animations = None.

## Pouch: the card is tucked under its source (all modes)

- `web/js/card-motion.js` `tuckStage` replaces the leather pouch (`pouch()` and its styles `.cm-pouch` / `.cm-front` / `.cm-mouth` / `.cm-portrait` / `.cm-name` / `.cm-cord` are gone). As in the board game, the pouched card goes under its source and peeks out below it: the animal or sponsor card the cards go under (`under[key]`: an animal with Pouch, Expert On Australia, a Symbiosis animal that copied Pouch), or, when a map ability pouched it (`pouched`: map 7), a small model of the player's map with its placement bonuses (`zooBoard` of `S.replay.maps[seat]` without buildings, 25% larger than a card). The source fades in where it stays (straight, on whole pixels, no bounce), every card flies to it on one smooth curve (up out of the hand, round below the source, up under it; several cards peek a little lower each), then source and cards fade together. Position: above the hand, never cut off by the hand (folded or open) or by the bottom of the window.
- The other seat (`runOther`): the backs fly out of that player's info box and are tucked under the same public source, next to the box (above the hand when the source would cover it). Before, the backs only slid a few pixels out of the box. `web/js/card-motion-plan.js` `otherPlan` returns `pouchSource` (the card the cards went under, null for the map); tests in `tests/card-motion-plan.test.mjs`.
- `web/css/card-motion.css`: `.cm-tuck` (the source) and `.cm-tuck.map` (the map model's frame).

## Taken conservation bonuses (5 / 8) and the 16 reputation bonus no longer come back (replay data, all modes)

- Root cause: a step whose state the log builds (not the engine) - typically "X pays N xtoken for increasing card strength", the step before the action card is chosen - kept the conservation bonuses of the setup (`conservation_options` was never updated by `replay/builder.py`), so a taken bonus showed on the track again for that one step (table 943170782, move 207). The bonus on 16 reputation (`"99"`, Marine Worlds) had the same bug (table 927683584 after it was taken).
- `src/ark_nova/replay/builder.py` `h_take_bonus`: the takeBonus event of a random bonus (`remove` "5-1", "8-2", "99-0") takes it out of `conservation_options`, as the engine does. Fork, sandbox and live play use engine states and were not affected; a fork shows the fixed replay steps up to its fork point. Test `tests/test_taken_bonuses_stay_taken.py`. Python change: restart the server.

## 147. Sandbox: turns are confirmed like in a live game; display fit after a card pick; BDW2 crane button
- `replay/sandbox.py`: the sandbox game has `confirm_turns` (the bot confirms its own turns first in `bot_action`; an edit makes a new checkpoint, `edit_payload`). A snap (or any move that ends the turn) no longer refills the display: the turn waits at `confirm_turn`, where Confirm, Undo last step and Restart turn are moves of the engine (`playTurnButtons`, now also in the sandbox: `fork.js`, `action-bar.js` `!SANDBOX` for the client-side gate). Only the confirm, which reveals the new cards, is irreversible (the warning dialog). `PROMPT_TEXT.confirm_turn`.
- `shared.js`: choosing a card of the display draws the display again; `fitDisplay()` is called after it (it was shown at zoom 1, bigger than its column, until the next full render).
- BDW2: the crane move is a `build` effect with `rules.reposition`; its `place_building` actions carry `move: true`. The effect has the name 'Crane: reposition the enclosure' (`options.effect_name`), `action-bar.js` `effectButton` draws a 'Reposition (crane)' button that starts the placement (`S.placement.move`), `fork.js` / `board.js` keep the move apart from a new building (piece 'Reposition the size-2 (crane)', button 'Move the enclosure').

## 148. Enclosure flip without animated filters

web/js/card-motion.js 	urnOver: the flip is four stacked outer svg layers (shadow of the old side, shadow of the new side, old side, new side; will-change: transform, opacity) instead of one svg with a ilter: drop-shadow keyframe and a swap of two child groups. The shadow is the sprite alone, a static rightness(0) blur() filter, animated only by transform and opacity; the sides are swapped by the opacity of their own layer; the lift and shadow follow sin(pi*t) over the whole turn (no peak at the edge-on moment). The masks of the building copy stay (the gap / border between buildings needs them). Not yet checked in a browser trace.

Section 148, addition: the copies of a building that flies or turns (card-motion.js itMasks) get their masks cut down from the whole board (3000 x 3000 units) to the building's own box; the flight's svg has will-change: transform, opacity. Found in a screen recording of a new enclosure gliding in from the deck: frames repeated at intervals during the glide.

Section 148, addition: debug log for the card / building flights: open any viewer page with ?cmdebug=1 (or localStorage.cmdebug = '1') and the console lists every stop() that cancelled running animations (with the caller), every main-thread task over 40 ms while an animation runs, and the key presses (card-motion.js, CM_DEBUG).
